# I. First treatise

**Author**: Bahayla Mikael  
**Series**: PO Tome 1  
**Columns**: pp. 2–92  

[p. 2] {#po.1.p2}

THE BOOK OF THE MYSTERIES OF HEAVEN AND EARTH In the name of the Father, of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, one God. This is the book of the mysteries of heaven and earth, which exposes the mysteries of the first and the last tabernacle, which makes known the mysteries of all creation and how everything was created in its turn, mysteries in which Abba Bahayla-Mikä'êl was instructed and whose understanding was given to him * by Tamhâna-Samây. The angel who was sent to him, the sparkling Gabriel, said to him: Listen, that I may speak to you of the origin of all things and that I may inform you exactly about each of them in its rank.
ነገር ፡ በእንተ ፣ ምስጢረ ' ፡ መለኮት ፤ ኢሀሎ ፣ አብ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ወልድ ፡ወ ኢሀሎ ፡ ወልድ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ አብ ፤ ወኢሀሎ ፡፣ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ አብ፣።ወ "ወልድ ። ሀልዎቶሙ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ፍጥረተ ፡ ዓለም ። ወአሐዱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሀልዎቶሙ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ፍጥረተ ፡ ዓለም ። ፻ ስመ ፡ ሠለስቲሆሙ ፡፣ ብሂል ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ፅሩይ ፡ መለኮቶሙ ፤ ወ ፳ ውእቱ ፡ ሀልዎቶሙ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወምድር። አልቦ ፡ አመ ፡ ኢሀለዉ ፤ ወአልቦ ፣ ቅድመተ ፡ ለሀልዎቶመ ፣ ወአልቦ ፣ ማኅለቅተ ፣ ለሀልዎቶሙ ። ያ ሌብውምዎ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘ'ያሌብዎሙ ፤ ያአምርዎ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ወአልቦ ፣ ዘያአምሮ ሙ ፤ ይሬአይዎ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘይሬእዮሙ ፤ አኃዝያን ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘይእኅዞሙ ። ሚመጠነ ፡ እዜኑ ፡ ዕበየ ፡ ጊሩቱ ፡ ለፈጣሪ ። ወሚመጠነ ፡ ፍጥረተ ፡ዘ ፈጠረ ፤፣ እስመ ፡ ኢይትከሀል ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትበሀል ፡ መጠነዝ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ወመጠነዝ ፡ የ
Chapter concerning the mystery of the divinity. The Father did not exist before the Son, nor the Son before the Father, nor the Holy Spirit before the Father and * the Son. They existed before the creation of the world and one was their existence after the creation of the world. The three names of their three (persons) signify one God equal in their divinity and one was their existence before heaven and earth. There is no (moment) when they did not exist; there was no beginning to their existence, which will have no end. They explain all things, and no one can * explain them; they know everything, and no one knows them; they see everything, and no one sees them; they embrace everything, and no one grasps them. How shall I recount the great goodness of the Creator? How shall I expose the creation that he has made! For it is not possible to say how great he is and what his greatness is.

Praise to you, O sea of mercy and humility; you * forgive whom you wish and you punish whom you wish; O sea of grace and majesty, you give to whom you wish grace, majesty, and beauty; O sea of wisdom and knowledge, you give to whom you wish wisdom and science. O sea of sweetness and compassion, you give to whom you wish purity of heart. O sea of glory and wealth, you enrich and you honor whom you wish, and you reveal your secrets to whom

you wish. * Since the Scriptures mention you before the world, that is to say before Adam, there was therefore no world before Adam. Genesis cites you when Adam was created, heaven and earth were finished and God finished the whole universe, accomplishing his work. On this point the Scriptures agree. The inheritance of the just and the punishment of the impious were created on Sunday; as he says: When he created heaven and earth, he made beforehand for the world, * that is to say for Adam, judgment and punishment. The earthly paradise was created on Tuesday; but what was created on Sunday was the inheritance of the just.

Note well that the kingdom of heaven was created on Sunday, and the kingdom of heaven is the heavenly Jerusalem, which will later be opened to the righteous. And Peter said to Our Lord: "Before heaven and earth, nothing * was created." When the Gospel tells you: "Come, you blessed of my Father, inherit the kingdom which was prepared before the world was created," do not believe that it was before heaven and earth, for nothing existed before heaven and earth, except the three names (persons) which are the Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit.

On the first day, our God created the heaven which is called Ge'ergel. And as proof that heaven is * his seat, listen to what Isaiah said: "Heaven is his

throne and the earth the footstool of his feet." This heaven, he formed of white crystal, just as Hezeq'el (Ezekiel) says: "I saw the heaven the color of crystal, stretched out above the head of the animals." As for the crystal, from where did he draw it? He alone knows his secrets. Let us all say: There is nothing that is impossible for God. And do not believe that he began to * create it from below, but from above. Moses said at the beginning of Genesis: "In the beginning God created the heaven and the earth, and the earth existed at the origin, and the spirit of God moved above the water."
ርኢኬ ፣ ከመ ፡ ሥላሴ ፡ ተዐውቀ ፡ በርእሰ ፡ ኦሪት ፤ በቀዳሚ ፡ ዘይቤ ፤ በእንተ ፡ወ ልድ ፤ እስመ ፡ ቀዳማዊ ፡ ይሰምይዎ ፡ መጻሕፍት ፡ ለወልድ ። ወእግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘይቤሂ ፡ በእንተ ፡ 'አብ ፡ ወምድርኒ ፡ አዳም ፡ ዘሀለወ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሕሊና ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አምትካ ት ፤ ወበእንተሂ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ገሃደ ፡ ይብለከ ፡ ወመንፈሰ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይጹል ል ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ማይ ። ወኩሉ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ኦሪት ፡ ወነገራ ፡ ለኦሪት ፡ በሥላሴ ። ወዝውእ ቱ፣ቃል።፣ትቤ፡፣ኦሪት ፤ ንግበር ፡ ሰብአ ፣ በአርአያነ ፤ ርኢኬ ፡-ከመ ፣በሥላሴ ፣ያርኢ፣፤ በዝኒ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ንዑ ፣፡ ንርአይ ፡ ዘገብሩ ፡ እጓለ ፣ እመ"ሕያው ፤ ርኢኬ ፡ ከመ ፣ ሥላሴ ፡ ያርኢ ፡ በዝኒ ። ርኢኬ ፤ ከመ ፣ ሥላሴ ፡ ያርኢ ፡ እምቀዳሚ ።
Note then well how the Trinity appears at the beginning of Genesis. It says: "In the first place" because of the Son, for the Scriptures call the Son "the first," and it says God * because of the Father, and the earth is Adam, who was in the thought of God beforehand; as for the Holy Spirit, it tells you clearly: "The spirit of God moved upon the water." The entire work of Genesis, the entire narrative of Genesis rests on the Trinity. It says again this word: "Let us make man in our image." Note well that it thus indicates the Trinity. It also says: "Come, let us see what the sons * of men have done." Note again that it indicates the Trinity and see thus that it shows the Trinity from the origin.


[p. 5] {#po.1.p5}
ንግባእኬ ፣ መንገለ ፣ ነገሮሙ ፡ ለፍጥረት ፤ ለዳግሚትኒ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ እምነ ፡ ጊሜ ፡ ፈጠ ራ ባ ወሰመያ ፤ ኤራር ፤ ወእምአይቴ ፡ ፈጠሮ ፡ ለጊሜ ፤ ለሊሁ ፡ ያአምር ፡ ኅቡኣቲሁ።
Let us return then to the narrative of the creation. He made the second heaven of mist and named it 'Erar. From where did he draw the mist? He alone knows his secrets.
ወሣልሲት ፡ ሰማይ ፡ እምነድ ፡ ተፈጥረት ፡ ወሰመያ ፡ ሬማ ፡ወቀደሳ። ወለራብዕኒ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ እምነ ፡ ነድ ፡ ለይእቲኒ ፡ ገብራ ፣ ወሕበሪሃ ፡ “ከመ ፡ ዕንቄ ፡ ባ ሕርይ ፤ ወሕንጸታ ፡ በከርከዴን ፣ ወበ፲ወ፪አዕናቀ፦ ፡ ወ፳ጥኀት ፡ እምኔሃ ፣ ይርሕብ ፡ም እልፊተ ፡ እምዝ ፡ ዓለም ። ወሶበ ፡ ጸፕኀት ፡ ይርሕብ ፡ እምዝ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ምንተ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ዙለንታሁ ። ወይቤለኒ ፡ ዘተፈነወ ፡ ኅቤየ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ያአምር ፡ መጠና ፤ ወ፲ወ፪መላ እክት ፣ የዐቅብዋ ፤ ወአንቀጽ' ፡ እምአናቅጽ ፡ ውሉጥ ፡ ሕብራ ፤ወመሳክዊሃ ፡ "ዘእግረ ፡ አሚር ፡ ወስቅሎሃ ፡ ልዑል ፡ ወቀመራ ፡ በሕብረ ፡ ቀይሕ ፡፣ ሥዑል ። ወመራብዕታ ፡ ይ መስል ፡ ዕፅ ፡ ፍርም ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ሕብረ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወገበላቲሃ ፡ ከመ ፡ ወርኅ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይመል እ ፡ አምጣና ። ወሥዕለ ፡ ፍጥረት ፣ ሀሎ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ በከመ ፡ ፪ፀሐይ ፡ አው ፡ ከመ፣ ፲ወ፪ ፡ አው ፡ ከመ ፡ ፪ ፡ አው ፣ ከመ ፡ ከዋክብት ።
The third heaven was created of fire. He called it Rema and sanctified it. As for the fourth heaven, he also made it of fire. Its colors * are those of a sea pearl. It is built of chalcedony, with twelve pearls, and one of its gates is ten thousand times larger than this world. And if a single one of its gates is larger than this world, what must it be in its entirety! He who had been sent to me said to me: God alone knows its dimensions. Twelve angels guard it. The main gate is of various colors; its windows * [are the] color of the sun's rays, its ceiling (?) high, and its roof painted red. Its quadrature (The four walls?) resembles the ferem tree which is the color of the sky and its battlements (?) the moon when it is at its full. A representation of the creation is inside, [as large] as seven suns, or as twelve, or as four, or as the stars.
ወሀየ ሀሎ ፣ መልክአ ፡ገጽ ፡ ብሩህ ። ወእምቅድመ ፡ ይፍጥሮ ፡ 'ለአዳም ፡ህየ ፡ ተሥዕ ሉ።፣ኅሩያን ፡ በከመ ፣ ይቤ" ፡ ጥበብ ፤ እሉሰ ፡ ዕደው ፡ ሥዑላን ፡ ወኢተረስዐት ፡ ሎሙ ፣፡ ጽድቆሙ ፤ ወህየ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ መሥዋዕት ፡ ዘእሳት ፤ ወህሀየ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ታቦት ፡ ዘእሳት ፣ ወህሀየ ፡ ሀሎ ደብተራ ፡ዘእሳት ፡ ፡ ወሀየ ፡ ሀሎ ፣ ባሕረ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ፡ ወህየ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ልብሰ፣0 ብርሃን ፤ ወህየ ፣ ሀሎ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ብርሃን ፤ ወህየ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ቀጸላ ፡ ሥን ፣ ወበሀየ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡
There is the image of the luminous face. Before he created * Adam, the elect were represented there, as wisdom says: "As for these men who are figured there, their justice will not be forgotten." There is the altar of fire, as well as the ark of fire. One sees there seven tabernacles of fire, seven seas of light, garments of light, crowns of light, diadems of beauty. There


[p. 6] {#po.1.p6}
መናብርት ፡ ሱሩራት ፤ ወበሀየ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ አፍራስ ፡ ጸዓድው ፣ 'ወቀይሓን ፣ ወህየ ፡ ሀለዉ፡ አርማሐ ፡ ብርሃን ፤ ወሀየ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ አስይፍተ' ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ወአድርዓ ፡ ብርሃን ፤ ወህየ ፤ ሀለዉ ፡ ወላትወ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ወሀየ ፡ሀለዉ ፡ ኩያንው ፡ ወሰዋትል ፡ ዘእሳት ፣ ወሀየ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ሐጸ ፡ ወወረዋት”" ፡ዘእሳት ፣ ወህሀየ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ መስቀለ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ወማዕጠንተ ፡ ብርሃን ፣ ወዕፀ ወ ፡ ብርሃን ፤ ወዝኩሉ ፡ ሀሎ ፣፡ ውስተ ፡ ራብዕ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ እንተ ፡ይእቲ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም። ወካዕበ"መ ፡ ለመላእክትኒ ፡ ኢያርአዮሙ ፡ ኪያሃ ፤፣ እምድኅረ ፣ ልደቱ ፡ በሥጋ ፣ ወለሰብእ ኒ ፡ ኢያብኦሙ ፡ እንበለ ፡ ይልበስ ፡ ሥጋ ፤ ስብሐት ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ለዘይከሥት ፡ ዘሰማዕነ፣፡መ
are the solid thrones and white * and red horses. There are also flaming lances, luminous swords, breastplates and sparkling shields, javelins and fire cutlasses, as well as incandescent arrows and spears. There also are the luminous cross, the luminous censers, the luminous trees. All this is in the fourth heaven, which is Jerusalem. * He did not show it to the angels since his birth in the flesh and he did not introduce man into it before he had clothed himself in flesh. Glory to him who revealed to us what we have learned of his wonders, as well as to the Spirit who sanctifies everything.
ንክራቱ ፡ ወመንፈስሂ ፡ ቀዳሴ ፣፡ ዙሉ ። ወለኃምሲትሂ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ እምነ ፡ ማይ ፡ ፈጠራ ፡ ወሰመያ ፡ ሌዌን ፣ ወለሳድሲትኒ ፡ ከመሁ ፡ እምነ ፡ ማይ ፡ ፈጠራ ፡ ወሰመያ ፡ ዲሪቆን ። ወለዝ ፡ ሰማይ ፣ ዘይሬእዮ ፡ አዕይንተ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እምነ ፣ ማይ ፡ ው”እቱኒ ። ሠለስቱ ፡ ሰማያት ፣ ተፈጥሩ ፡ እምነ ፡ ማይ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ለጴጥሮስ ፣ ወነሣእነ ፡ ማየ ፡ ጽሩየ ፡ ወፈጠር ነ ፡ መሰማያተ ፡ ወባሕቱ ፡ መጻሕፍትሰ ፡ ይብሉነ" ፤ በቀዳሚ ፡ ገብረ ፡እግዚአብሒር ፡ ሰማ የ ፡ ወምድረ ፡ ወእሳተ ፡ ወነፋሰ ፡ ወብርሃነ ፡ ወዘመደ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ቅዱሳን ፣ እሎንተ ፡
God created the fifth heaven with water and named it Lewen. He also formed with water the sixth heaven and called it Dirigon. This heaven which the eyes of man see * [is drawn] from water. Three heavens were made with water, just as Our Lord said to Peter: "We took pure water and we created three heavens." But the Scriptures tell us: "In the beginning God created the heaven and the earth, the fire, the wind and the light, and the families of the holy angels." These six things he made on Sunday.
ገብረ ፡ በፅለተ ፡ እሑድ ። ወከመ ፡ ምንትመ ፡ ይብል ፡ ነሣእነ ፡ ማየ ፡ ወፈጠርነ ፡ ሰማ”ያተ ፤ማይኑ ፡ ቀደመ፡ተ ፈጥሮ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ሰማይ ። ወኢይብሉ፤ ፡ መጻሕፍት ፡ ማይ ፡ ቀደመ ፡ ተፈጥሮ ፣ ቅድ
Since he says: We took water and we created the heavens, * was water then created before heaven? The Scriptures do not say that water was formed before heaven. And we, what can we add! He alone knows his secrets.


[p. 7] {#po.1.p7}
መ ፡ ሰማይ ፤ ወምንትኑመ ፡ ንብል ፡ ለሊሁ ፡ ያአምር ፣ ኅቡኣቲሁ ። ወመላእክትሰ ፡ ተፈጥሩ ፡ እምነ ፡ ነደ ፡ እሳት ፡ ወልሳናቲሆሙ ፡ዘዘዚአሆሙ ፤ወበከ መ ፡ በዝኀ ፡ ልሳኖሙ ፡ ለውሎሉድ ፡ አዳም ፣ ወብሔሮሙ ፤ ለመላእክትኒ ፡ ዘዘዚአሆሙ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ልሳናቲሆሙ ፡ በከመ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመሕያው ፤ ወያቤይ"ኖ፡8መልአ ክ ፡ ለክልኡ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እምአይቴ ፡ መጻእከ ፤ ወይቤሎ ፡ እምእገሌ ። ወበከመ ፡ ውሉጥ ፡ ልሳኖሙ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ውሉጥ ፡ ስብሐቲሁ ፤ ፻ይዘይብሉ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ወ ይዘይብሉ ፡ ስቡሕ ፡ ወ፻እኩት ፣ ወ፻ዘይብሉ ፡ ኢይክል ፡ ይስምዖ ፡ እዝነ ፣ መዋቲ ። ወ ፍጥረቶሙ ፡ ለመላእክት ፣ ኢይምሰልከክ ፡ ዘተፈጥሩ ፡ በ8ጊዜ ። በቀዳሚት ፡ ሰዓተ ፡ መ ዓልት ፡ ተፈጥሩ ፡ መላእክተ ፡ ገጽ ፡ ዘውእቶሙ ፡ ነገደ ፡ ሚክኤል ፣ ወከመሰ፣፡እሙ'"ን፣፣ ሚካኤል ፡ መልአክ ፡ እመላእክት ፡ ቀደምት ፡ መጽአ ፡ ይርድአነ ። ወበዳግሚት ፡ ሰዓት ፡ ተፈጥሩ ፡ መላእክተ ፡ ቅዳሴ ፡ ዘውእቶሙ ፡ ካህናት ።
The angels were created from the flame of fire. They each had their own language. Just as the children of Adam have several languages and several countries, so the angels have several languages, each their own, as the sons of men have diverse regions. One angel made himself understood * by another. He said to him: Where do you come from? - The latter replied: From such and such a place. But just as their languages are of diverse kinds, so their praises are different. There are three who say: "Holy," three who say: "Praised," three who say: "Blessed," and three who say what the ear of a mortal cannot hear. Do not believe that the angels were created at the same moment; at the first hour of the day were created the angels of the face who are of the family of Michael, and as Michael, the prince of the first angels, is * faithful, he came to help us. At the second hour were created the angels of divine service, who are the priests.
ወበሣልስ ፣ ሰዓት ፣ ተፈጥሩ ፣ መናብርት ፤ ወበራብዕ ፡ ሥልጣናት ፤ ወበኃምስ ፡ አጋእ ዝት ፤ ወበሳድስ ፡ ኀይላት ፤ ወበሳብዕ ፡ ረበዋት ፣ ወበሳምን ፡ መኳንንት ፤ ወበታስዕ ፡ አ ርባብ ።'
At the third hour were formed the thrones; at the fourth the dominions; at the fifth the lords; at the sixth the powers; at the seventh the myriads; at the eighth the princes; at the ninth the archangels.
ወቅድመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ መላእክት ፡ መምልአ ፡ ዓሥር ፡ ፈጠረ ፡ ዘመደ ፡ ስጥናኤል ።
But before all the angels, who form the tenth order (?), he created the family of Setna'el.


[p. 8] {#po.1.p8}
ወኩሉ ፡ግብሩ ፡ ለአምላክነ ፡ በጥበብ ፡ ወበኪን ። ወዘንተ ፣፡ ሰሚዐነ ፡ ንበል ፡ ዙልነ፣ ዙ ሉ ፡ ይትከሀለከ ፣ ወአልቦ ፣ ዘይሰአነከ ፡ 'ለከ ። ወግብሩሰ ፡ ለመንክረ ፡ ኪን ፡ ይበዝኅ ፡ ዘኢተጽሕፈ ፡እምዘ ፣ ተጽሕፈ ፤ ወባሕቱ ፡ ጐልቁቄ ፡ ጅወ፪መጻሕፍትሰ ፡ነገሩነ ፡ በአምጣ ነ ፡ ንክል ፡ ሰሚዐ ፤ ወነፍስነ ፡ ይጌይል ፣ እምኩሉ ፡ ፍጡራን ፡ ወእሱር ፡ ውእቱ ፡ በቃ ለ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ። ንትመየጥ ፡ካፅበ ፡ መንገለ ፡ ፍጥረቶሙ ፡ዘምድር ፤ በከመ ፡ ሂሰማያት ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ምድር ኒ ፤ እንተ ፡ ተፈጥረት ፡ በማይ ፣ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፣ ሐዋርያ ፤ ወለምድርኒ ፡ እም ነ ፡ ማይ ፡ ፈጠራ ፡ ወአጽንዓ ፣ በቃለ ፡እ”ግዚአብሔር ። ወመትሕቴሃ ፡ ፈጠረ ፡ ባሕረ ፡ዐ ቢየ ፡ እንስቲያዊተ ፡ ክፍሉ ፤ ለተባዕታይ ፡ ማይ ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ። ወዛቲ፡ም ድር ፡ ላዕለ ፡ማይ ፤ ወመትሕቴሁ ፡ ለውቅያኖስ ፦ሀሎ ፡ ቀላየ ፡ ማይ ፡ ግሩም ። ወመትሕ ተ ፡ ቀላይ ፡ ኩኩሕ ፤ ወመትሕተ.፣ ኩኩሕ ፡፤ ሲኦል ፡ ወመትሕተ ፡ ሲኦል ፡ ነፋስ ፣ወ
All the work of our God is done with wisdom and with art; after having learned all this, let us all say: "You can do everything, nothing is impossible * for you." His work is a marvel of art; what is not described surpasses even what has been; but the eighty-one books have told us what we can understand; our soul surpasses all creatures, for it is attached to the word of God. Let us return now to the creation of the earth. Just as the seven heavens, the earth was formed from water, just as the apostle Peter says: "He created the earth with water and strengthened it by the voice * of God." Below it, he created a great female sea, a division of the male sea which is in heaven. This earth is above the water and, below the Ocean (Wqyanos), is a frightening abyss of water, under the abyss a rock, under the rock Sheol, under Sheol the wind and under the wind the confines of darkness.
መትሕተ ፡ ነፋስ ፡ ጸናፌ ፡ ጽልመት ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ምንት ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ለሊሁ ፡ ያአምር ፡ ዓቡኣቲሁ ፣ ወባሕቱ ፡ ለእሉሂ ፡ቀ ደዕሙ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። "ኅድግሰ ፡ ለባዕድ ። ለሲኦልኒ ፡ ቀደሳ ፡ በከመ ፡ ግዕዛ ፤ ወሶ በ፣ ኢቀደሶ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ባሕቱ ፡ ምድርኒ ፡ እመ ፣፤ ኢክህለት ፡ ጸዊረ ፡ ዥሉ፣ ፍጥረት ። ወኑፕንሰ ፡ ለሰማያት ፡ ፪አምዝ ፡ ሰማይ ፣ ወእምዝ ፣፡ ምድር ፡ ዘን1ሬኢ ፡ም ክዕቢተ ፡ ይነውኃ' ። ወካዕበሰ ፡ ሰማይ ፣ ይነውኅ ፡ እም5ሰማያት ፡ ወእምሂምድር ። ኩሎ
And beyond that, what is there? He alone knows his secrets. All these things, the Holy Spirit has sanctified (?). * Leave that to another. He sanctified Sheol according to its nature, for if he had not sanctified everything, the earth itself could not bear all the creatures. As for the height of the heavens, it is six times higher than that of our heaven and double that of the earth that we see (?). And again the heaven is higher than seven heavens and than seven earths.


[p. 9] {#po.1.p9}
ን ፡ መንክራቲከ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ ኅያላን ፡ ሰማዕነ ። ወኢይምሰልከ ፡ ኦብእሲ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ቀዳሚት ፡ ዘ”ኸመ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ መዋፅል ፤ አላ ፡ 8ዕለት፣ የአክል ፡ ከመ ፣ መጠነ ፡ ኒዓመት ። ወበዝኒ ፡ ናነክር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ንብል ፡ መንክራቲከ ፣ መ ንክር ፡ ኪን ፤ እስመ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ኒሰማያት ፡ አንተ ፣ ወመትሕተ ፡ ፄምድር ፡ አንተ ፤ እስመ ፡ አልቦ ፡ መክን ፡ ኀበ ፣ ኢሀሎክ ፣ በመለኮትከ ፡፣ ወአልቦ ፡ ጽንፈ ፡ ለእጊዞትከ ። ወአልቦ ፡ ወሰነ ፡ ለግዝአትከ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ “ልቄ! ፡ ብዕለ ፡ ጥበብከ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ መስፈርተ፣ ብዝኀ ፡ ምሕረትከ ፤ "ወባሕቱ ፡ ምሕረትከ ፡ ይቄርሮ ፡ ለመዓትከ ፡ ወሣሀልከ ፡ ያርሕቆ፣ ለኩነኔከ ፤ ኢትኩንኖ ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ በአምጣነ ፡ ጌጋዩ ፡ ዳአሙ ፡በከመ ፣ ብዝኅ ፡ ምሕረት ከ ፤ ወመኑ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ዘእንበሌከ ። ስብሐት ፡ ለከ ፣፡ ወዕበይ ፡ ለመንግሥትከ ፤ እምቅድ መ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አንተ ፡ ወእስከ ፡ ለዓለም ፣ አንተ ፣ ወይእዜኒ ፡ አንተ ። ወበሰኑይ ፡ ፈጠረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ፳ምጽናዕ ፡ ወዐምዶ ፡ ለነፋስ ። ወእንተ ፡ ምድር፣ በታሕቱ ፡ ፈጠረ ፡ 8መዛግ”ብተ ፡ ነፋሳት ። ወንኡሳን ፡ መሳክው ፡ ወ፻በየማኑ ፡ ወ፻በጸጋ ሙ ፡ ወ፳ፕኅት ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ወእምኩሉ ፣ ኅዋኅው ፡ መንክራቲከ ፣ እግዚኦ ፡ ሰማዕነ ። ወበሠሉስ ፣፡ ፈጠረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ባሕረ ፣ ወየብሰ ፣ ወገነተ ፡ ኤይም ፡ በኤይም ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ ዕፀወ ፡ ዘይፈሪ ፡ ወዘኢይፈሪ ፤ ወብዙኀ ፡ ገነታተ ፡ ፈጠረ ፡ ድኅረ ፡ ምሥራት፣ ወምዕራብ ።
O God of the strong, we have learned all your wonders. Do not believe, O man, that the first day * was (a day) like those of now; it was a day encompassing the space of seven years. We admire this and we exclaim: "Your prodigies are wonders of art." For above the seven heavens, you are found, and below the seven earths, you are still. There is no place where you are not in your divinity: there are no bounds to your power, there is no limit to your dominion, no number to express the richness of your wisdom, no measure to your great mercy, * for your mercy soothes your anger, and your pity wards off punishment. You do not punish man in proportion to his fault, but according to your great clemency. Who is God, except you? Praise to you, glory to your royalty. Before the world, you existed, and you will exist until the end of the ages; just so you still exist. The second day, God created a firmament and the column of wind. Then below the earth he made (one or) four receptacles * for the winds, (with) small windows, one hundred on the right and one hundred on the left, and a gate. By all this and by all these openings, we have known your prodigies, O Lord. The third day, God created the sea and the dry land, as well as the garden of Edom at Edom, and every tree that bears fruit and does not bear fruit; he created several gardens beyond the east and the west.


[p. 10] {#po.1.p10}
ወቦ ፡ እምዕፀዊሃ ፡ ለገነት ፡ እለ ፡ ይመስሉ ፡ በረደ ፡ ጸዐዳ ፤ ወቦ ፣ እለ ፣፡ ይመስሉ ፣ነ ደ ፡ እሳት ። ወቦ ፡ እለ ፡ ይመስሉ ፀሓየ ፡ ወያንጸበርቁ ። ወፅፀዊሃኒ ፡ ለገነት ፡ ኢኮኑ' ፡ ከ መ ፡ ዕፀወ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፣ ወልዕ[ል]ት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ አድባር ፡ ነዋኃት፤ ፤ ከመ መጠነ ፡ ፲ወ5ደበእመት ፡ በእመተ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወሀሎ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ኩሉ፣ሠናያት፣፤ ዘዐይን ፡ ኢርእየ ፡ ወእዝን ፡ ኢሰምዐ ፡[ወ|ውስተ ፡ ልበ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ዘኢተኅለየ ፡ ዘአስተ ዳለወ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለእለ ፡ ያፈቅርዎ ።
Among the trees of the garden, there were some that resembled transparent hail, others that resembled * a flame of fire; others were like the sun and sparkled. The trees of the garden were not like those of this earth; it was higher than the highest mountains by about fifteen cubits, in cubits of the Holy Spirit. One found there all sorts of beautiful things, which the eye has never seen and which the ear has not heard, which do not rise to the heart of man, and which God has prepared for those who love him *.
ወበረቡዕ ፣ ፈጠረ ፡ ፀሓየ ፡ ወወርኀ ፡ ወከዋክብተ ፤ ወብርሃነ ፡ ፀሓይ ፡ ኢይምሰልክ ፡ ከመ ፣ይእዜ ፡ዘትሬእዮ ፤ አላ ፡ አሜሃሰ ፡ ፲ወ፪እደ ፤ ይቤሉ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ስእነ ፡ አንዕስዎ ፡ ኀበ ፣ ፈኖውከነ ፤ አሰስል ፡ ንስቲተ ። ወአሰሰለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ መንፈቅ ፡ ክፍል ፣ ወፄአትረፈ ፤ ወ፪እድ ፣ እምኔሃ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ለወርኅ ፡ ወጸ፳እድ ፡ ቶስሐ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ከዋ ክብት ፡ ወ፳ውስተ ፡ ማያት ፡ ወ፳ውስተ ፡ ደመናት ፡ ወ፳ውስተ ፡ "መብረቅ ፣ ወሶበ ፡ ዘን ተ ፡ ትሰምዕ ፡ ኢትትዋክሕ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትብል ፡ በእፎኬ ፤ ይብል ፡ መጽሐፍ ፤ በረቡዕ ፡ገ ብረ ፡ ፀሓየ ፡ ወወርኀ ፡ ወከዋክብተ ፤ ወአማንኬ ፡ ከማሁ ። ወአሜሃ ፡ ሶበ ፡ርእየ ፡ስጥናኤል ፣ ሥነ ፡ ፀሓይ ፡ ወወርኅ ፣ ወድቀ ፡ በኅሊናሁ ፣ እን ዘ ፣ ይብል ፤ አነብር ፡ መንበርየ ፣ መልዕልተ ፡ ከዋክብት ፣ ወእትሜሰሎ ፣ ለልዑል ፡ወ ለኀያል ። ወወዲቀሰ ፡ ዘወድቀ ፡ ፍጹመ ፡ ኢይምሰልከክ ፡ በረቡዕ ፡ አላ ፡ በዓርብ ፡ ወድቀ ፤
The fourth day, he created the sun, the moon and the stars. Do not believe that the light of the sun was like that which you see now; it was twelve times stronger (?). The angels said to him: We cannot go where you send us; withdraw a little from it. And God withdrew a little from it, [by dividing it]. He took six parts from it and left six. From four parts he made the moon; he mixed one with the stars, another with the waters, one with the clouds and one with the * lightning. And when you hear this told, do not argue by exclaiming: "How so?" The Scriptures say that the fourth day he made the sun, the moon and the stars. In truth, it is so. But then, when Setna'el saw the brightness of the sun and the moon, a thought struck his mind, and he said: "I will establish my throne above the stars and I will become like the Most High and the Almighty." But this thought which invaded him, do not imagine that it was the fourth day that it


[p. 11] {#po.1.p11}
በዳግም ፡ሰ"ንበት ። ወአሜሃ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ጸብአ ፡ ኮነ ፣ በሰማይ። ወተደለወ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ለጸብእ ፡ ወተካወሰ ፣ ኀይለ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፤ ንፁ ፡ ንርአይ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ረኣዬ ፡ ዙልሄ ፣ ይቤ ከመዝ።
assailed him. It was Friday, in the second * week. Then there was a great battle in heaven. Mika'el prepared himself for combat, and the army of heaven set itself in motion. God then said: "Let us go see," and he inspected from all sides, while speaking thus.
ወስጥናኤልሂ ፣ ኅዩል ፣ ፍጥረቱ ፡ እመላእክት ፣ ወተንሥአ ፡ ለተጻብኦ ። ወልዑልኒ፤ አዘዞ-ለሚካ[ኤ]ል ፤ ወኦሆ ፡ይቤ ፡ወተንሥአ ፤ ወሐራሁሰ፡ሰብ!አ ፡ አፍራስ ፡ ወኮኑ ፲ወ፪፳ ፡ ወሰብአ ፡ ወልታ ፡ ፡ ወአፍራስ ፡ እለ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ድር[ዕ ፡ አፍራስ ፡ ዘእሳት ፣ ድ፳ ፡ወ”ሰብአ ፡ ሞጸፍ ፡ዘእሳት ፡ ድጽ፳ ፡ ወሰብአ ፡ ሰዋትል ፡ ዘእሳት ፡ ሮ፳ ፡ ወእለ ፡ ያሀይ ዱ ፡ በእብን ፡፻፳ ፡ ወአለ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ጐድበ ፡ ዘእሳት ፡ ፃ፳ ፣ ወእለ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ መስቀለ ፡ብ ርሃን ፡ ፀ፳ ፡ ወአለ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ማኅቶተ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ዓ፳ ፡ ወዝ ፡ መንክር ፡ ኮነ ፡ በሰማይ ።
As for Setna'el, his most valiant creature among the angels, he stood against him and attacked him. But the Most High gave orders to Mika'el and the latter obeyed him. He rushed forward, as well as his army [composed] of horsemen to the number of 120,000, of 600,000 warriors equipped with shields, 700,000 armored horsemen mounted on horses of fire *, 700,000 warriors armed with slings of fire, 800,000 provided with swords of fire, 1,000,000 who threw stones, 500,000 with the axe, 300,000 carrying the cross of light and 400,000 with flaming torches. This admirable army was in heaven.
ወ[ወ]ውዑ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ትጉሃን ፣ ወአኀዙ ፣ይትቃተሉ ፡ ወሰዐሮሙ ፡ስጥና ፡ ወጐዩ። ወካዕበ ፣ ተጻብኡ ' ፡ ወሰዐሮሙ ፡ ወጐዩ ። ወበሣልስ ፡ ወሀቦ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ መስቀለ ፡ ብርሃ ን ፡ "ወጽሑፍ ፡ውስቴቱ ፡ ስም ፡ ዘይብል ፡ በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱ ስ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ስጥና ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ጽሕፈት?” ፣ ወተነጽሐ ፡ ወአውደቆ ፣ ሚክኤል፡ወ ነተዐ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ሰራዊቱ ፡ እለ ፡ ኅብሩ ፡ ምስሌሁ ። ወከመዝ ፡ ፍጥረቱ ፡ለስጥና ፡፲፻ወ፲፻ ፡ በአመተ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ቆሙ ፣ ወርእሱ ፡ መጠነ፣
All the angels pushed out cries, and quickly they began the attack, but Setna broke their ranks and they took flight. They returned to the charge, but they were again repulsed and put to flight by Setna. The third time God gave him (to Mika'el) the cross of light * on which was an inscription thus conceived: "In the name of the Father, of the Son and of the Holy Spirit." When Setna saw this inscription, he was annihilated, and Mika'el overthrew him; he fled with all the troop that he had with him. Now such was the conformation of Setna: his height was 1,700 cubits

ደብር ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ወአፉሁ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ዓሻ ፡ በእመት ፤ ወቀራንብተ ፡ ዐይኑ ፡ ምሕዋረ ፡ ሠሉስ ፡ ፅለት ፤ ወሶበ ፣ ፈቀደ ፣ ያስተጋብእ ፡ ብንተ ፡ ዐይኑ ፣ በ፪ዕለት ፡ እምዕጹብ ፡ ያስተጋብእ ፤ ወእዳኒ ፡ ድ በእመት ፤ ወእገሪሁ ፡ ፳፻ ፡በእመት ፡፣ ወገጹኒ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ምሕዋረ ፡ ዕለት። ወነ"ፍስቱሂ ፡ ፻ ፡ በእመት። ወከመሰ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ነፍስተ ፣ ለመላእክት ፡፣ስምዖ ፣ለሕዝቅኤልዖ ነቢይ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፤ በ፪ክነፊሆሙ ፡ ይከድኑ ፡ ገጾሙ ፡ወበ፪ ፣ ይከድኑ ፣ እገሪሆሙ ፡ወበ፪ ፣ ይከድኑ ፡ እደዊሆ ሙ ፤ ወበ፪ ፡ ይከድኑ ፣ ነፍስቶሙ ። አእምርኬ ፡ ከመ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ነፍስተ ፡ ለመላእክት ፤ወ አልቦ ፡ እምውስቴቶሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ተባዕት ፡ ወአንስት ። ወኢይወልዱ ፣ ወኢይትዋለዱ ። ወ ስጥናሰ ፡ ዘይጐሥዕ ፡ክምረ ፡ እምአፉሁ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ደብር ፡ ዘያውዕል ፡ እምነግሀ ፡ እስከ ፡ ጻሰዓት ፣ ምሕዋሩ ። ወምራቅ ፣ ዘይወፅእ ፣ እምአፉሁ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ዮርዳኖስ ። ወከመዝ ፡ አግሪሞ ፡ ፈጠሮ ፡ ለ”ስጥና ፡ ወኢዮብሂ ፡ ይብለከ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፤ ወአልቦ ፡ ወኢምንተ ኒ ፡ በዲበ፣ምድር ፤ ወከማሁ ፡ ዘያንስዐስዐ « ለሲኦል ፡ ከመ ፣ ጽሕርት ። ወብዙኅ ፡ መበሉ ፡ አጋንንት ፣፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ምጽአቱ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፣ ወቦ ፡ ለዘ ፡ አዕወ
of angels, his head like a large mountain, his mouth of 40 cubits, the eyebrows of his eyes the length of three days' journey, and when he wanted to cover the pupil of his eyes, he arrived at it with difficulty in seven days, his hand had 70 cubits, his feet 7,000 cubits, his face the length of a day's journey and his penis * 100 cubits. And as proof that the angels have a penis, listen to what the prophet Hezeq'el (Ezekiel) says: "With two of their wings, they cover their face; with two others, they cover their feet; with two others, they hide their hands; and with two others, they hide their penis." Know then that the angels have a penis and that there are not among them males and females. They do not give birth and are not born. As for Setna, the matter that he rejected from his mouth was similar to a mountain whose circuit would require a walk from the morning until seven o'clock and his spit was like the Jordan. It is thus that to make him terrifying, God had created * Setna. Job tells you about him: "There is nothing on earth that is like him, nor that, like him, makes Sheol boil like a cauldron." The demons had great power before the coming of Our Lord; there were people whose eyes they struck with blindness, others

ሩ ፡ ዐይኖ ፤ ወቦ ፡ ለዘ ፡ አጽመሙ ‹፡እዝኖ ፤ ወቦ ፡ ለዘ ፡ ሰበሩ ፡ እግሮ ፡ ወቦ ፡ ለዘ ፡ አ ይበሱ ፣ እደዊሁ ፤ ወቦ ፡ ለዘ ፣ ርእሶ ፤፡ አሕመሙ ፤ ወቦ ፡ ለዘ ፡ አድፈኑ ፣ ወቦ ፡ ለዘ ፡ ወ ርኃ ፡ ረሰይዎ ፤ ወዝውእቱ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይኔጽሩ ፡ ኀቤከ ፣፡ ይኔጽር ፣ ማዕዶ ፣ ይኔጽር ፣ ይመ ስል ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ይመስል ፤ ሕማማተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሰዐሮ ፣ በመስቀሉ ። ወኢይምሰልከ ፡ ዘረሳዕኩ ፡ ለከ ፣ በእንተ ፣ ስጥ”ና ፤ ወጽንሐኒኬ ፣ እስከ ፡ እነግር ፡ በእንተ ፣ ፍጥረት ፣ ዘተፈጥረ ፣ በዕለተ ፣ ኀሙስ ። ወአሜሃ ፣ ተፈጥሩ ፣ አዕዋፍ ፣ ወዓ ሣት ፣ ወዐናብርት ፡ ወእንስሳ ፤ ዘውእቶሙ ፡ ብሔሞት ። ወሌውያታን ፡ ይነብር ፡ ውስ- ተ ፡ማይ ፤ ወበእንተዝ ፡ ይብለከ ፡ ኢዮብ ፤ ወእምኔሆሙ ፡ ተቀጽዐ ፣ መንበሮሙ ፡ ለዐ ናብርት ፡ እለ ፡ መትሕተ ፡ ሰማይ ። ወሄኖክኒ ፣ ይብል ፤ ሀለወት ፡ ዕለት ፣ ወሀለወ ፡ መ ሓሪ ፡ ርሑቀ ፡ መዓት ፤ ወይትከፈሉ ፡ ፪ዐናብርት ፡ ዐበይት ፤ ወይከወን ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ዕለ ት ፡ለኅሩያን ፣ መሐላ ፣ ወለኃጥኣን ፣ ሐተታ ። ርኢኬ ፣ ከመ ፡ ሰመይዎሙ ፡ እንስሳ ፣
whose ears they deprived of the faculty of hearing, others to whom they broke the feet or whose hands they dried up, others to whom they gave headaches, others whom they hid, and others whom they rendered lunatic (?). And that, while looking at you; he looks toward you, he looks … all that and all similar sufferings (?), Christ suppressed them by his cross. But do not believe that I have forgotten to tell you [something] touching Setna *. Wait a little and I am going to resume the creation, [by the creatures] which were created the fifth day. Then were created the birds, the fish, the cetaceans and the reptiles, which are the behemoth. And the leviathan inhabits the water; that is why Job tells you: "It is by them that the seat of the cetaceans which are under the heaven has been twisted." And Enoch also says: "There was a day when he was merciful, far from all anger." The great cetaceans will divide into two groups; there will be in this day an oath for the elect and an interrogation for the sinners. See then how the animals that you have created have been called.
ዝፈጠርከ ። ' ኦሪትኒ ፡ ትቤ ፤ ወይቤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ያውፅእ ፡ ባሕር ፡ እንስሳ ፡ ወኣዕዋፈ። ወርኢ ኬ ፡ ወስማዕ ፡ እንግርከ ፣ መንክረ ፡ ዜናሆን ፣ ለሰሚዕ ፡ ዕፁብ ፤ ዘከመ ፡ ይፀንሳ ፡ ወይ ወልዳ ፤፣ ወንኩር ፡ ፍጥረቶን ፡ እምነ ፡ አዕዋፍ ፤ ወአልቦ ፤ አምሳሊሆን ፡ በታሕተ ፡ ሰማ ይ ፡ ወንብረቶን ፡ ጠቃ ፡ ኽገነት ፤ ወአምአመ ፡ ተፈጥራ ፡ አልቦን ፡ ተባዕተ ፡ እምኔሆን ፤ ለእማንቱ ፡ አዕዋፍ ፤ ዳእሙ ፡ አንስት ፤ ኩሎን ። ወእምዘ ፣ ተወልዳ ፡ በሳድስ ፡ ዓም ፤ ይትለዐላ' ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኑኅ ፡ ሰማይ ፤ ወተልዕሎቶንሂ ፡ በእሉ ፣ የአውራኅ ፡ በቀዳሚ ፣፡ወ
Genesis says again *: God said: "Let the sea produce reptiles and birds." See then well, and listen, that I tell you their wonderful history, admirable to hear; how they conceive and reproduce, their creation more surprising than that of the birds. There are none like them under the heaven, and their dwelling is near the garden. Since they were created, there are


[p. 14] {#po.1.p14}
ርኅ ፡ ወበሣልስ ፡ ወርኅ ፡ ወበሣድስ ፡ ወርኅ ። ወበእሉ ፣ አውራኅ ፡ ፅንሶ”ን ፣ ለእማን ቱ ፡ አፅዋፍ ፡ ወበምንት ፡ ይፀንሳ ፡ ለፀሓ[ይ ፡ ይፀንሳ ። መንክር ፡ ነገር ፡ ሰማዕነ ።
no males among these birds, but all are females. In the sixth year after their birth, they rise to the highest of the heavens. They thus ascend into heaven for three months, the first month, the third month and the sixth. And it is during these months that their fertilization takes place. * - By what are they fertilized?
ወዕበ ፣ ፀንሰት ፣ ለፀሓይ ፡፣ ይእቲ ፡ ዖፍ ፣ ትፈነቅል ፣ ልጐተ ፡ ወትበውእ ፡ ውስተ ባሕር ፡ ዕሙቅ ፡ ወነቢራ ፡ህየ ፡ ኒአውርኀ ፡ ትወልድ ፡ በመንፈቀ ፡ ወርኅ ፡ ሳምን ፤ወ ሶበ ፡ ትወልድ ፡ ታቀድም ፡ አውፅኦ ፡ አፅዋፍ ፡ ዘሀለዉ ፡ በገቦ ፡ ከርሣ ፡ ዘፀጋም ፤ እስ መ ፡ ክልኤ ፡ ጾታ ፡ ትጸውር ፡ በከርሣ ፣ በዘየማን ፡ ትጸውር ፡ እንቁ ፡ ባሕርይ ፡ ክቡር፣ መንክራዊ ፡ ፅንቀ፦ ፤ ወበከርሣ ፣ ዘፀጋም ፡ ትጸውር ፡ ደቂቃ ፣ ወስማ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ዖፍ፣ ከርቤ ፡ ዲንል ። ወዝኒ ፡ ብሂል ፣ ንጽሕት ፣ እምአዕፅዋፍ ። ወሶበ ፡ ትወልድ ፡ ታቀድም፣፥ አውፅኦ ፡ አ”ዕዋፍ ፡ እለ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ በከርሣ ፡ ዘፀጋም ፣ ወበቀዳሚ ፡ ልደታ ፡ ትወልድ ፡ ፳ ፡ ወበዳግም ፡የ፻ ፡ ወበሣልስ ፡ ጓ፪ ፡ ወካዕበ ፡ ትዊጥን ፡ ትለድ ፡ ዕንቄ ፣ ባሕርይ ፡ እለ ፡ በየማነ ፡ ከርሣ ፤ ወበቀዳሚ ፡ ትወልድ ፡ ፲ወ፪ ፡ ወበዳግም ፡ ትወልድ ፡ ድ ፡ ወበሣልስ ፡ ፳ ፡ ፅንቀ ፡ ሠናይ ። ወበዝኒ ፡ ታስተዐጽብ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትብል ፡ ዐፀበኒ' ፡ ግብሩ ፡ ለኬን
- By the sun. - It is a wonderful thing that we have learned. When this bird has been fertilized by the sun, it plunges into the abyss of the sea, penetrates there to the bottom and, after having remained there for seven months, it gives birth in the middle of the eighth month. When it gives birth, it begins by bringing out the birds which are in the left flank, for it carries in its flanks two different things; in the right flank, it has precious pearls, admirable pearls, and in the left flank, it carries its young. The name of this bird is Karbe'e-Dinel, which means the purest of birds. When it gives birth, it first gives life * to the birds which are in the left flank. In the first birth, it produces 5 young, in the second 3 and in the third 49. Then it begins to bring out the pearls that it has in the right flank; the first time, it gives 12, the second time 70, and the third time, it produces a magnificent pearl. And in learning this thing, you will be astonished and you will exclaim: Admirable is the work of this artisan who is the Son of God.

Attracted by the brightness of the precious pearls, there arrives a great beast which absorbs them and which goes into the sea during the night; then it tears the other beasts which are on the ground. As for the birds which have come out of the left flank, when they

have remained * in the sea for 40 days and 40 nights, they come out of the water and begin to fly, after having let their wings grow. It is astonishing that in 40 days, they produce wings. Merchants then come to look for the pearls; they attack the fish, make it come out of the water and remove the pearls that it has in its belly. This thing is very exact; it exists in the land of the Orient, of the magi.
ወዝኒ ፡ ዖፍ ፡ ይትረጐም ፡ በወልድ ። አንሃብሰ" ፣፡ ይፀጌሳ ፣፡ በምዕር ፡ ሶበ ፡ ዐንዘረት'፣ ንግሥቶን ፡፣ በዝኒ ፡ ናንክር" ፡ ወንበል ፣ መንክራቲከ ፡ እሙን ፡ ነገር ። ወዝሰ ፡ ዖፍ ፡ ፪ ፅንሱ"፣በፀሓይ ፡ ፀሓይ"ኬ ፡ ዘይነብር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ፤ ወዕንቄ ፡ ባሕርይ ፡ እለ ፡ ተወ ልዱ ፡ እምዖፆፍ ፡ ሕዝበ ፣ አሚን ፣ እሙንቱ ፤ ወ፲ወ፪አለ ፣ ተወልዱ ፡ ፲ወ፪ሐዋርያት ፤ ወፎዘይቤ ፡ ድአርድእት ፡ ወጸዘይቤ ፡ ፳ሃይማኖት ፤ ወዓሣኒ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ውእቱ ፣ ወ5አዕዋ ፍ ፡ ይኦሪት ፡ ውእቱ ፤ ወ፻ዘይቤ ፡ ይመጻሕፍት ፡ ዘኢያሱ ፡ ዘመስፍን ፡ ዘሩት ፣ ወ፳፪8ወ ወወ፪መጻሕፍተ ፡ ነቢያት ። ወበሣድስ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ፈጠረ ፡ አምላክነ ፣ አራዊተ ፡ ወእንስሳ ፡ ወዙሎ ፡ ዘይትሐወስ ። ወፈጢሮ ፡ ዘንተ ፣ ይቤሎሙ ፡ ለመላእክቲሁ ፤ አምጽኡ ፡ መሬተ ፣ ወ[ለ1]መላእክ ተ ፡ እሳትኒ ፡ ይቤሎሙ ፡ አምጽኡ ፡ መሬብ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ እሳት ፡”በነገረ ፡ ሱርስት ፣ ወኦ ምጽኡ ፤ ወለመላእክተ ፡ ማያት ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ አምጽኡ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ማየ ፡ ወአምጽኡ ፤
This bird is explained by the Son. Moreover, the bees conceive once when their queen sings. Let us admire this and say: Your wonders are authentic. These birds which are fertilized by the sun * are so by the sun which dwells in the heavens; the pearls of the sea which are brought forth by the bird, are the faithful peoples; the 12 pearls which are brought forth first are the twelve apostles, the 70 which are mentioned next are the 70 disciples and the single pearl is the single faith. The fish is the world; the five birds are the five books of the Pentateuch, the three are the three books of 'Iyasu (Joshua), of Judges and of Ruth, and the 39 are the 39 books of the prophets. The sixth day our God created the animals, the quadrupeds and everything that moves. After having created all that, he said to his angels: "Bring me earth," and to the angels of light, he said: Bring me mareb, which is fire * in the Syriac language, and they brought it to him. To the angels of the waters, he said: Bring me


[p. 16] {#po.1.p16}
ወለመላእክተ ፡ ነፋሳትኒ ፣ ይቤሎሙ ፡፣ አምጽኡ ፣ ንስቲተ ፡ ነፋስ ፤ ወአምጽኡ ፤ ወቶስ ሐ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ጠባይዕ ፣ 8ወገብሮሙ ፡ ሰብአ ፣ ፍጹመ ፣ ወመንፈሰ ፡ ሕይወተ ፡ እንተ ፤ ውስተ ፡ አፉሁ ፡ ነፍኅ ፡ ላዕሉ ፡ ገጹ ፣፡ ለአዳም ፤ ወሶበ ፡ ፈጠሮ ፣ ሰመዮ ፡ አዳም ፡አ ደምከኒ ፡ ብሂል ፤ ወለመላእክቲሁኒ ፣ ይቤሎሙ ፤ ዝንቱ ፡ አምሳልየ ፤ ወወሀብክዎ ፡ ዙሎ ፡ ዘመትሕትየ ፤ ወሜምክዎ ፡ እንዘ ፡ እብል ፤ 3ሣእ ፡ 8በግዐ ፡ እለ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገነት ፡ ወሕርድ ፣ ወተቀ”ብእ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እዴከ ፡ በደሙ ፡ እዝነከ ፣ ዘየማን ፣ ወአጽባዕተ ፡ እዴክ ፡ ዘየማ ን ፡ ወእግረከ ፡ ዘየማን ፣ ወዝክረ ፣፡ ይከውን ፡ ለውሉድክ ፤ ወምስለ ፡ ሱራፌል ፡ ተሳተ ፍ ፡ ምስጢረ ። ወአልበዕሶ ፣ ልብሰ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ዘይመስል ፡ ጽን ፡ ረዳ ። ወአስተቀጸሎ ፤ ዲበ ፡ ርአሱ ፡ ቀጸለ ፤ ሥን ፡ ዘሕብረ ፡ ነድ ፡ ወመንፈቁ ፡ ሕብረ ፡ ፀሓይ ። ወገብረ ሎቱ ፡ በጌደነ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ወአዕጠቆ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ሐቋሁ ፣ ወጌራ ፡ ዘኀጺን ፡ ገብረ ፡ ለፍጽ ሙ ፡፡ ወአምጽአ ፡ ፈልፈል ፡ ወአጽዐኖ ፡ ወወሀቦ ፡ ረምሐ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አዴሁ ፡ ወአሣእ ነ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ለእገሪሁ ።፡
a little water, and they obeyed. To the angels of the winds, he said: Bring me a little breath, and they brought it to him. He mixed these four elements and he made of them a perfect man. Then he breathed on the face of Adam the breath of life which was in his mouth. When he had created him, he named him Adam, which means "you please me." Then he said to his angels: "This one is my likeness, I give him everything that is below me, and I constitute him the possessor of it in these terms: Take four sheep which are in the garden, sacrifice them and * anoint your hand with their blood, as well as the right ear, the fingers of the right hand and the right foot. This will be a memory for your children and you will participate in the mysteries with the seraphim. Then he clothed him in a luminous garment, which resembled the rose, crowned his head with a beautiful crown part color of flame and part color of the sun. He also made him luminous garments, girded his loins and put a helmet of iron on his forehead. Then he brought the elephant, upon which he made Adam mount, put a lance in his hand and shoes of gold on his feet.
ወነገደ ፣ ሚካኤል ፡ ተጽዕኑ ፣ አፍራሰ ፣ ጸዓድወ ፣ "ወአንፈርዐጹ ፤ ወሱራፌላዊያን ፡ ነሥኡ ፡ መስቀለ ፡ ብርሃን ፣ ወሐለዩ ፤ ወአኀዙ ፣ ማዕጠንተ ፡ ፳፻ሮ ፡ ዘወርቅ ፡ ፳፻ድ ፡ዘ ብሩር ፡፳፻፳ ፡ ዘኀጺን ፣ ፳፻፳ ፡ ዘ]ሰንፔር ፤ ፳፻፳ፎ ፣ ዘኢያስሏስ ፡ ፳፻፳ ፡ ዘ|ሕብረ ፡ ሰ
The companions of Mikä'êl rode white horses * that pranced; the seraphim carried the cross of light and sang; they had 570 censers of gold, 570 of silver, 570 of iron, 570 of sapphire, 570 of jasper, 570 of sardonyx color, 570 of chalcedony color, 570 of topaz, 570 of


[p. 17] {#po.1.p17}
ርድዮን ፡ ፳፻፳ ፤ ዘሕብረ ፡ ከርከዴን ፡ ፳፻ድ ፡ ዘወራውሬ ፡ ደ፻ሮ ፡ ዘብረሌ ፡ ፳፻ሮ ፡ ዘአ መቴስጢኖስ ፤ ወዝኩሉ ፡ ለክብረ ፡ አዳም ፡ ተግሀደ ፤ ወበኅብረተ ፡ ቃል ፡ ይቤሉ' ፡፳አ ብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡፳ወልድ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ 8ውእቱ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ወእገሪሁ ፡ ይመስል ፡ ከመ ፡ እብነ ፡ ሰንፔር ። ለአምላክነ ፡ ቴር ። ወበእንተ ፡ ኪኑ ፡ ለአምላክነ ፡ መንክር ፡ ሰማዕነ ።
crystal, and 570 of amethyst. All this display was exhibited in honor of Adam, and with a unanimous voice they said: One is the holy Father; one is the holy Son; one is the Holy Spirit. The foot of our excellent God resembles a sapphire stone, and as for its craftsmanship, we have learned that it is admirable.

And the man of God spoke * and said to me: If a man celebrates the commemoration of the angels, will God have mercy on him? - And he answered: Yes, if he also celebrates the feast of the prophets and the apostles. - And the angel resumed and said to me: Listen while I tell you what power has been given to the angels. There was one of the sons of men who had never done good a single day, but who had lived in evil since his birth; he had only one quality: every month, he celebrated the feast of Mikä'êl and had pity on the poor. This man died; the demons uttered cries of joy regarding him and said: This is a soul that belongs to us, * and they came to claim it from God. God said to them: Choose between these two things: Mikä'êl will hide this man and you will search for him, or you will hide him and Mikä'êl will search for him, but if he finds him, he will keep him. These demons said: What shall we do? If Mikä'êl hides him, we will not find him, for he will place him near the throne of the Divinity, and where shall we find him? Then they answered: We will hide him ourselves. And these impure demons hid this man in a corner of Gehenna, in the place

ርኩሳን ፣፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልገተ ፡ ገሃነም ፡መትሕተ ፣ ታሕቲት ፣ ወይቤሉ ፣ ይእዜ ፣ ይኅሥሦ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ እመ ፡ ይረክቦ ፡ ወይ'ስዐፎ(7)' ፡ ወይረክቦ ፡ ሚክኤል ።
the deepest. - Then they said: Let Mikä'êl search for him now, and if he finds him, * let him send him back. And Mikä'êl discovered him.

Mikä'êl said: "Go away, depart from this place." Then he entered Sheol and plunged, but he did not find the one he was looking for. A first time, he brought out with his wings 60,000 damned (?), and thus with his six wings, he brought out each time 60,000 damned (?), a second time he brought out more, and did not find this man. He searched a third time minutely, overturned Gehenna, and brought out this man. The number of those who came out because of him was 546,000. Among them were pagans, and the angels of heaven said: This thing is astonishing (?) God says in the Gospel: "He who believes and has been baptized * will be saved, but he who does not believe will be damned." How then did these come out? - And you, son of man, have you heard it said that pagans have been saved? But they did not enter paradise without being baptized, for Mikä'êl baptized them and they were resplendent like the sun. - The holy Abba fell into admiration and cried out: "It is possible for Amanuel to do everything." - Again Gabriel resumed and said: "I am surprised because of the children of men; they do not reward the one who has done them good, it is barely that one meets a few (?)."
ወበእንተኒ ፡ ስጥና ፡ ኢይምሰልከ ፡ ዘረሳዕኩ ፡ ለከ ፡ ተናግሮ ፣ ወቀዳሚሁሰ ፣ ተመየ
As for Setnä, do not believe that I forget to speak to you about him. First of all,

ነ ፡ ወመጽአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ገነት ፡ ከመ ፣ ያስሕቶ ፡ ለአ"ዳም ፤ ወቅድመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ረከበ ፡ዖ ፈ! ፡ ጸዓዳ ፡ ወስሙ ፣ አርዝል ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ስጥና ፡ ግበር ፣ ዘእቤለከ ፣ ወይቤ ፡ ምንተ ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ ጹረኒ ፡ ወአብጽሐኒ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሔዋ ፤ ወይቤሎ ፡ እንቢ ። ወሖረ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወኅለ ፈ ፡ ወረከበ ፡ ፆፈ ፡ ሐመልሚለ ፡ ወስሙ ፡ ብርል ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ስጥና ፣ ጹረኒ ፡ ወአብጽ ሐኒ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሔዋን ፡ ወአንቢ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ወአበየ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ረከበ ፡ ዖፈ፣ ቀይሐ! ፣፡ዙልን ታሁ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፤ ጹረኒ ፡ ወአብጽሐኒ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሔዋ ፡ ወአበዮ ፣ ወአበይዎ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ አዕዋፍ ፡ በበ ፡ ዘመዶሙ ።
he used guile and came into the garden to make * Adam succumb. He met, before any other, a white bird whose name is 'Arzel. Setnâ said to him: Do what I am going to command you. - The bird answered: What? - Setnâ resumed: Carry me to Eve. - The bird replied: I do not want to. Setnâ left, went further, and found a green bird called Berel. He said to him: Transport me to Eve. - The bird answered no and refused. Setnâ then met an entirely red bird and made the same request to him. This one also refused, and it was the same for all the birds of every kind.

He then headed toward the animals and first met a large animal whose name is Falfal, which means in Ethiopian Harmaz * with him was also a lion. Setnâ said to them: Transport me to Eve. - They answered: No, we do not want to, and refused. He then found a wicked animal named Yeberd, which means in Ethiopian Namer, and said to him: Carry me to Eve. He also refused. Setnâ went further and found an impure animal named Fenfenet and in the language of the Abyssinians Zeeb, who also refused. Then he met a black animal called Magdel, which is the Deb. This one refused again. He then found the Haräweyâ which is the Mafeles, whose

He said to him: Transport me to Eve, and this animal refused. He then met an animal called Taman in the language of the Abyssinians and which, at first sight, resembles a young camel. Setnâ made the same request to him and he accepted.

Setnä mounted on the body of the animal and thus came to place himself before Eve and said to her: "What did your God command you?" - She answered him: He said to us: Eat of the fruit of every tree that is in the garden, except for one of which you shall not eat. - And the serpent, through the mouth of Setnâ, said to her: If he spoke to you thus, it is so that you might not be God like him. - And Eve took [of the fruit of this tree], she ate of it and gave some to her husband. They both ate of it together. The name * of this tree that Adam and Eve ate is sezen, which means sendälé in the language of the Ethiopians. But do not imagine that it was similar to a tree of this world, to an ear of grain of this world. Its appearance was imposing; the ear had 150,000 grains. And the trees that surrounded it were [four in number], one on the side of the east, one toward the west, one toward the north, and one toward the south, just as Our Lord said to the cherubim and the

And it is the body of Christ * that the seraphim themselves touch only with respect. When Adam succumbed and ate of the fruit of the tree, it was three o'clock. And just as the tree suffered through Adam, so Our Lord suffered on the cross, and before being crucified, Pilate had him given forty lashes. Let us pass to the history of the people of the flood. At that time, angels descended from heaven and when they had taken the body of man, the madness of sin seized them; they were excluded from the mysteries they had seen in heaven.

Tigâna showed how to make the shield; Horêëri how to play the cithara; Ybë, how to work iron; Mêgêd, how to ride a horse. Negodi learned the signs that indicate the sources of thermal waters, how to heal the sick in this way, and the suitable hour to heal them (?). One of them wanted to snatch everything that the children of Cain (Qâyal) had taken, and took in an instant 50 sâdâla, that is to say 50 'entalam *, which he carried in his right hand. O what an astonishing thing, gift of the one who created the weak and the strong! Gargé taught them how to grind; Sétêr showed them how to knead; Gimêr, how to draw their food from the dough (7); Zârê, how to milk the animals; Hegegê, how to work wood and build the roofs of houses; Tentorab, ... how to make doors; Sêper, how to make curdled milk; Halêgê taught them sculpture; Hêder, how to make trees bear fruit; Sinô, how to build; Τοῦ, how to make pottery; Artorbegäs, how to make sewing tools; Cébêdêguëz, how to make eye salve; Zârë, how to prepare * beer; Béténëlâdas, how to build ovens for bread; Nâfil, how to plant trees; Yärbeh, how to split wood; 'Élyo taught them dance; Pênêmus, architecture and writing; Agâlêmun, how to put a yoke on oxen and make the handle of the plow; Kuers, how to make the plow and the whip. Akor, how to work bronze.
አየ ፡ ጸሪበ እርፍ ፤ወሰሪሐ ፣ ጽጉራፍ ፡ ወከርፈስ ። ወአኮር ፡ አርአየ ፣ገቢረ ፡ናሕክ። ወቦእምኔሆሙ ፡ እለ ፣ አርአዩ ፡ ጸሪበ ፡ ጽኅድ ፡ ወዙሓ ። ወዝኩሉ ፡ አርአዩ ፡ ወሰ

ግ ፡ ወአቤርግያ ፡ እለ ፡ ተበርሁ ፣ ርእሶሙ ፣ በደመና ፡ ወመሀሩ ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ ተላህዮ ፡ ጣበት ፤ ወ"ኔር ፡ ምስለ ፣ ዘቤሬኒጋድ ፣ መሀሩ ፣ ተላህዮ ፣ አታውማ ፡ ወአኪስ ፡ መሀ ሩ ፡ ቀርቂስ ።
this was taught by Wasag and Abêrgya whose heads shine in the clouds; they taught men to play tabat; * Nêr and Zabëréniguëêd showed them the game Atawma, and Akis, the circus game.
ወበእንቲአሆሙ ፣ መጽአ ፡ ማየ ፡ አይኅ ፡ ዲበ ፣ ምድር ፡ ወ፪ ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ ወ ፡ ድእ ምድር ፤ ወዝኒ ፡ አስራብ ፡ ዘተርኀወ ፡ በውእቱ ፣ መዋዕል ፡ በዝኅ ፡ ወተመልአ ፡ እኩይ ፤ ላዕለ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወዐቢየ ፡ ዝሙተ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እስከ ፡ እንስሳ ፡ አብዱ። ወኢአብ ፡ ወልይ ፡ወ አልቦ ፡ ወልድኒ ፡ ዘያአምር ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወኢአብ ፡ ወልዶ ፤ ወኅበ ፡ አራዊት ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ተ ዐደዉ ፤ወሰክየት ፡ ምድር ፡ ወአውየወት ።
It is because of them that the flood came upon the earth. Seven in heaven and seventy on earth, such are the cataracts that were opened in those days. Malice was great and had filled the earth. A great impurity reigned there; the animals themselves were led astray. The father no longer recognized his son nor the son his father; men sinned with the animals (?). The earth was moved by it and groaned.
ወይቤላ ፡ ፈጣሪ ፡ አርምሚ ፡ ዐይንየ ፡ ይሬአዮሙ ፡ ወእዝንየ ፡ ይሰምዖሙ ፣ ወትእግ ሥትየ ፡ ያ'ረም[ም ፡ ሎሙ ፤፣ ወበውእቱ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ በዝኀ ፡ ዝሙት ፡ በበቅል ፡ ዘኢ አዘዘ ፡ እግዚአብሒር ። ወበውእቱ ፡፣ መዋዕል ፡ ተበቀሎሙ ፡ ለረዓይት ፣ ወወፅአ ፣ ቃ ል ዘይብል ፤ ተቀትሉ ፡ በበይናቲክሙ ፡፣ በሰይፍ ። ወውኅጠቶሙ ፡፣ ማይ ፡እስከ ፡እንግ ድአቲሆሙ ፤ ወተመትሩ ፡ ርእሶሙ ፡ በሰይፍ ። ወበውእቱ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ኖኅ ፡ ምስለ ፣ ደቂቁ ፡ ዐቀባ ፡ ለጽድቅ ፡ ወድኅነ ፡ አሙስና።
The creator said to it: Silence! My eye sees them and my ear hears them. My patience has been great * for them, but today the impurity is sufficient for the vengeance that God has not yet ordered. Then he took vengeance on the giants; one heard a voice that said: Kill one another by the sword. Then the water engulfed them up to the chest and they cut off their heads with their swords. At that time Noah (Noh) and his sons had kept justice and were saved from destruction.
ወአለ ፣፡ በመትልው ፡ ኢዐቀብዋ ፡፣ ለጽድቅ ፣ ወአኀዙ ፣ ይሕንጹ ፣ ማኅፈደ ፤ ወእን ዘ ፡ ይነድቁ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ይሰምዑ ፡ ድምፀ ፣ መላእክት ። ወይቤ ፡ እግዚእ ፣ ንዑ ፡ ንረድ ፡ ወንርአይ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመሕያው ፡ ዘገብሩ ፣ እስመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ግብሮሙ ፡
Later their descendants did not observe justice, they began to build a tower. While they were building it, they arrived [at a place] where they heard the noise of the angels. And the Lord said: Let us go down and see what the children of men are doing *. For all their work is vain and idle,


[p. 24] {#po.1.p24}
በከ ፣ ወፅሩዐ ፡ ወነገሮሙኒ ፡ በከ ፡.ወፅሩዐ ። ወገፍትአ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለማኅፈድ ፡ ወሞቱ ፡ በንጥረ ፣ መሬት ፣ ወሐመድ ፡ ድዘ ፣ ዘእንበለ ፡ አንስት ፡ ወደቅ ፣ ወእንዘ ፡ አ፳ ነገሩ ፡ ለዙሉ ፡ ተወለጠ ፡ አሜሃ ፡ ወስእኑ ፡ ተዋሥኦተ ፡ ነገር ፣ ወተዘርዉ ፡ ለለ ፣ብሔ ሮሙ ፡ወተዘርዉ ። ዝንቱ! ፡ አስማተ ፡ ልሳናቲሆሙ ፡ ለደቂቀ ፡ ሴም ፤ አሪ ፡ አዚ ፡ ሶሪ ፡ ፅብሪ ፡ ፊሊ፣ ኬሊ ፤ ሄኒ ፡ ሴኒ ፣ አኤዩ. ፤ ተኬሊ ፤ኖጊ ፣ ቄኒ ፤ ሜልያሪ ፡ ከክሊ ፣ ማቲ ፣ ሌሚ ፣ ሕሕ ኒ ፡ ሴሜዴ ፡ ዬዬኒ ፡ ኬጤጢ ፡ ሌሜሚ ። አሴሪ ፡ ቶሪ ። ሞዲ ። ወፈያድሊሆሙ ፡፳ቿ። ወኦሪ ፡ አረብ ፡ ብሂል ። በከመ ፡ ትቤ ፡ ኦሪት ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ እለ ፣ ይነ”"ብሩ ፣ ሴሪ ፣፡ዘሚ ን ፡ ይብልዎሙ ፡ ወዖሪ ፡ ፅብራዊያን ፣ ይብልዎሙ ።፡ ፊሪ ፡ ፋርስ ፣፤ ብሂል ። ወኬሊ፡ ከለዳዊያን ፡ ወሄኒ ፤ ሄኖሳውያን ፡ ብሂል ። ወሴሪ ፡፤ አሴራዊያን ፡፤ ብሂል ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ወ ልዱ ፡ ለሴም ። ወትኩሊ ፡ ትኩን ፡ ብሂል ፡ ኖጊ ፡ ናጌባዊያን ፡ ብሂል ፣ ወቀኔ ፡ ቀናዊያ ን ፡ ብሂል ፤ ሜሊ ፡ ሜላዊያን ፡ ብሂል ፣ ያሬ ፡ ያሬማዊያን ፡ ብ ፤ ክክኒ ፡ ከናናዊያን፣ብ ሂል ፤ ማቲ ፡ ማቴናን ፡ ብሂል ፤ ሌማ ፡ ሌማዊያን ፡ ብሂል ፣ ሕንኒ ፡ ጌኖታዊያን ፡ ብሂ ል ፤ ስምዲ ፡ ስምዳዊያን ፡ ብሂል ፤ ዬዬኒ ፡ ዮናናዊያን ፣፤ ብሂል ፤ ኬጤጢ ፡ ኬጤዎን ፡ ብሂል ፤ ወሌሜሚ ፡ ሌማዊያን ፡ ብሂል ፡ ሶሪ ፡ ሶርያዊያን ፡ ብሂል ፣ ወሞጺ ፡ ሞጻዊያ ን ፡ ብሂል ። እሉ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ በበ ፡ነገዶሙ ፡ "ዘሴም ፡ ወበበ ፡ ልሳኖሙ ።
and their words are likewise vain and useless. Then he overturned the tower, many perished by the lightning of the earth and the dust; there were 70,000 of them without counting the women and children. They then had only one language; it was struck with confusion and they could no longer speak to one another. They went each to a country and were dispersed. Here are the names of the languages of the children of Shem: 'Ari, 'Azi, Sori, 'Ebri, Fili, Këli, 'Hêni, Séni, 'A'êyi, Takêli, Nogi, Qêni, Mêleyâri, Kakeli, Mâti, Lêmi, Heheni, Sémêdé, Yëyêni, Kêététi, Lêmêmi, 'Asêri, Tori, Môdi. - Their names are eight in number. 'Ori means 'Arab, just as you call the people 'Orit those who remained *. By Sêri one designates Zamin and by 'Ori ('Ebri?), the Hebrews. Firi means Fars. Kêli means Kalâädäwiens and Hêni the Hnosäwiens; and Séri means Aséräwiens, it is a son of Shem. Tekuli means Tekun; Nogi, the Nagêbäwiens; Qanê, the Qanäwiens; Mêli, the Mêlâwiens; Yarê, the Yârêmawiens; Kekeni, the Kanânäwiens; Mâti, the Matênân; Lêma, the Lêmawiens; Heneni, the Hënotawiens; Semdi, the Sémadäwiens; Yéyêni, the Yonänäwiens; Kétêti, the Kétéwon; Lêmêmi, the Lëmâwiens; Sori, the Soryäwiens, and Mosi, the Mosäviens. These are the peoples descended from Shem, according to their families * and their languages.


[p. 25] {#po.1.p25}
ወደቂቁኒ ፤ ለካም ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ልሳናቲሆሙ ፤ ቅብጢ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ሐበሲ ፡ ኖዘዚ ፡ ዴን ኪል ፡ ዴሜሳዊ ፡ እንዚ ፡ እንድሪ ፣ ሱሊ ፡ ዲዱቢ ፡ ከኒ ፡ መንበሪ ፣ ተሪብኒ ፡ ቶሪ፣፡ዱ ሪ0ሚኪ ፡ ኩዕዚ ፡ ሄሚ ፡ ሴቢ ፡ ሴሬዊ ፡ሌዚ።
As for the children of Ham, such are their languages: Qebti, Gebes, Habasi, Nozâzi, Dênkil, Démésäwi, 'Enzi, 'Enderi, Suli, Didubi, Kani, Manbari, Taribni, Tori, Duri, Miki, Kue'ezi, Hêmi, Lêbi, Séréwi, Lêzi.
ቅብጢ ፡ ቅብጥ ፡ ብሂል ፤ ወግብጽ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ብሂል ፤ ወሐበሲ ፣ ዘውእቶሙ ፡ ትግራ ይ ፤ ወኖቢ ፡ ዘውእቶሙ ፡ ኖባዊያን ፡ ብሂል ፤ ፌሲ.፡ ኤሎፍሊ ፡ ብሂል ፣ ወዛሪ ፡ ዘራ ዊያን ፡ብሂል ፤ ሕዝብ ፡ እለ ፡ ይነብሩ ፡ መንገለ ፡ አድባረ ፡ እሳት ፣ ወሊባ ፡ ዐረቢሆሙ ፤ ለእሉ ፤ ወባሊ ፡ ይነብሩ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ትነግሥ ፣ ብእሲት ፣ መንገለ ፡ ሠርቁ ፡ ለያፌት ፤ ወለ ይእቲ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ እንተ ፡ትነግሥ ፡ ሶዕበ ፡ ቀርበ ፡'መዋዕሊሃ ፡ ይእኅዝዋ ፡ ሰያጥን ፣ወ ያነግሠ ፡ ፪ሀየንቴሃ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ያነግሠ ፣ ለውእቱ ፡ ብሔር ፥ እስከ ፡ ለዓለም ፣ ወዜዚ ፡ ዛጓ ፡ ብሂል ፤ ዛሂ ፡ ሴሆ ፡ ብሂል ፤ ምሲ ፡ ሜስ ፡ ብሂል ፣ ሐዚ ፡ ሐዞ ፣ ብሂል ፣ ወናኪ፣ ደንኬልን ፡ ብሂል ፤፣ ወሴሚ ፣ ዐረቢሆሙ ፣ ወሴዊ ፡ ሴዋ ፣ ብሂል ፤ ወእንዚ ፡ አንጎት፣ ብሂል ፤ ወእንድሪ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ስልዕዳን ። ወሶሪ ፡ ሰራዊቱ ፡ለስልዕድን ፣ወዴዱቢ ፡አኀ ዊሆሙ ፤ ወኮኒ ፡ ካናአን ፤ ወመንበሪ ፡ መንበርታ ፤ ወዴሪ ፡ እንድርታ ፣ ብሂል ፣ወቡበኒ፣ ብጐና ፡ ብሂል ፤ ወአኮ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ደቀ ፡ ከም ። ወእሉ ፡ ዘክም ፡ ዱሪ፡፣ደብር ፡ ብሂል ፤ ማኪ ፡ መለኪ ፡ ወኩዕዝ ፡ ዙዔን ፡ ብሂል ፤ -ሃሚ ፡ ሐም ፡ ብ"ሂል ፤ ሌቢ ፡ ሊባን ፡ ብሂ
Qebti means Copt, Gebes means Egyptian; the Habasi are the Tigreans; the Nobi are the Nubians; Fêsi means Philistine (?); Zâri means Zâräwiens, a people who live toward the mountains of fire; the Libâ are to the west of these. The Bâli inhabit (the country) where a woman reigns, to the east of Japheth. As for this woman who reigns, when her days approach *, the demons seize her and make another reign in her place. It is thus that they will make this region governed until the end of the world. Zêzi means Zaguâ; Zâhi means Séhô; Mesi means Mês; Hazi means Hazo; Näki means Dankêlen; and to the west of these are the Sémi. Séwi means Séwa (Shoa); 'Enzi means 'Angôt and 'Enderi is Sel'edan, Sôri, the army of Sel'edan and Dêdubi (are) their brothers. Kôni is Kâna'ân; Manbari, Manbartâ; Dêri, Enderta; Buni means Beguenâ. And these are not all the sons of Ham; those of Ham (are) Duri (which) means Dabr; Mâki, Malaki, and Kue'ez means Kue'ên. Hami means Ham *; Lêbi means Libân; Sôri, Sirê; Lëgi, Lago, the envious (7)


[p. 26] {#po.1.p26}
ል። ሶሪ ፡ ሲሬ ፡ ብሂል ። ሌጊ ፡ ለጎ ፡ ብሂል ። መደንጽዋን ፡ ወቁሪ ፡ ወቀርት ፡ ብሂ ል ፡ ወቀርተ ፡ እብን ፡ ክቡራን ፡ አመ ፡ ገብረ ፡ መቅደሰ ፡ ሰሎሞን ። ወከመዝ ፡ ንብረ ቶሙ ፡ ወልሳናቲሆሙ ፡ ለውድድዶ ፡ ክም ፤ ወፕልቁቄ ፡ ልሳናቲሆሙ ፡ ፲።
Waquri means Wagart (cutters), the cutters of precious stones when Solomon built the temple. Such are the regions and the languages of the children of Ham. They are 30 in number.
ወዝውእቱ ፡ ልሳናቲሆሙ ፡ ለደቂቀ ፡ ያፌት ፡ ሮሜን ፣ አፍንንን ፣ አጥርኒ ፣ አፍል ክኒ ፡ ናኒ ፡ ካለኒ ፡ አሪ ፣ ይሲ ፡ ይኒ ፡ ዲቢዙሪ ፡ ያዲ ፡ ፒሪ ፣ ማሪ ፣ ሴጢ ፡ በሪ፡ና ሪ ፡ ወእሉ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ ያፌት ፡ በበነገዶይሙ ።
And here are the languages of the children of Japheth: Romên, 'Afengên, 'Aterni, 'Aflekni, Nâni, Kälani, 'Ari, Yesi, Yeni, Dibikueri, Yadi, Piri, Märi, Séti, Bari, Näâri. These are the children of Japheth according to their families.
ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ተረክበ ፣ አብርሃም ፡ ምእመን ፡ ወበዕለተ ፡ ልደቱ ፡ በርሀ ፡ ቤት ፤ ወድቁ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ወኮነ ፡ "ጽራኅ ፡ በዐቢይ ፡ ቃል ፡ ወይቤ ፤ ወይልየ ፡ ወይልየ ፡ ተ ወልደ ፡ ዘይዴድቃ ፡ ለመንግሥትየ ፤ ወበከየ ፣፡ ወአስተማሰለ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ዘይውያያ ፡ ለ ቤተ ፡ ማኅደርየ ። ወቦእ!ለ ፡ ይቤሉ ፡ እምነ ፣ ይእዜ ፡ ቅትልዎ ፡ ለዝ ፡ ሕፃን ። ወዘንተ ፡ ዘይቤሉ ፡ አእሚሮሙ"'" ፡ ዘተውህሀበ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ለአብርሃም ፡ ወእግዚእ ፡ ወደየ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልበ ፡ አቡ ሁ ፡ ለአብርሃም ፡ ምሕረተ ፣፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ለሰያጥን ፤ እምአይቴ ፡ አንትሙ ፡ እለ ፣ት ብሉኒ ፡ ቅትል ፡ ወልድየ ፡ ጸጋ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአልሐቆ ፡ ለሕፃን ፣ ወበሠርቀ ፡ሳ ብዕ ፡ ወርዓኅ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ በሌሊት ፡ ወርእየ ፡ ወርኀ ፡ ወከዋክብተ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ለ ዘ ፡ "ፈጠረ ፡ እሎንተ ።
Later Abraham was found believing. The day of his birth his house shone, many were overturned and one heard * a great cry, uttered by a loud voice, which said: "Woe to me, woe to me! He who will overturn my royalty is just born". And [the one who spoke thus] wept, uttered riddles (?), and cried out: "[it is] he who will burn my dwelling". Some said: From now on, kill this child. And those who gave this advice, [gave it] after having known the grace that had been granted to Abraham. But the Lord had put pity in the heart of his father; the latter said to the demons: Where do you come from, you who advise me to kill my son, who is a gift of God, and he raised the child. At the beginning of the seventh month, he went out during the night, saw the moon and the stars, and cried out: God who * created these stars!


[p. 27] {#po.1.p27}
ወሶቤሃ ፣ ይቤሎ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፤ አነ ፡ አምላከ ፣ አበዊከ ፣ አጸድቀከ ፣ ወአዐቢየ ከ ፡ ወወሀቦ ፡'ኪዳነ ፡ ሕግ ፡ ወሥርዓት ።
Then God answered him: It is I, the God of your fathers; I will sanctify you and make you great, and he gave him a code of law and institution.
ወተገዝረ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ በእደ ፣ ገብርኤል ፡ ወሚካኤል ፡ ዘተሐበዮ ። ወይቤ ፡ ብእ ሴ ፣ እግዚአብሐር ፣ መድምም ፡ ዝነገር ፡ ዘምስጢር ፣ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘረከበ ፣ እምሰብእ ። ወይቤለኒ ፡ መልአክ ፤ ወዝኒ ፡ ኢነገሮ ፣ ለሙሴ ፡ ከመ ፡ ተገዝረ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ በእደ ፡ መላእክት' ፡ ወዝኒ ፡ ኢነገሮሙ ፡ ለነቢያቲሁ ፣ ወለሐዋርያቲሁ ። ወስብሐት ፣ ሎቱ ፡ ለዘፈቀደ" ፡ ይክሥት ፡ ሎቱ ፣ ኅቡኣቲሁ ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፦ለሊ ሁ ፤ ወርቅ”የ ፡ ለዘ ፡ ፈቀድኩ ፡ እሁቦ ፤ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘይብለኒ ፤ ዘንተ ፡ አሕሠምከ ፡ ወ ዘንተ ፡ አሠነይከ ፣ ለዝ ፡ አብዐልከ ፡ ወለዝ ፡ አ[ን]ድይከ ።
Abraham was circumcised by the hand of Gabriel, and of Mikä'êl who assisted him. - And the man of God said: It is surprising, this revelation of mystery; there is no one among men who knows it. - The angel answered me: God did not tell Moses that Abraham had been circumcised by the hand of the angels; he told it neither to his prophets, nor to his apostles. Praises to him, who discovers his secrets to whom he wants. As he says himself: I give my * gold to whom I want and no one can say to me: You have done evil to this one and good to that one; you have enriched this one or you have impoverished him.
ወኩሉ ፡ ሥርዐተ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ዘአርአየ ፡ በአብርሃም ፣ አሚን ፡ በአብርሃም ፡ ወሥር ዐት ፣ በአብርሃም ። ወበዝኒ ፡ ናነክር ፡ እስከ ፣ ዙሉ ፣ ሕጉ ፡ ለአምላክነ ፡ ይጸንሕ ፡ ለ ዝሉፋ ፣ ወኢይስዐር ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ሄኖክ ፤ ርኢኩ ፣ ፲ወ፪ዕፀወ ፡ እለ ፡ ኢይነግፋ ፡ አቀ-ጽሊሆሙ ። ወኩሉ ፡ ፅፀው ፡ ይቡስ ፣ ወንጉፍ ፣ አቀ'ጽሊሆሙ ። ወአሉ ፡ ፲ወ፪8ዘይ ቤ ፡ ሄኖክ ፡ ምንት ፡ ውእቱ ። ወይቤ ፡ አባ ፡ በኀይለ ፣፡ ሚካኤል ። ወ|ይ](7)ቤ ፡መ ንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ፲ቃላት ፡ ዘኦሪት ፣ ኪዳነ ፡ ኖኅ ፣ ወግዝረተ ፡ አበው ፣ ወክህነተ ፡ መል ከ ፡ ጹዴቅ ፡ ወጥምቀተ ፡ ዮሐንስ ።
What he showed through Abraham was the whole work of justice; faith [was shown] by Abraham, the law [was shown] by Abraham. And in this we admire even the whole law of our God which remains always, without being abolished. Just as Enoch says: I saw fourteen trees not deprived of their leaves, and all the other trees dry and whose leaves have fallen. - And these fourteen trees of which Enoch speaks, who do they signify? Asked Abba Bahayla-Mikä'êl. - The Holy Spirit said: These are the ten words of the law, the covenant of Noah, the circumcision of the patriarchs, the priesthood of Melchizedek (Malka Sédêq), and the baptism of John.


[p. 28] {#po.1.p28}
ወባሕቱ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እ”ግዚአብሔር ፡ ለአብርሃም ፡ አመ ፡፣ ገብረ ፡ መሥዋዕተ ፣ አእመ ር ፡ ከመ ፡ ወረደ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ፈላሲ ፡ ይኩን ፡ በምድረ ፡ ነኪር ፡ ወይቀንይዎሙ ፡ ፲ወ ፪ዓመት ። ወባሕቱ ፡ ዝሰ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘሰሐቀት ፡ ሳራ ፣ ኢአሚና ፡ ቃለ ፡ እግዚ አብሔር ። ወሶበ ፡ ኢድኅነ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ እመ ፡ ኢወጽኡ ፡ ፅብራዊያን ፡ እምባሕር ። ወበ ሐዲስሂ ፡ ሕግ ፡ ይብል ፡ ዕበ ፡ ኢድኅነ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ እምእደ ፡ ሄሮድስ ፡ እመ ፡ ኢወ ፅኡ ፣ ሕዝብ ፡ እምባሕረ ፣ ኀጢኣት ። ወባሕቱ ፣፡ ኢይምሰልክ ፡ ዘመጽኡ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ሰገል ፡ እምድኅረ ፣ ተወልደ ፣ ወዘተል ዕሉሰ ፡ እምብሔሮሙ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ፪ዓም ፣ እምቅድ'"መ ፡ ይትወለድ ፡ እግዚእነ ። በ አምሳሊሁ ፡ ለኮከብ ፡ መርሖሙ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ አውፅኦሙ ፡ ለዕብራዊያን ። ወሶበ ፡ ዘበጦ ፡ ለደመና ፡ ኮነ ፡ ዝናም ፡ ወመላእክተ ፡ ደመናት ፡ ሔቡ ፡ ወአውረዱ ፤ ወበዝናም ፡ ሞቱ ፡ ፲፻ሰራዊት ፣፤ ወየዕለት ፣ ወ፻የለያልይ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቱር ፡ ወበሞር ፡ ሞቱ ፡ ፲፻ዕደው ፤ ወዕብራዊያንሰ ፡ ነበሩ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ማይ ፡ ቦዕለተ ፡ ወ፻ለያልየ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ወፅኡ ፡ አራዊተ ፡ ማይ ፣ ወቀተልዎሙ ፡ ለግብጽ ፣ ወቦእለ ፣ ይመስሉ ፡ ዐንበሳ ፡ ወ ቦ ፡ እለ ፡ ይመስሉ ፡ ነምረ ፡ ወቦ ፡ እለ ፡ ይመስሉ ፡ ድበ ፡ ወቦ ፡ አለ ፣ ይመስሉ፡ተ መነ ፤ ወ ኩሎሙ ፡ "ይኔቅዉ ፡ ኅቡረ ፣ ወደንገፁ ፡ ጥቀ ፡ ግብጽ ፤ ወዘእንበለ ፣ ይብጽ ሑ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ማይ ፡ ተቀበልዎሙ ፡ አሉ ፡ አራዊት ።
* God said to Abraham when the latter offered him a sacrifice: Know that [your children] will go down into Egypt, that they will be strangers in a land that will not be theirs, and that [the Egyptians] will oppress them for twelve years. This happened because Sarah had laughed, not having confidence in the word of God. If Moses had not been saved, the Hebrews would not have come out of the sea. In the new law, it is said: If Our Lord had not escaped the hand of Herod (Hérodes), the nations would not have come out of the sea of sin. But do not believe that the magi came after his birth nor that they came up from their country beforehand, two years * before Our Lord was born (9). In the form of a star, Mikä'êl led them. It is he also who brought the Hebrews out; when he struck the cloud, there was rain; the angels of the clouds drew water and made it fall. Through the rain died 1,000 warriors; then for three days and three nights, it was cold and through the cold died 1,000 men. As for the Hebrews, they remained in the water three days and three nights; then there came out of it aquatic beasts that killed the Egyptians. Some resembled lions, others leopards, others bears, others finally were similar to dragons. All * howled at the same time and the Egyptians were very frightened. Before arriving at the water, they encountered these animals.


[p. 29] {#po.1.p29}
ወበእንተዝ ፣ ይቤ ፡ መምህሮሙ ፡ ለጠቢባን ፤ እምነ ፣ አራዊት ፡ አለ ፣ የሐውሩ ፤ በጥርእ ፡ በቃል ፡ ደንገፁ ፡ ግብጽ ። ወፈረሱሰ ፣ ለፈርዓን ፣ ፈቀደ ፡ ወዒኦ ፡ ውስተ ፣ የብስ ። ወሙሴኒ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ንሴብሖ ፡ ይሴባሕ ፡ ስቡሕ ። ወፈረሰ ፡ ፋርዖን ፡ ሰረረ ፡ ፲ወ ፳በአመት ፡ ወደንገፁ ፡፤ ሕዝብ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ ስረተ ፡፣ ፈረሶ ፣ ለፈርዖን ። ወረገሞ ፤ ሙሴ ፡ ወእምአሜሃ ፡ ኅይሎ ፡ ለፈረስ ፡ እምፀአእድ' ፡ ተረፈ ፡፻እድ ፤ ወኩሉ ፡ ዘዚአሁ ፡ ለፈርዖን ፡ ተሰጥሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ማይ ፡ ወወረደ ፡ ውስተ ፡ሲኦል። ወ"በእንተዝ ፤ ይቤ ፡ ሕዝቅኤል ፡ ላሐወት ፡ ቀላይ ፡ ወወረደ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ፤ወላ ሐወት ፡ቀላይ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ሰቡዐ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ወሰቡዐ ፡ ለያልየ ፣ እንዘ ፡ ትበኪ ፡ ከ መ።ሕፃየን። ሽ
It is for this reason that the prince of the wise says: By the voice of the beasts that walk and cry out, the Egyptians were terrified. Pharaoh's horse wanted to pass on land, but Moses said: Nesébeho, yesëbah sebuh (We glorify him, let the glorious one be glorified) and Pharaoh's horse leapt to a height of 15 cubits. When the people saw the leap of Pharaoh's horse, they were afraid. Moses cursed it, and from that moment, the vigor of the horse was reduced by two-thirds. Everything that belonged to Pharaoh was swallowed up in the sea and descended into Sheol. * It is regarding this that Ezekiel says: "The abyss groaned and it descended into Sheol." The abyss that groaned is the sea, which for seven days and seven nights weeps like a child.
መንክራቲክ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ ወናሁ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ በእዘኒነ ፡ ሰማዕነ ። መንክራቲከ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ ዕፁብ ፡ ወናሁ ፡ ቃል ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለሊከ ፣ ወመንበርከ ፡ ሕኑጽ ፡ በርትዕ ፡ ወጠፈሩኒ ፡ በጽ ድቅ ፡ ወቅድመ ፡መንበር ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ውልውል ፡ነፋስ ፡ ወአህለለ ፡እሳት ፡እንተ ፡ ዐውዱ፣ ይእኅዞ ፡ ለነፋስ ። ወእንተ፪ ፡ ገበዋቲሁ ፡ ለነፋስ ፣ በበ፲፳ ፡ ወነድ ፡ ይመስል ፡ ውእቱ፡ነ ፋስ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘይሬእዮ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ዐይነ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወለሰረገላ ፡ ኪሩቤል ፡ ይመስቦ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ነፋስ ። ወህሀየ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ባሕረ ፡ እሳት ፣ ወፕላቀ፡ሁ ፡ ወቀላያተ ፡ ብር
These are your wonders, O Lord, and behold, we have all heard them recounted with our ears. Your wonders, O Lord, are admirable; you are a holy word, your throne is built upon righteousness and its roof rests upon justice. Before the throne is a billowing wind and a burning fire, which all around seizes the wind, on four sides, for [a length of] 10,000 cubits. This wind resembles a flame, and no one sees it but God. This wind carries * the chariots of the cherubim (Kirubêl). There is the sea of fire and its number is [seven]; there are the seven abysses of light, the seven resplendent stars, the seven

This is the genealogy of the patriarchs: Abraham begat Isaac (Yeshaq); Isaac begat Jacob (Yä'eqob); Jacob begat twelve patriarchs.

These were jealous of Joseph and sold him. But before selling him, they said: Let us kill him. Rôbêl (Reuben) prevented them and said to them: Far from us be this thought * of killing him, and he saved him. And because he had saved Joseph, his sin was forgiven him. While Joseph was taking shade under a tree, this tree said to him: Your brothers have the intention of putting you to death. Joseph was afraid and fled; he remained in the desert for three days and three nights and he was hungry. A stone said to him: Eat me, for I am bread; he ate it and gave thanks to his Creator. Water also came out of this bread. He moved a little away from that place and met a partridge hunter who asked him: Where do you come from? - He replied: I have hidden myself from my brothers out of fear. The hunter gave him one of the partridges he had caught in his nets. On the seventh day his brothers found him and sold him so that [he would go to] another country *. There too the grace of God did not abandon him. What then shall we say, O

men, if the grace of God was with him? Upon an accusation, he was imprisoned and there too the grace of God assisted him. As Solomon says: Even in prison, it did not leave him. The king named him ‘El, 'El and 'Abrir which means: Listen, listen! Other facts concerning the history of Joseph are reported in Genesis (the law). The Israelites went down into Egypt, and the Egyptians oppressed them. At that time lived the son of Fânika, whose name was Finâhas and the name of his wife Râhêl. While they were working on the bricks, with great fatigue, his wife who was carrying two twins in her womb miscarried and cried out: Have you forgotten your people *, God of Israel! Then God descended on Mount Sinai and said: I have seen the sufferings of my people, I have heard their cries of anguish and I have descended to save them.

Moses obeyed his Lord and this is the law that God imposed upon the children of Israel in the land of Egypt. He said to them: Take a lamb in the first month, on the tenth day, and keep it until the fourteenth day. feet * on the left are the prophets from Moses until Elijah and its hands on the right are the prophets from Samuel until Jeremiah (Érmeyäs). The fingers of its hands and its feet are the children of the prophets; its skin is the gift of the Holy Spirit that warms the peoples, as Solomon says: If two people sleep together, they will warm each other, which signifies the old and the new law. Its eyes are the apostles, for their eyes are a light for the whole world. Its two ears are the apostles Peter and Paul; the breath of its two nostrils is the ardor of faith in the martyrs; and its inner viscera the voice of the Scriptures; its belly the priests, faith, love *, baptism and the holy Sacrifice. The two horns of the lamb and its bone which is not broken (represent) the body of Mary which no man has approached.

It is for this reason that Isaiah says: A bruised reed that is not broken and a smoldering wick that does not go out. The bruised reed of which he speaks is the body of man (or [of the true] Adam) which was not broken on the cross and the smoldering wick is the sacrifice of the nations whose rite will never disappear.

with a belt and shall wear the headband (turban). And here is the symbolic (spiritual) explanation [of this passage]. When he says: the calf, he indicates Adam, as Enoch says: A white calf came out and from its flank came out the heifer; what Enoch told you relates to (the creation of) Eve. The horns of the altar which are sprinkled with the blood of the calf are the prophets who received the gift of prophecy. When he speaks of the fat of the intestines of the calf, this signifies the priests; its fatty covering and its two kidneys are Abraham, Isaac and Jacob.

* When he says: You shall burn the calf outside, after having brought it out of the camp, the calf is the second Adam, that is to say Christ, who died outside the city, as the Gospel recounts: "They brought him out of the city and led him away to crucify him." Adam himself, the father of the human race, went out of the earthly paradise and was burned by fire. But do not imagine that he was burned absolutely like those who were perverse among his children; prophets remained in Sheol and Sheol did not consume them, it was for them as cool as the abysses of water, for for him who has not sinned, Sheol is an abyss of mercy. It is for this reason that David says: "From the depths of the earth, you have brought me up again." *

I will now say how the Levites are consecrated. And the angel said to me: Just as the Levites, the priests are consecrated with bread, when the bishop ordains them. When they receive the bread, * they bring it back onto the paten and perform their [priestly] functions. And then, under various aspects, they see the mystery; some see it under the appearance of a perfect bread, others under the aspect of fire, others in the form of an ear of wheat.

Upon hearing this revelation touching the mystery, the man will cry out: You can do everything; there is nothing that is impossible for you. As Solomon says: Since ancient times, you resemble all sorts of things. But the priests, although they are ordained over all things, do not all apply themselves to considering the mysteries. Among them, there are those who during the whole day offer the sacrifice and see the sheep while it is slaughtered on the dish, others who, as usual *, offer the holy sacrifice without seeing the sheep. As the apostles say, they have the same (priestly) dignity, but their portions

ክፈልታተ ፡ ወሕዝበ ፡ ክርስቲያንሂ ፡ እንዘ'፣ አይነሥኡ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ወ፳ሃይማኖት ። ወአለሰ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ በትሕትና ፡ ልብ ፡ ኢይደልወነ ፡ ለቢሰ ፡ ክህነት ፣ ሎሙሰ ፣ ኀቤ ሆሙ ፡ ይኔጽር ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወርቱዕሰ ፡ ይደልዎ ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ ያጥሪ ፡ ትሕትና ። ወ ቦ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ጥምቀትኒ ፡ አለ ፡ ኢከሠቶሙ ፣ ልቦሙ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወበከመ ፡ ይትቀብኡ ፡ አሮን ፡ ወውሉዱ ፡ ከተማ ፡ ዙሉ ፡ አጽባዕቶመ። ወካህናትኒ ፡ ሶበ ፣ ይሠይምዎሙ ፡ ይቀብኦሙ ፡፣ መንፈስ ፡ቅዱስ ፡ ከተማ ፡ እዝኖሙ ፡እንተ ፡ የማን፣ ወከተማ ፡-እዶሙ ፡እንተ ፡የማን ፡ወዝ ፣ ይከውን ፣ በትእምርት ፡ መንክር ፡ ዘኢይትረአ ይ'፡ለዐይን።
(of grace = meris) are not equal; while the Christian people must receive (the mysteries) in a single spirit and a single faith. As for those who say in the humility of their heart: "I am not worthy to be a priest," God looks toward them. It is fitting and it is just that man should acquire humility; there are those to whom the Holy Spirit has not opened the intelligence after their baptism. Just as the extremities of all the fingers of Aaron and his sons were anointed, so the Holy Spirit anoints the extremity of the right hand and the extremity of the right ear of the priests, when they are consecrated. And this happens by a surprising sign invisible * to the eye.
"ወለዲቆናት ፡ከተማ ፡ አጽባዕት ፡እዶሙ ፡ለጸጋም ። ወኢትክሥት ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ለሰብእ፣ ዘእንበለ ፡ ለጠቢባን ፡ ወለማእምራን ፡ እሎንቱ ፡ ቃል ፡ ለእመ ፡ ከሠትከ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ አ ልብከ፣መክፈልተ ፡ ውስተ፣መንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ይቤለኒ ፡ መልአክ፣ ኀቤየ፡ዘተፈነወ። ወበእንተኒ ፡ ነፍስ ፡ ስእንት ፡ በእንተ ፡ ምንት ፡፣ ዘኢያአምር ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሕሊ ና ፣፣መዋቲ ፡ጽሉም ጾ። ዕ።
For the deacons, it is the extremity of the fingers of the left hand [which is anointed]. Do not reveal this to all men, but only to the wise and the doctors. If you reveal these words to all, you will have no part in the kingdom of heaven, said to me the angel who had been sent to me. As for the soul, it is powerless because of what man does not know (?), for the thought of the mortal is dark.
ወባሕቱ ፡ ገሐፍትሰ ፡ ትትረጐም ፡ በክርስቲያን ፣ እስመ ፣ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፣ው ስቴትነ ፡ ይትቀበር ። ወአመ ፡ ሞትነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይጸርኅ ፡ ወይብል ፡ አባ ፡ ወአቡየ ፡ በ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዳስ ፡ ወይብል ፡ ኦነፍስ ፡ መሐርኩኪ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘበላዕኪ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ወል ድየ። ፥
The basket signifies the Christians, for the body of Our Lord is buried in us. And when we die, he cries out: "Abba, my Father, through the Holy Spirit!" - The Father replies: "O soul! I have pity on you because you have eaten the body of my son."


[p. 36] {#po.1.p36}
ወሥጋኒ ፡ ዘይ”ከውን ፡ ሐመደ ፡ በውስተ ፡ መቃብር ፡ ያዐቅብዎ ፡ መላእክት ፣ ወይብ ል ፡ መጽሐፍ ፡ ብዙኃን ፣ ይትነሥኡ ፡ እምጸበለ ፡ ምድር ፡ እምነ ፡ ውዕየታ ፡ ለምድር ፤ ወለሊሁ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ያዐቅብ ፡ አዕጽምቲሆሙ ፡ ለጻድቃን ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዳንኤ ል ፣ ይትነሣእ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ መልአክ ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ዘይቀውም ፡ ለደቂቀ ፡ ሕዝብ ፣ ወይከው ን ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ሕማም ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ዘኢኮነ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ እምአመ ፡ ተፈጥረ ፡ ዓለም ። ሚካኤ ል ፡ ብሂል ፡ ዕፁብ ፡ ነገር ። ወዝኒ ፡ ዘቆመ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ዘቆመ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መስቀል። ወበከመ ፡ ሐመ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘሐመ ፡ እምሰብእ ፡ ኢቀደምት ፣ ወኢደኃርት ። ወ በአሪና ፣ ናመልኮ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ።
The body itself which * is in dust in the tomb is guarded by the angels. The Scripture says: Many will rise from the dust of the earth, from the conflagration of the earth. Our Lord himself guards the bones of the just. It is for this reason that Daniel says: Mika'êl will rise, the great angel, who is appointed over the children of the people. There will be by his doing a great suffering, such as there has not been since the world was created. Mika'êl signifies an admirable thing. And when he says that he rose, he indicates the Son who rose on the cross; and the sufferings that Our Lord endured, there is no one who can bear them among those who have lived or who will live. We adore him equally with the Father and the Holy Spirit.
ንግ”ባእኬ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ነገራ ፡ ለቅድስት ፣ ደብተራ ፤ ወዝውእቱ ፡ ቃል ፡ ዘአዘዞሙ ፤ ለደቂቀ ፡ አስራኤል ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፤ ያምጽኡ ፡ እምንዋየ ፡ ብሩር ፡ ወልብስ ፡ ወያክንት፣ ወፐፒራ ፡ለይ ፡ ወክዑብ ፡ ወሜላት ፡ ወጸጐረ ፣ ጠሊ ፣ ወማእአሰ ፡ በግፅ ፣ ሕሱይ ፡ወ ማሐን' ። ወማእሰ ፣ በግፅ ፡ ዘመሃን ፡ ብሂል ፡ አዲም ፡ ቀይሕ ። ወመጸጺትሂ ፡ ብሂል ፤ ሳራክ ፡ ብሂል ፣ ዘይበውእ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እግረ ፣ ሌዋዊያን ፡ ወአማን ፡ ተገብረ ፡ ዘአዘዘ፣ለ ሙሴ።
* Let us return to the description of the holy tabernacle. Here is the word that [Moses] addressed to the children of Israel. He said to them: Bring me, from among what you possess, silver, fabrics, hyacinth, purple, scarlet, byssus, millet, goat hair, prepared sheepskins, and mahan. The sheepskin that is called mahan is red leather, and masasit signifies särâk, that is to say what the Levites put on their feet. What he had commanded Moses was executed punctually.
ወዕዕ ፡ ዘኢይነቅዝ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ሶም ፡ ዕበ ፣ ይብል ፡ ፅፅ ፣ ጸሊም ፣ ዘውእቱ ፡ ዘይብል ዋ ፡ ዞጴ ፡ በግዕዝ ። ወዕዴሬ ፡ ትተረጐም ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወፅዴሬ ፡ ብሂል ፡ል ብስ ፡ ጻዐዳ ። ወፐፒረ ፡ ብሂል ፡ ዘነበ ፡ ላሀም ፡ ጻዐ”ዳ ፡ ዘይሰድድ ፣ አራዊተ ፣ ደቃው
When he speaks of the incorruptible tree and whose name is sôm, it is a question of a black tree that is called zopê in Ge'ez; podêrê alludes to the Holy Spirit, podêrê signifies a white garment; papira a white bull's tail * to


[p. 37] {#po.1.p37}
ቀ ፡ ወሜላት ፡ ጸዐዳ ፡ ሕብሩ ፤ ወዝንቱ ፣፡ ይትረጉም፤' ፡ በአዳም ፡ ሜላት ፡ ይነጽሕ ፡ እ ምዙሉ ፡ ልብስ ።
chase away insects; the mélât is white in color and is interpreted as Adam, the mêlât is the purest of fabrics.

When it is said that the columns of the tabernacle are of gold, gold designates the apostles, silver the prophets. The bars of the grilles of the columns, their tips and their bases, the utensils, the supports (?) of the columns which are at the top and at the bottom, and their shafts were of bronze. And they made in gold what they placed on the garment of Aaron. They put four rows of stones there, engraved the names of the children of Israel, and sealed it with each of these names.
ወዝውእቱ ፡ትርጓሜ ፡ መንፈሳዊ ፡ 8አእባን ፡ ይተረጐም! ፡ በፀወንጌል ። ወ፲ወ፪አስማ ተ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ይተረ”ኾም፤ ፡ በ፲ወ፪ሐዋርያት ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፤ ክብረ ፡ አበ ው።፡ውስተ ፡ ጾታ ፡ 8አእባን" ፡ ግሉፍ!። ውስተ ፡ ልብሰ ፡ካሀን ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዓለም ። ወዘን ተ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይብል ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ እስመ ፡ ክህን ፡ ይዜከር ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዓለም ። ዕበ ፣ የዐጥን ።
Here is the symbolic explanation of this passage: The four (rows of) stones signify the four gospels. The twelve names of Israel are explained * by the twelve apostles, as Solomon says: "The glory of the fathers is engraved on four rows of stones; on the garment of the high priest is the whole world." And if Solomon says that, it is because the high priest mentions the whole world when he burns the perfumes.
ወልብሰ ፡ ሎግዮን ፡ባዕ ፡ አውዱ ፡ ወ[ባዕ ፡ ወርዱ ፡ ዘይቤሂ ፡ ልብሰ ፡ ሎግዮን ፡ ሕብ ራ ፡ ይመስል ፡ ከመ ፡ ባሕርይ" ፤ ወእሪት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ወርዳ ፡ ወእዳ ፡ ወክብርት ፡ አምዙሉ፣ ልብስ ። ወኢይለብስዋ ፡ አሮን ፡ ወደቂቁ ፡ ዘእንበ[ለ ፡ በበዓላት ፡ ክቡራት ። ወልብሰ ፡ ቃስ ፡ ወልብሰ ፡ ሜጥራ ፡ ወልብሰ ፡ አጌ ፡ ጸሊም ፡ እንተ ፡ ይለብስዋ ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ዙስትር፣0፣ ወበጊዜ ፡ አብስሎ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ወልብሰ ፡ ሜጥራ ፡ ወትረ ፡ ለሥርዐት ፡ "ዚአሃ ፤
The logyon garment is as long as it is wide and the color of this garment which he calls logyon resembles that of the sea; it is equal in length and in width, it is the most precious of garments. Aaron and his sons only wore it on the days of great feasts; but the garment of Qâs, the miter and the 'Agê garment (cotton) which was black, they took at the moment of sweeping and when they cooked the meat. The miter was always worn for this ceremony.


[p. 38] {#po.1.p38}
ወአዕጻድሂ ፡ ወለለ ፡ ዐጸድ ፡ ኑጐ ፡ ርብዕ ፡ ወስዝር ፣ ወገብሩ ፣ መንጦላዕት ፡ ዘዳርከ ኖ ፡ ወዘሕብረ ፡ ከብድ ። ወመሰሉ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ኪሩቤን ፣፡ ወወደይዋ ፡ ውስተ ፡ 8አዕማ ድ ፡ ዘኢይነቅዝ ፡ ቅፍሎ ፡ ወርቅ ፤ ወመካይዲሆን ፡ ወርቅ ፤ ወፕኅተ ፣፡ ደብተራሂ ፡ በ ደርከኖ ፣ ግቡር ፣ ወውስቴታ ፣ ኪሩቤን ፡ መሰሉ ፡ ዘፍጥረት ።
* As for the enclosures, each had four cubits and a palm in width; they put a veil there the color of hyacinth and the color of liver, they represented a cherub inside and placed it between four columns of incorruptible wood covered with gold, the bases were of gold, the door of the tabernacle was made of yellow purple, and inside they represented cherubim of feterat (of creation).
ወፍጥረት ፡ ዘይቤሂ ፣ ክልኤ ፡ ጾታ ፡ ምንባሪሁ ፡ እንተ ፡ ፪ገበዋቲሁ ፡ ወእንተ ፡8፪ገጸ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወገጸ ፡ እንስሳ ፡ ገጸ ፡ ላህም ። ወገጸ ፡ ዐንበሳ ፣ ወገጾ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ይሥዕል ፡ ለሰብ እ ፤ ወገጸ ፡ ዐንበሳ ፡ ለዐናብስት ፡ ወንስርሂ ፡ ዘገጹ ፡ ይሥዕል ፡ ለአፅዋፍ ፡፣ ወገጸ ፡ እንስ ሳ ፡ ለእንስሳ ። ወእምክነፊሆሙ ፡ ይወርድ ፡ ጠለ ፡ ምሕረት ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ዘሰአሉ ።
What he calls feterat are two series of thrones toward the four sides and of which one [bears] a human face, the second an animal face, the third an ox face and the fourth a lion face. The human face represents man; the lion face the lions; the eagle also, whose face is represented, figures the birds and the animal face the animals. From their wings (of the cherubim) falls a rain of mercy * on those who implore them.
ወነዋ ፡ፍካሬሁ ፤ለነገር ፡ ይቤ ፡ ብርሃናዊ ፡ ለ ፡ ለሀልው፡ቃል ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ንብል ፡ በትርጓ ሜ ፡ መንፈሳዊ ። ዐጸድሰ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወማርያም ፡ ይእቲ ፣ ወከመሰ ፡ ዐጸድ ፡ውእቱ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ለሊሁ ፣ አነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዐጸደ ፡ ወይን ፡ ዘጽድቅ ። ወተኅፅቦ ፡ በ ማይሰ ፡ ዘይቤሂ ፡ ጥምቀት ፡ እንተ ፡ ኅቤነ ። ተኀፅቦሙ ፡ በማይ ፡ዕበ ፡ ታለብዕሙ ፡ ክሀነ ተ ፡ ሌዋዊያን ። ዘይቤሂ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ተጠምቀ ፡ ይትከሀን ፡ በብሉይሂ ፡ ወበሐዲስሂ ፡ መንፈቀ ፡ ሌሊት ፣ ሶበ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ነፍሐ' ፡ የጊዜ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ከመ ፡ ሕፃን ፡ዘኢለከፎ፣፡ር ስሐተ ፡ ኅጢአት ።
And here is the explanation of the passage relating to the enclosure, says the luminous (angel?); each true word we interpret thus allegorically: The enclosure of which he speaks is Christ and Mary. Christ himself says that he is a vine since he said: I am the true vine. When he says: You shall wash them with water, he designates the baptism that exists among us. - You shall wash them with water when you clothe them with the priesthood of the Levites, he also says, for after a man has been baptized, he is consecrated according to the old and the new law. In the middle of the night, when a man has been purified (by the immersion of baptism) three times, he becomes like a child that the malice of sin has not reached.


[p. 39] {#po.1.p39}
ወከመሰ ፡ እሙን ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ነገር ፡ ይቤሉ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፣ መንፈቀ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ "ሶበ ፣ተ ጠምቀ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ንጹሐ ፡ እምርእሱ ፡ እስከ ፡ እግሩ ። ወዕበ ፣ ኢተኅፅበ ፡ ይ ቴክዝ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፤ ወመልአክኒ ፡ ዘጽሙር ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ይይቴክዝ ፡ ወናሁ ፡ ዕፁብ ፡ ነገር ፡ ዝኒ ።
As proof of the truth of this word, the apostles say: In the middle of the night *, when a man is baptized, he is purified from head to toe. If he had not been washed, the Holy Spirit would be grieved by it, and the angel who is attached to him would also be saddened. And that is certainly a surprising thing.
ወቅድስት ፡ ልብሱ ፡ ለአሮን ፡ ይተረጐም ፡ በደናግል ፡ ንጹሓን ፣ ወዐይነ ፡ ማኅተምሂ፣፤ ይተረጐም ፡ በማኅተመ ፣ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለወልድ ። ወደርከኖ ፡ እንተ ፡ ጸገየት ፡ ይተረጐም ፡ በትስብእተ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ አስካል ፡ ዘጸገየ ፡ ዘወልድ ፡ እቱየ ፡ ሊተ-፡ ጽጌ ፡ ትስብእቱ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ። ወበእንተኒ ፡ ዐጸድ ፣ ዘመንገለ ፡ አዜብ ፡ ፻በእመት ፣ ወዐውዶ ፡ ዐጸድኒ ፡ ዘመንገለ ፣ ደቡብ ፡ ፳ ወዐአ”ማዲሆን ፣፡ ወመካየዲሆን ፡ ዘብርት ፡ ወመንገለ ፡ መስዕኒ ፡ ኗበአመት ፡ ወዐጸድኒ ፡ ዘመንገለ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ፪(?)በእመት ፣ ወአዕማዲሆን ፡ወአዕጻድኒ ፡ ዘመንገለ ፡ ጽበ ሕ፡፳በእመት ፡ ውእቱ ፣ ግድሙ ። ወመጣቅዕት ፡ ዘብሩር ፡ ወልብሰ ፡ ዐስቅ ።
Aaron's holy costume is interpreted by chaste and pure men; the engraving of the seal signifies the seal of the body of the Son; the flowering hyacinth purple represents the incarnation of Our Lord, just as Solomon says: "The flowering grape of my brother's son is mine,” it is the flower of the incarnation of Our Lord. As for the enclosure which is towards the south, it is 100 cubits; around it is another enclosure towards the north of 20 cubits *, its columns and their bases are of bronze, and towards the northwest there is also an enclosure of 20 cubits. The one on the sea side has seven (?) cubits and the columns and the enclosures which are towards the east have 20 cubits of width, silver trumpets and garments of precious fabrics.
ወአሉ ፡ አዕማድ ፡ ወአዕጻድ ፣ ይተረጐሙ ፡ በነቢያት ፡ ወሐዋርያት ፡ እስመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ አዕማድ ፡ የማኖሙ ፡ ወአዕማድ ፡ ጸጋሞሙ ፡ እለ ፡ በየማን ፣ ሐዋርያት ፡ ወእለ ፣ በፀጋ ም ፡ነቢያት ፡ ወአለ ፡ በመስፅዕ ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡ ወእለ ፡ በሰሜን ፣ ካህናት ። ወታፀታኒ ፡ እን ተ ፡ በወርቅ ፣ ቀሩልፍት ፡ ማርያም ፡ ይእቲ ፡ እንተ ፡ በንጽሕ ፣ ኅትምት ።
These columns and these enclosures signify the prophets and the apostles, for it says that there are columns on their right and on their left; those on the right are the apostles, those on the left, the prophets; those which are in the north are the martyrs and those which are in the south, the priests. The ark which was closed with a golden lock is Mary, who was sealed in chastity.


[p. 40] {#po.1.p40}
ወገብሩ ፡ ምሥሃለ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ታቦት ፣ ወ፪ኪሩቤን ፡ ይጹ"ልልዋ ፡ ወ፪ኪሩብ ፡በ ከመ ፡ ጸለሉ ፡ ምሥሃለ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ 8ወንጌላዊያን ፡ አሰርገውዋ ፡ ለቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ በ ጽላሌ ፡ ትምሀርቶሙ ። ወዐሥሩ ፡ ቃል ፡ በከመ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ታቦት ፣ ከማሁ፣ቃለ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኀደረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ከርሣ ፡ ለድንግል ።
They made a mercy seat above the ark and four cherubim * covered it with their wings (overshadowed it). Just as four cherubim spread their wings over the mercy seat, so four evangelists adorned the Christian church with the shadow of their doctrine. Just as the decalogue was placed in the ark, so the word of God resided in the body of the Virgin.
ወማእዳኒ ፡ እንተ ፡ ቅድሜሃ ፡ ትነብር ፡ ወእስርት ፣ ይእቲ ፡ በሕልቀት ፡ ዘወርቅ ፤ ማ እዳኒ ፡ አስተማሰሎ ፡ ለእግዚአ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ሕይወት ፡ እስመ ፡ ማእደ ፡ ሰመይዎ ፣፡ መጻሕፍ ት ፡ ለእግዚእነ ። ይቤ ፣፡ ኢሳይያስ ፡ ሥርዑ ፡ ማእደ ፣፡ ወብልዑ ። ወኢዮብኒ ፡ ይቤ ፡ማ እድከ ፡ ጥሉል ። ወዳዊትኒ ፡ ይቤ ፣፡ ወሠራዕከ ፡ ማእደ ፡ በቅድሜየ ። ወ፪ሕልቀት ፡ ዘወርቅ ፣ ዘይቤሂ ፣ ፪ሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ።
The table which is placed before the ark and which is attached to it by [four] golden rings, this table represents the Lord of glorious life, for the Scriptures call Our Lord a table. Isaiah (Isäyeyâs) says: "Set the table and eat." Job (Iyob) also says: "Your table is well furnished." And David (Däwit) cries out: "You have set the table before me." The four golden rings which are spoken of are the four patriarchs [of the churches].
ወገብሩ ፡ ተቅዋመ ፡ መሕቶት' ፡ “ወአዕፁቅ ፣ በየማና ፣ ወበፀጋማ ፣ ወተቅዋም ፡ክዘ ወርቅ ፣ ዘይቤሂ ፡ ወርቅስ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ወማኅቶትኒ ፡ ዘላዕሌሃ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ውእቱ ። ወ አዕጹቅኒ ፡ እለ ፣ በየማና ፣ ወበፀጋማ ፡ ኦሪት ፡ ወነቢያት ፣ ውእቶሙ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ሠርፅ፣ይ ወፅእ ፡ እምነ ፡ አዕጹቂሃ ፡ ሠርፅ ፡ የወሠርዕፅኒ ፡ ቦወአዕፁቅኒ ፡ አበዊነ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ እሙን ቱ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ሥላሴ ።
And they made a lampstand * with branches [which extended] to the right and to the left. The words he said: The stand was of gold, but gold is faith, and the lamp which was superimposed is Christ. The branches which extended to the right and to the left represent the law and the prophets. Now it is said: "A shoot will come out of its branches; three shoots, plus three branches." The branches are our fathers the apostles, the men of the Trinity.
ወገብሩ ፡ ማዕከክ ፡ ዘብርት ፡ በዘ ፡ ይትኀፀቡ ፡ እደዊሆሙ ፡ ማዕከክሰ ፡ ዮርዳኖስ ፡ ው እቱ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ ሕንብርትክ ፡ ከመ ፡ ማእከክ ፡ ዘኢየዐርቅ ፡ እምቱስሕቱ
And they made a bronze basin to wash their hands in. The basin is the Jordan (Yordânos), just as Solomon says: "Your navel is like a

cup that does not lack drink." By drink is meant the Holy Spirit. And Méré'ébêl, which he says, is the name that is written on the tabernacle; it means: praised in the name and praised in the counsel. The man who does not want counsel is a fool. And God spoke * to Moses and said to him: At the beginning of the first month, you shall set up the tabernacle, for it is on the first day of the first month that Noah (Noh) set up the ark and finished its construction. He finished it on the first day of the first month and until the sixteenth he brought [things] into it. On the 17th, he closed the ark. When he released the raven, it left and did not return.

The angel gave me the explanation of this and said to me: The raven is Satan who did not return to his dwelling after having left it. And the first dove signifies the law, the prophets, and their tabernacles.
ወተዐጊሦ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ሰቡዐ ፣ መዋዕለ ፡ ፈነዋ ፡ ለርግብ ፣ ዘይቤሂ ፡ ርግብሰ ፣ ቅድስት ፡ ቤተክርስቲያን ። ወአለ ፡ ተዐገሠ ፡ ሰቡዐ ፣ መዋዕለ"፣0 በጴመዋ”ዕል ፡እምሰኑየ ፡ ሕማማ ት ፡እስከ ፡ ትንሣኤሁ ። ወገብረ ፡ኖኅ ፡ መሥዋዕተ ፡ በመሓስዐ ፡ ጠሊ ፡ ወአስተስረየ፡ ኀጢአተ፣ኩዙሉ፣ ዓለም። ወታቦተ ፡ ኖኅኒ ፡ አኮ ፡ እምዕፀወ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ። አላ ፡ በቂለት ፡ እምነ ፡ ዕፅ ፡ ዘወገ
Then having waited for seven days, Noah released the dove. The dove in question is the holy Church. The seven days during which Noah waited are the seven days [during which Christ endured] sufferings, * from Monday until his resurrection. Noah offered a young ram as a sacrifice and implored the forgiveness of the sins of the whole world. Noah's ark was not made with wood of this world, but it came from a tree that an eagle had thrown. - And the altar of Melchizedek

That is why the apostles say: Where the altar of Melchizedek is, there is the head of the dragon. The dragon is Satan, and if one says there, it is because, at that same place, God cut the serpent. Just as Paul says: "He killed the enemy by his cross." The ram which was offered as a sacrifice in place of Isaac belonged to the * race of those that Israel offered and to the race of the one that Abel immolated. This ram is the image of Christ, and they are rams of paradise

And Moses set up the pavilion (?) surrounded by a golden veil. He also set up the table, with its furnishings, the candlestick, the lamp; he finished all his work. The glory of God filled the cloud; God sent his glory. The cloud which hovered above is the Holy Spirit; its furnishings, the Son, and the lamp, the Father who strengthens it; the incense, the prayer of the saints; the oil, the baptism, the law of the holy Church, the property of the Christians, which was made for them, * for Christ was the complement of everything and it is the whole work of the tabernacle (7)

And here is the explanation that the angel gave me of it. The twelve who bring for the dedication on one side and the other in the holy Church are the twelve apostles who bring everyone to their Lord. Instead of the silver plate, the new law has chaste men; instead * of the crater, a source of benefits, the remission [of sins] which is in the mouth of the priest; instead of the seventy shekels, the seventy disciples: instead of the ox, Christ; in place of the sheep, Mary; in place of the rams, the doctors; in place of the 5 calves, the 5 wise virgins; in place of the 5 sheep, the 5 loaves; in place of the two heifers, the two fish which satisfied 5,000 people; instead of the 5 goats, 5 foreign peoples who believed and were baptized. And again in place of the sheep, the women who put their goods at the service of Our Lord, and whose names are: Salomë, Magdaläwit (Magdalene), Sarâ, Yohanna (Joanna), wife of Chuza, and Mary who anointed Our Lord with perfumes; in place of Moses, Christ *; in place of Aaron, Peter, the leader; in place of Joshua (Iyâsu), the chaste John.
ወባሕቱ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ደብተራ ፡ ኢይትከሀል ፡ ይንግርዋ ፡ በበገጹ ። በከመ ፡ ይ ቤ ፡ ጳውሎስ ። ወለከሰ ፡ነገርኩከ ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ በአምጣነ ፡ ትክል ፡ አጽንዖ ፡ ወነዋ ፡ ነገረ፣
