as much as you can grasp.” Here now is a beautiful word (allegory). - Instead of the golden plate, [we have] Christ, and the plate which contained ten golden shekels represents the ten fingers of Our Lord which he stretched out on the cross; when it is said that it is full of incense, that means that Our Lord prayed on the cross and then erased the sins of the world. The hands that Moses raises also represent the hands of Christ and Amalek the demons *. Joshua (Iyâsus) who goes out to make war is interpreted by the Holy Spirit who went out to fight the wicked. Aaron and Hur who hold up the hands of Moses signify the two thieves who were crucified with Our Lord. That is the word which was written for the wise and which the angel said to the blessed Bahayla-Mikä'êl. And now we have finished the description of the tabernacle and everything concerning it, a description in which there is wisdom, intelligence, and science. After hearing it, the wise man increases his wisdom. | (the Gospel?) says: let him who has ears to hear, hear. The first speaks about the last and the last completes the first. A single word coming from a single discourse. And when it says that they made a single seal, that means that the Gospel is the seal * of all

the Scriptures. I have finished for you the explanation of the mystery of the tabernacle. And concerning the tabernacle, the prophet saw; Ezekiel (Hezeq'êl) said the vision concerning Jerusalem (Iyarusälêm). The people of Jerusalem were divided there according to their families, like the twelve tribes of Israel. These are the twelve apostles. And here are those who were there: 25,000 heads of righteous at one gate;

[at another] 10,000; in the third 20; in the fourth 85,000; in the fifth 25,000; in the sixth 10,000; in the seventh 10,000; in the eighth 25,000; in the ninth 4,500; in the tenth 40,500 * and a tenth 4005 (?). Those who had remained in the dark enclosure [were in the number of] 400, and 500 to the west, and on the sea side, and towards the sunset 10,000, and they did not pass to the east side and did not remain near this dark enclosure. Towards the south [there were] 18,000. At all the entrances, it tells you that there is a tehe, and at the place where this tehe is, [there are also] 30 on one side and 30 on the other. And where it says that there is a tehe, 6 on one side and 6 on the other; 30 on one side and 30 on the other, these are the 60 births of the patriarchs. And when it says tehe, it means design. From one tehe to another means from one portrait to another. When it says six on one side and six on the other, these are the twelve apostles.

There are three groups that remain near the enclosure, these are the avengers who possess justice * and in the first as well as in all the entrances those whose heads were mentioned, that is to say the righteous and their angels and again their number (?). From one tehe to another, there are those who approach, others who move away from each other according to their grades in holiness and those who are equal [are recognized by] the equality of their seats.

are complete in the number of seven. - One gate is further away than the other and for an opening, the space of a righteous man is greater than double this world and than the gate through which the light shines *. And one shines more than the other; there are some whose gates are of gold, others of silver, others of pearls, others of crystal. Three gates are of gold; three are of silver, one is higher than the other, as Isaiah says "The lintel of the gate was raised by the voice of those who cried out." In all these openings, the gates are different, and the souls of the righteous enter and leave through the windows of Jerusalem. There are the breasts from which flow into Jerusalem the honey, the milk, the wine, and the oil which descend into the garden and water the whole earth.

Then he (Ezekiel) tells you At each of its edges, there was a tohi and an Elam (vestibule), and six rooms that looked towards the east * and towards the west, two towards the north and towards the south. The vestibule was five cubits with the epemede of the Elam and three gates. The Elam had 20 cubits and the width of the elam, its steps and its top, each six; the gate of the epemedes, five and its length one (?) cubit, and the epemedes six on each side (?); inside six, on the sides four and thirty-three equal floors, and a separation. And in front of the apolisu [was] like a sea of 70 cubits [long (?)] and 5 cubits wide. The Apolisu has a hundred windows and its openings with 2 and 3 cherubim towards a single sabart which

ብ፡ኀበ።፣አሐቲ፣ጸበርት ፡እንት ፡አጸበርት ። ወዕፀ ፡ መሥዋዕኒ ፣ ዘያበአመት ፣ ኑኑ ፣፡ወ ማእዳተ ፡ ውቅሮ ፡ ዘእብን ፣ ኑነ ፡ በአመት ፣፡ ወመንፈቅ ፣፡ ግድሙ ፡ ወቆሙ ፡ በእመት።
is a palm tree. The wood of the altar was three cubits high and the tables of cut stone one and a half cubits long and one cubit wide and high.
ወዝውእቱ ፡ ፍካሬሁ ፤ ኤላመ ፡ ”መራብዕ' ፡ ብሂል ፤ ወ፪ኤክስድሬ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡፪መሶ በ፣መሥዋዕ ፡፣ወበመንገላ ፡፪ክሩብ ፤ ወኤላምኒ ፡ ዐምድ ፡ ብሂል ፤ ወኤጴሜድ ፡ አጎበር፣ ብሂል ። ወአጳሊጹ ፡ ብሂል ፡ መቅደስ ፡ ብሂል ። ወኢያዝኃ፣ ቀመር፣ብሂል። ወትርጓሜ ፡ መንፈሳዊ ፡ ፪ኤክስድሬ ፡ ብሂል ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ብሂል ፤ ወ፪ዘይቤሂ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፣ ጠባይፁዑ ፡ለሰብእ ። ወኤክስድሩ ፡ለባሴ ፡ ሰብእ፣ዘውእቱ ፡የማነ፡፣አብ ፣ብሂል ፣ወኤጴሜ ድ፣ይተረጐም ፡ኅኀበ ፡ ዮርዳኖስ ፤ ወያኀዋኅዊሁ ፡ በሥላሴ ፤ ወኤላም ፡ በወንጌል ፡ ይተረ ጐም ፤ ወአ፲ወ፪ዘይቤሂ ፡ በ፲ወ፪ሐዋርያት ፡ ይተረጐም ። ወተፍጻሜቱ ፡ ፄኔዘይቤ ፡ ሂ ፡ ቃል ፡ዘይጹውፁ ፡ ቅዱሳን ። ወበ5ቃል ፡ ዘይትወቀሱ ፡ ርጐማን ፤ ወእ"ለ ፡ ይቀውሙ፣፤መ ንገለ ፡ የማን ፡ክርስቲያን ፡ ወእሉ ፡ርቱዓን ፡ በሃይማኖት ። ወእሉ፣ይብሉ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ወ ኢኮኑ ፡ርቱዓን ፡ሌሙሰ፣ቁመቶሙ፡መንገለ፣ፀጋም ፤ወለረሲዓንሰ ፣ኢያስተዋቅስዎሙ፣ እስመ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ፍዳ ፡ በዘ ፡ ይትፋረዳ- ፤ ወይመጽኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ፡ እንዘ፣ ከመ ፣፡ማ የ፣፡ይውሕዙ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዳዊት ፤ አሳት ፡ የሐውር ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ወነድ ፡ የዐግቶሙ ፡ ለጸላእቱ ። ወእግዚእነሂ ፡ ይቤ ፡ በወንጌል ፡ ይትጋብኡ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ።
And here is the explanation of this passage: the elam * means divided into four parts; and the two eksedere are the two baskets of the sacrifice where the two cherubim are. Elam also means column and epemed means platform. Apolisu means sanctuary and iyazeha, chamber. And here is the symbolic explanation: The two eksedere signify men, and by saying two, it indicates the two natures of man. The eksedere which envelops man (7) signifies the right of the Father; and the epemed is interpreted by the Jordan (Yordanes); the three gates by the Trinity; the elam by the Gospel. When it says twelve, these are the twelve apostles. If it says that it was finished in 6 days (?) the 6 of which it speaks are the 6 words that the saints invoke and also because the damned plead by 6 words. - * Those who stand towards the right are the Christians, who are orthodox. As for those who call themselves Christians, but who are not orthodox, their place is on the left. But the impious are not summoned to appear, for there is no ransom by which they can be redeemed. They come into Sheol, flowing like water, just as David says: "Fire goes before him; the flame surrounds his enemies." Our Lord also says in the Gospel: "Let the peoples gather before him."
ወፅዝራኒ ፡ ይቤ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዕለተ ፣ ደይን ። እምለፌኒ ፣ ይመጽእ ፡ ትፍሥሕት ፣ ወ

joy, on the other judgment and punishment." It has five names: Sheol, darkness, punishment, that which is an evil smoke *, which is Gehenna; remains, fifth, judgment; Gehenna does not include (devour) it. Judgment is the punishment of Muslims and Jews; demons are swallowed up in Gehenna. Let us resume our story. When it says 70 for the epemedes, it indicates the 70 disciples; the four sides are the four evangelists who are equal in their words (?); when it says that the apolisu has 70 cubits, it speaks of this world. That is why Ezekiel said: "That day, after the captivity, I saw a vision; he led me to a high mountain; there was a city built there. I entered it and I met a man: his face was of bronze heated in the fire; he had in one hand a measuring rope * and in the other a measuring rod. This rod stood straight in the entrance whose height was six cubits and the width equal. || measured exactly the six steps with the rod, then the tehe, then he measured a second tehe, then three tehe in relation to the first, then the elaw and the 'aeli, crowning of the gates (7). He inspected everything as much as he could do.
ወብእሲ ፡ ዘርእየ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወኅለትሂ ፣ ዘርእየ ፣ ቃለ ፣፡ ወንጌል ፣ ወቆሙ ፣ በአመት ፡ ዘይቤሂ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ቦቶን ፣ ይከውን ፡ ቅስት ፡= ወሂጻመዐርጊሁ ፡፣ ዘይቤሂ ፡5 መዓርገ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወይቀውም ፡ ውስተ ፡ አንቀጽ ፡ ዘይቤሂ ፡ አንቀጽሰ ፡ ጥ
The man he saw is the Son; the rod he glimpsed is the word of the Gospel, when it says that its height was 6 cubits, it indicates the 6 words, by which one presents one's defense; the seven steps are the seven grades of the Church; when it says that he stood at the gate, the gate is baptism;


[p. 49] {#po.1.p49}
ምቀት፤ውእቱ ፡ ቆሙ ፡ "ወግድሙ ፡ ዕሩይ ፡ ዘይቤሂ፣ዙሎን ፡ ቃል፡ዘነበበ ፡ እግዚአብሔ ር ፤ አአም8አልቦ ፡ ሕጸተ ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፣ ወጠንቀቀ ፣ ወቴሔ ፡ ዘይቤሂ ፡ በወንጌል ፡ተ ፈጸመ ፡ ዙሉ። ወቴሔ፣ዝዘይቤሂ ፣ህየ፣ተሥዕሉ ፡ኅሩያን ። ወኩሉ ፡ ዘኢተሥዕለ፣ይወድይዎ፣ውስተ፣ እሳት ፡ እንተ ፡ ገሃነም ። ወኩሉ ፣ ዘኢተዐትበ ፣ ፍጽሙ ፡ አልቦቱ ፡ ተስፋ ፡ ለድቲን ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ዘዕቱብ ፡ፍጽሙ ፡ወልድ ፡መሐለ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ከመ ፣፡ ኢይትኅተም፤'። እስመ፣ይቤ፣፤ አብ ፡ ለወልድ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ተኀትሙ ፡ በደመ ፡ ገቦከ! ፡ ኢይትሐጐሉ ፣ ወኢይ ትሐሙ ፡ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ። ወሞትሂ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ ጸብ ኦ ፤ለውእቱ ፡ መልአከ ፡ ሞት ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ሰይጣን ። ውስተ ፡ ክር”ስቲያን ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ዐቢ ይ፤ምስጢር ፡ኅድግሰ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ግብር ፡ እለኒ ፡ መጽወቱ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ማርያም ፤ ኢይትሐጐሉ ።፡
when it says that its height * and its width were equal, these are all the words that God has spoken and of which one is not less than the other in anything whatsoever; he measured the tehe carefully, he said, that means that everything has been accomplished by the Gospel. The tehe of which it speaks is the place where the elect are represented; and all those who are not represented, they are thrown into the fire of Gehenna; for the one who has not been marked with a sign on the forehead, there is no salvation; as for the one who is marked with a sign on the forehead, Christ has promised him that he will be sealed. For the Father said to the Son: "All those who will be sealed with the blood of your ribs (?) will not perish and will not suffer in Sheol." Death itself, which is Satan, just as Paul says: "He fought this angel of death, which is Satan" among Christians, * is a great mystery, more than anything, those who will have given a small alms for the love of Mary will not perish.
ወበእንተኒ ፡ ሥርዐቶሙ ፡ ለእስራኤል ፡ ስማዕ ፡ እንግርከ ። ወሶበ ፣ ይብል ፡ አሮን ፡ ንሴብሖ ፣ ይትሜጠዋ ፡ ቃሎ ፡ አፅዋፈ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወይኔቅዋ ፡ በመንክር ፡ ጾታ ፡ ወጣዕመ፣ ቃሎን ፡ይመይጥ ፡ ልበ ፡ ወየዐርግ ፡ሕዝብ ፡ ለአጽምኦቶን ፡ ወአዕዋፈ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ እለ ፣ ይትዐየና ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ የአክላ ፡ ፲፻አርጋብ ፡ ወአዕዋፈ ፡ ባቢል ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ አጎርጋናት፣፣ ይ፻ወአፅዋፈ ፡ መንጤጤ" ፡ ፪፻ ።
Regarding the ordinances of Israel, listen to what I am going to tell you. When Aaron says: 'We praise Him,' the birds of Judah (Yehudä) answer him, crying out in an admirable order. The sweetness of their voice turns the heart, and the people go up to hear them. The birds of Judah that are in his camp number 1,000 doves, 300 birds of Babil, which are the agorgânât, and 200 turtledoves.


[p. 50] {#po.1.p50}
ወሶበ ፡ ይብል ፡ አጽምእ ፡ ሰማይ ፣ ወእንግርከ ፡ ይትሜጠዋ ፡ አፅዋፍ ፡ እለ ፡ ሮቤል ወኖልቆን ፡ ፲፻ ወ፻፻ አዕዋፈ ፣ ባቢል ፣ ወአዕዋፈ ፡ ዮሴፍ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ኖልቆ”ን ።
When he says: 'Give ear, O heaven, for I will speak,' it is the birds of Reuben (Robël) that answer him; their number is 1,000 and 300 birds of Babil, and the birds of Joseph (Yosêf) are just as numerous.
ከማሁ ፡ኣካዕበ ፡ ሶበ ፡ይብል ፡ እስመ ፡እሳት ፡ትነድድ ፡፤እመዓትየ ፡ እለ ፡ ዮሴፍ ፡ አዕዋ ፍ ፡ ይትሜጠዋ ፡ ቃሎ።
Then * when he says: 'For the fire of my wrath is kindled,' the birds of Joseph answer him.

When Aaron says: 'May God bless you!', the birds of Dan, Naphtali, and Asher answer him. An admirable work has been done in the tabernacle of Israel. On other days, not all the birds cry out at once, but only ten in turn; on feast days, they all cry out together. Their food is provided by a tribe; they each receive ten measures. The measure is the Hamor, according to the measure of the Ethiopians, and among the Abyssinians its measure is 2 entalam. And also one ephah (filled) a single time, half at dawn and half at twilight. The Komon tree is planted in the tabernacle; every morning the priest comes * and says to it: 'We water you with water,' and it grows in hope; if one did not speak to it thus, it would not be as beautiful. - When a lamb is sacrificed for sins, it is cooked with this tree; for other sacrifices, other trees are used. This is what the angel of God told me, and he added: So that you do not forget the word of God, so that you understand, so that your heart opens to the

word of God, so that you grasp it, repeat these names and say: Adâm, Zarudä'êl, Bazanadagä'êl, Nâtnäw, Daflà, 'Awlâlä'êl, Kabikä, Tâtä, 'Elâdä'êl. After having pronounced this, let him read the psalter; whoever brings him a little water, let him drink and absorb it without breathing. This is what * I have told and explained to you. Let me now set forth for you a surprising mystery concerning the tabernacle. The sacrifice was offered in the seventh enclosure, and, as I told you, where the samâsaye (?) is, there is the sacrifice of fire. As for the altar and the enclosure, such is their description and their ordinance. The seventh tabernacle, which is Karâ, has nine columns and eighteen beams; it contains a terrifying altar furnace and its length [is 1,000]; above it a bronze bed is spread, of the same length, pierced with eighteen holes. Below, a bronze plate is spread, its length 1,000 [cubits]. There are three pots, one of gold, the second of silver, the third of bronze; it is in this tabernacle of testimony that their sacrifices are accomplished.
ወፍካሬሁኒ ፡ ነገሩኒ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ወአሮን ፣ ወይቤሉኒ ፡ ስማዕ ። ፀጸድ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይብለከ ፤ ቅድስት ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲ'"ያን ፡ ይእቲ ፤ በከመ ፣ ዮሐንስ ፡ አቡ ፡ ቀለምሲስ ፡ ርኢኩ ፤ አብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት ፤ ወ፪፳አፅማድ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይብለከ ፡ ዘሕዝብ ፣ ተፄወዉ ፣ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለስልምናሶር ። ፲ወ፳አሕባል ፡ ሶበ ፣ ይብል ፣ ቀዳሚት ፡ ሐብል ፡ ከመ ፣፡ ኢታምልክ ፡ በዕዳ ፡ ወከልእ
And the explanation of it was given to me by Moses and Aaron. They said to me: Listen well. When one speaks to you of the enclosure, it is the holy Church *, as [says] John abu-galamsis (Apocalypse): 'I saw churches.' The nine columns in question are the nine peoples who were taken into captivity in the time of Shalmaneser. The eighteen beams signify: the first, you shall not worship another, the

second, love, the third, humility, the fourth, joy, the fifth, gentleness, the sixth, patience, the seventh, peace, the eighth, faith, the ninth, charity, the tenth, the care to avoid words (?), the eleventh, fasting, the twelfth, you shall not steal, the thirteenth, the absence of pride, the fourteenth, chastity, the fifteenth, abstention from false swearing, the sixteenth, what you hate for yourself, you shall not do to your neighbor, the seventeenth, you shall honor your father and your mother, and the eighteenth, you shall not desire all that * you see. He who observes these prescriptions shall inherit the kingdom of heaven.

There is a terrifying altar furnace, he says; the furnace is Adam, and the fire is the God Nar who dwelt near his daughter. - When he says that the height was 1,000 cubits, these are the 1,000 generations. - And above is spread a bronze bed; the bed is the cross, the bed of suffering of Our Savior, as [it is said]: 'He helped me on my bed of suffering.' He says again: 'He put a bronze bow on my arm.'
ወ፲ወ፪ስቀሩረታቲሁ ፡ ሶበ ፣ ይብል ፡ ፲ወ፪ኀዋኅወ ፡፣ ሰማይ ፣ በከመ ፡ ይቤሉ ፡ነቢያት፣ ሀአንቀጸ ፣ ሰማይ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ጽዮን ፣ ትፈሪ ፡ ፲፳ወደብረ ፡ ሲና ፡ ትፈሪ ፡ ፲፳ወደብረ ፡ታ ቦር ፡ ትፈሪ ፡ 8፳ ፤ ወዘመትሕቴሁ ፡ ጻሕል ፡ ሀየንተ ፡ ኪዳን ፡ አው ፡ ከርሦሙ ፡ ለመ ሃይምናን ።
The twelve holes of which he speaks are the twelve gates of heaven, as the prophets say. The four gates of heaven, namely Dabra-Seyon which bears fruit at the 100,000th, Dabra-Sina at the 100,000th, and Dabra-Tabor at the 400,000th. - The plate which is below is in place of the covenant * or the womb of the believers.
ወ፻መቅጹታት ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ዘወርቅ ፡ ዘሐዋርያት ። ወካልእ ፡ዘብሩር ፡ ነቢያት ። ወሣልስኒ ፡ዘብርት ፤ ክርስቲያን ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ይትፌጸም ፡ ደብተራ ፡ስምዕ ፡ ኩሎ ፣መ
Of the three pots of which he speaks, the one which is of gold represents the apostles, the second of silver, the prophets, and the third of bronze, the Christians.


[p. 53] {#po.1.p53}
ሥዋዕቲሆሙ ፡ ዕበ ፣ ይብለከ ፡ በመዓርገ ፣ ክህናት ፡ ይፌጽም" ፡ ሚመታ ፡ ለቤተ ፣ ክርስ ቲያን ። ወአኮ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ዘይገብሩ ፡ መሥዋዕተ ። ወሐዋርያሃኗ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ዐጸድ ፡ ታዴ ዎስ ፡ ውእቱ ፤ እሁ ፡ ለያዕቆብ ። ወመልአክኒ ፡ ሰላትያል ፡ ዐቃቤ ፡ ምሥዋዕ ።
That is why when he tells you that all their sacrifices were accomplished in this tabernacle of testimony, he indicates that the functions of the Church are accomplished by the priests of all grades; but they do not all offer the sacrifice. - The apostle of this enclosure is Thaddeus (Tädëwos), brother of James (Yä'eqob)
ወሳምን ፡ ዐጸድ ፡ እንተ ፡ ፳አፅማዲሃ ፡ ወ፳አሕባሊሃ ፡ ወቿሶበ ፣ ይብል ፡ ሂሚመተ" ፡ ክሀነት ፣ ወሳምን ፡ ሕዝበ ፣ ክርስቲያን ። ወ፳አሕባሊሃ ፡ ሶበ ፣ ይብለከ ፣ ሂመጻሕፍተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፣ ወሳምንቶንሂ ፡ ለሊሃ ። "ከመዝ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሥርዐቶሙ ፡ ለሌዋውያን ፣ እስመ ፡ ይነሥእ ፡ክህን ፡ መሙጐሥሰ።ስ ያወፅእ ፡ ሥጋ ፣ ወይወግሮ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዐራት ፤ ዘብርት ። ወያንጸፈጽፍ ፡ ሥብሕ ፡እን ተ ፡ ስጐረት ፡ ወይወግሮ ፤ ታሕተ ፤ ወይመልእ ፡ የመቅጹታተ ፡ ወያመጽእ ፡ ሾሮአርጋ በ ፡ በበ ፡ ሰንበት ፣፡ ለለሰሙን ፣፡ ወያጹንዋ ፣ ማዐዛሁ' ፡ ለምሥዋዕ ፡፡ ቀዳሚት ፡ርግብ ፡ ቀይሕ ፡=፡ ወክልእት ፣ ኩሣኩስ ። ወሣልስ ፡ ጸዐዳ ።፡ ወይትመዐዕ ፡ ካህን ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ይእቲ፣ ርግብ ፡ ጸዐዳ ፡ ወይዘብጣ ፡ ወይቀትላ ፣ ወይብላ ፡ አማሰነት ፣ መሥዋፅየ ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ ፈት ለ ፡ ደብተራየ ፡ ነሰተት።
and the angel is Salâtyâl, the guardian of the altar. The eighth enclosure has eight columns and eight rafters. - When he says eight [columns], he means the seven orders of the priesthood, the eighth is the Christian people; the eight rafters are the seven writings of the Church and the eighth the Church itself. * Now such is the order of the Levites: the high priest pulls the meat from the cauldron with a fork and throws it onto the bronze bed. The fat flows through the holes; he makes it fall below and fills three pots with it; then he brings three doves on the sabbaths of each week and they smell the perfume of the altar. The first is red, the second of several colors, and the third white. The priest gets angry at this white dove, strikes it, and kills it, saying to it: 'It has corrupted my altar and destroyed all the sons of my tabernacle.'
ወፍካሬሁኒ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ወአሮን ፡ ወይቤሉኒ ፡ ሌዋውያንሰ ፡ካህናት ፡ ወካህንኒ ፡ ወልደ “እግዚአብሔር ፣ ወመጐሥስኒ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ዘየኃሥስ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ለአሚን ። ወጽሕር ትኒ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወሥጋኒ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ ውእቱ ፡አለ ፡ ይጹውዑ ፡ ኀቤሁ ። ወሥብሕ
The explanation of this passage [was given to me] by Moses and Aaron: they said to me: The Levites are the priests, and the priest is the son * of God; the fork is the voice of the Gospel which seeks man for faith; the cauldron is the

ኒ ፤ ዘወረደ ፤ ታሕተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ዘተሰብከ ፡ በታሕተ ፡ ሰማይ ። ወ፻አርጋብ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይ ብል ፡፻ጾታ ፡ ዘነቢያት ። ወእምአዳም ፡ እስከ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ወእሙሴ ፡ እስከ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ወ እም]እግዚእ ፡ እስከ ፤ ኅልቀተ ፡ ዓለም ። ወቀዳሚት ፡ ርግብ ፤ ቀይሕ ፡ ሶበ ፣ ይብል ፣ መሥዋፅቶሙ ፡፣ ለእስራኤል ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፤ ዮሐንስ ፡ በራእዩ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ ፈረስ ፡ ሐመልሚል ፦ወዘይጹዐኖ ፡ ሞት ፡ ስሙ፣ወ ሲኦል ፡ ትተልቓዎ ፡ ፈረስኬ ፡ ሐመልሚል ፡ ዘኢበጽሐ ፡ ለፀጸድ ፡ ኦሪት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ "ወካ ልእት ፤ ርግብ ፣ ኩሣኩስ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ወደቂቀ ፡ነቢያት ፡ አለ ፡ ኢረከቡ ፡ ጥምቀ ተ ፡ ሕይወት ፣ ፍጹመ ፡ ወበእንተዝ ፣ ኢጻፅደወ ፣ ፍጹመ ። ወሣልስት ፡ ርግብ ፡ ጸዓዳ ፤ ወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘንጹሕ ፣ እምኅጢአት ። ወእለ ፣ ተምዕዑኒ ፡ ካሀናት ፡ ሰቃልያ ን ፡ ቤተ ፣፡ ሌዊ ፣፡ ወይቤላ ፡ አማሰነት ፡ መሥዋያየ ፡ ሶበ ፣ ይብል ፣ ይብሉ ፣ አይሁድ ፡ነ ሰተ ፡ሕገነ ፣ ወትምህርተነ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ፈትሉ ፡ ፈትለ ፡ ደብተራየ ፡ ነሰተት ፣ ዕበ ፣፡ ይብ ል ፤ አማን ፤ ተነሥተ ፣፤ ኦሪቶሙ ፣ ወትንቢቶሙ ፣ ወክህነቶሙ ፣ ወመንግሥቶሙ ፡ በከ መ ፡ይቤ ፡ ላእክ ፡ ፈለሰት ፣ ኦሪቶመ ፡ ወትንቢቶሙ ፣ ወክህ5ቶሙ።
world; the flesh represents the peoples who cry out to him; the fat which flows below is the faith which is preached under heaven; the three doves of which he speaks are the three series of prophets from Adam to Moses, from Moses to the Lord, and from Our Lord to the end of the world. When he says that the first dove is red, these are the sacrifices of Israel. As John says in his vision: 'There went out a pale horse; he who sat on it was called Death, and Sheol followed him.' Therefore the pale horse which did not reach the enclosure is the Mosaic law *. The second dove, which is multicolored, represents the prophets and the children of the prophets who did not receive the baptism of life completely; that is why it is not completely white. The third dove, which is white, is the Son of God who is pure of all sin. The priests who get angry at it are the people who crucify, the house of Levi. - When he says that they say to it (to the dove): 'It has corrupted my altar,' these are the Jews who say: 'He has destroyed our law and our doctrine.' - And when they add: It has destroyed all the sons of my tabernacle, it is that in truth their law has been destroyed, as well as their prophecies, their priesthood, and their kingdom, as the minister says: 'Their law has passed, as well as their prophecies
ወተንሚኦ ፡ አምውታን ፡ ቃለ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ዘለዓለም ፡ "ሐደሰ ፡ ለነ ፡ ሥርዐተ ፣ ወን ሕነኒ ፡ አማንያን ፡ ርኢነ ፡መንክረ ። ወክህናትሂ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ሚመቶሙ ፣ ይሬእዩ ፣ ምስጢ ረ ፤ ኅቡአ ፡ ቦአለ ፡ ይሬእዩ ፣ በአምሳለ ፡ ነደ ፡ አሳት ፣፡ ወቦ ፡ በአምሳለ ፣ ሕፃን ፡ ወቦ ፣ በ
and their priesthood.' Having risen from the dead, the word of eternal life has renewed * the law for us. We who are believers have seen a wonder, and the priests, on the day of their ordination, see the hidden mystery; there are some who

አምሳለ ፡ ኅብስት ፣ ዘስንዳሴ ፡ ወመብዝኅቶሙ ፡ ወይሬእይዎ' ፡ በአምሳለ ፣ ኅብስት ፡ ወ ዝኒ ፣ መንክር ፡ መስጢር ። ወበእንተሂ ፡ አርጋብ ፡ ፪ጾታ ፡ዘነቢያት ፡ ወየዐርጋ ፤ እምሲሷኦ ል ፡ ወያዐርፋ ፤ ውስተ ፡ ደብተራ ፣ ወዝኒ ፡ በሰንበታት ። ወከማሁ ፡ እምክርስቲያንሂ ፡እ ለ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ኃጥኣን ፡ እለ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ አስካለ ፣ ኢይትገደሩፉ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ አስካለ ፡ እ ለ ፣ ነሥኡ ፣ ሃይ”ማኖተ ፡ እለ ፣ ነሥኡ ፣ ብሂል ፣ ማኅተመ ፡ ጥምቀት ፣ ኢይትሐ ጐሉ። ወበእንተሂ ፡ ሳምንት ፡ ዐጸድ ፡ ይብለከ ፡ ሄኖክ ፡ ሰንበት ፣ እንተ ፣ጽድቅ ። ይእቲኬ፣ ሰንበታት ፡ ትስብኦቱ ፡ ለወልድ ፡ ወባቲ ፣ ይወርድ ፡ ብእሴ! ፣ ዘይቤ ፡ ወልድ ። ወዝኒ ፤ ወባቲ ፡ ይተክል ፡ ደብር ፡ ርቱዕ ፡ ዘይቤሂ ፣ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ እንተ ፡ ባቲ ሃይማኖት ፤ ርትዕት ። ወሐዋርያነኒ ፡ ለዛቲ ፣ ናትናኤል ፤ ወልድ ፣ ቀለዮጳ ። ወመልአክካኒ ፣ ሱራፌ ል ። እስመ ፡ በሳምንት ፡ ዕለት ፣ በውስተ ፣ ሳምንት ፡ ዐጸድ ፡ መሐሮሙ ፡ ለሱራፌል ። ወዝውእቱ ፡ ነገራ ፣ ለታስዕት ፣ ወባቲ ፣ ፳8አዕማድ ፣ ወ፪ጳአሕባል ። ወዝየ ፡ ይቀውም ፡ሞአብ ፡ካህን ። ወህየ ፡ ትኩል ፡ ዕፀ ፡ "ከሞን' ፡ ወየአክል ፡ ፍሬሁ፤ መጠነ ፡ ዐተር ፤ ወኢይበቀዕዕ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ለጸብሕ ። ወሀየ ፡ ሀሎ ፣ ሮሞን ፣፡ ወእምኔሁ ፤ ይወፅእ ፡ ማይ ፡ ዘይመስል ፡ ደመ ፡ ወዕበ ፡ ሰትዩ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ እለ ፡ አኅዞሙ ፡ጋኔን ይ
see it in the form of a flame of fire, others in the appearance of a child, others in the aspect of a bread of pure flour. Most see it in the aspect of bread, and this is an admirable mystery. And regarding the doves, they are two series of prophets; they rise from Sheol and land on the tabernacle. This takes place on the sabbath days. - Likewise among the Christians, those who are sinners, but who have a little grape, will not be rejected. Those who have a little grape are those who have received the * faith, that is to say those who have received the seal of baptism; they will not perish. And regarding the eighth enclosure, Enoch tells you the sabbath which is justice. These are the sabbaths of the Incarnation of the Son; when he says: In this enclosure descends a man, it is the Son, and when he says that he places a straight mountain there, it is the Church, in which is the straight (orthodox) faith. - And the apostle of this enclosure is Nathanael, son of Clopas (Qalayopä), and the angel is a seraph (Surâfêl), because on the eighth day, in the eighth enclosure, he had pity on the seraphs. Here is the description of the ninth enclosure: it contains 5 columns and 6 rafters. There stands Moab, the high priest; there is planted the tree * Kamon; its fruit is the size of a bean and it can only serve as a condiment. There is also seen the pomegranate tree, from which comes a juice that resembles blood. When those who were possessed by the demon drank this juice, they were healed


[p. 56] {#po.1.p56}
ትፈወሱ ፡ እምደዌ ፣ ኀጢአቶሙ ። ወኀልቀ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ማይ ፡ ወኢተረክበ ፡ እምአመ ፡ ሙሴ። ወፍካሬሁኒ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ወአሮን ፡ ወይቤሉኒ ፡ ታስዕት ፡ ዐጸድ ፡ሶበ ፣ ይብለከ ፣ ቅድስት፣ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ወባቲ ፡ ጾታ ፡ መዓርገ ፡ ካህናት ፡ ወዕድ ፡ወአንስት ፡እለ፣ ተአመኑ ፡ ወ፳አዕማድ ፡ ሶበ ፣ ይብል ፣ ጴጥሮ!ስ ፡ ወዮሐንስ ፡ ወያዕቆብ ፡ወያዕቆብ፣እ ጉሁ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ። ወፄአሕባል ፡ 8ቃለ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ እለ ፡ ቦን ፡ ይትወቀሱ ፡ ፍጥ(፡፡ ። “ወሞአብኒ ፡ ዕበ ፡ ይብለከ ፡ እግ.እ ፡ አበ ፡ ዙሉ ፤ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ዳዊት ፣ ወሞአብ ፡ ካህን ፡ ተስፋየ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ሞአባዊያን ፡ ይተረጐሙ ፡ በአጋንንት ፡ ወአኮ ፡ ዘናስተማስሎ ሙ ፡ በእግዚአ ፡ ዳእሙ ፡ ይሰደዱ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ተሰየመ' ፡ ስሞ ፡ ለእግዚ|እ]ነ ። ወሞአብኒ ፡እ ሳት ፡ ብሂል ። ወሕብረ ፡ ቀይሕ ፡አምሳለ፣ ፍጥረቶሙ ፡ ለመላእክት ።፡ ወከመሰ ፡ ሞዴአባዊያ ን ፡ አጋንንት ፡ ስምዓ ፡ ለኦሪት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትብል ፤ ይጠፍኡ ፡ መላእክት ፡ አለ ፡ ስሞሙ ፡ ሞአብ ፡ ወኢሳይያስኒ ፡ ይቤ ፡ በሌሊት ፡ ያጠፍእአዋ ፡ ለሞአብ ።፡ ወዝኒ ፡ ብሂል ፡ አመ፣ ይሰቀልዎቻ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ቀትረ ፤ መዓልት ፣ ሌሊተ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ወአሜሃ ፣ ጠፍአት ፡ ሲኦል ፡ ወተፀብጠ ፡ ዲያብሎ'ስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያፍ ፣ ወበሌሊት ፡፣ ያንኅልዋ ፣ ለጥቅመ ፣ ሞአብ። አማን ፡ ንኅለት ፣ ደብተራኒ ፣ ወጥቅማኗ ፡ ኦሪት ፡ ወበከመ ፡ ተብሀለ ፡ አእትት ፡ ጥቅ ሞ ፡ ወካዕበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ኢሳይያስ ፡ ሞአብ ፡ ተክዙ ፡ ለርአስክሙ ፡ ወአማን ፡ ተከዙ ፡፣ አ
of the sickness of their sin. This juice has been lost and has not been found since Moses. And the following explanation [was given to me] by Moses and Aaron; they said to me: When one says to you the ninth enclosure, it is the holy Church which comprises seven grades of the clergy, and men and women who have faith. - When he mentions 5 columns, these are Peter, John, James (Yä'eqob), and James, brother of Our Lord. The six rafters are the six words of the Gospel by which one contests (?) * ... When he speaks to you of Moab, it is the Lord, Father of all things, as David says: 'Moab, the priest, is my hope.' And again, the Moabites (Mo'abawiyän) are interpreted as the demons. It is not that we want to assimilate them to the Lord, on the contrary, they are chased away when one pronounces the name of Our Lord. Moab also means fire; and the red color is the emblem of the creation of the angels. And as proof that the Moabites are demons, listen to the Pentateuch which says: 'The angels whose name is Moab will be destroyed.' And Isaiah also says: 'In the night, Moab will be exterminated.' This means: When Our Lord is crucified, the noon will be night and then Sheol will disappear, the devil * will be caught like a bird and, in the night, they will overturn the wall of Moab. In truth the tabernacle has been overturned, as well as its wall which is the law, as it has been said: 'Move back its wall,' and again Isaiah cries out:


[p. 57] {#po.1.p57}
ጋንንት ፡ ወሰቃልያን ፡፡ ወካዕበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ኢሳይ![ያ]ስ ፡ ተሀጐለት ፡ ለዴባን ፡ ወዝኒ ፡ ብሂ ል ፡ተሀጐለት ፡ምኩራብ ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ርዴ ፡እንተ ፡ ወለደት ፡ ኦሪት ፡ ወነቢያት ፡፡ ወሀየ ፡ት ዙል ፡ ፅፀ ፡ ከሞን ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይብል ፡ ዕፀ ትንቢት ፣ ብሂል ። ወኢይበቀዕዕ ፡ እንበለ ፡ ጸብ ኅ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይብል ፡ ኦሪት ፡ ወነቢያት ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘበቀ፥ዕዎ ፡ ለአዳም ፡ ወኢክሀልዎ ፣ አድኅኖ ቶ፣፡ወጸብኅ።ሂ ፡ ተሰምዩ ፡እስመ ፡ ኢኮኑ ፡ፍጹማነ ፤ መብልዐ ፡ ወ”ክርስቶስሰ ፡ባሕቱ ፡ ኅብስተ ፡ ሕይወት ፣ ፍጹመ ፣ መብልፅ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ኢሳይያስ ፣ ወለከሜንሂ ፤ ይበልዕዎ ፡ በእክል ፡ ወአማን ፡ ተበልዐ ፡ ትንቢተ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ በእክለ ፣ ሕይወት ፡ ክርስ ቶስ።= ወህየ ፡ ትኩል ፡ ዕፀ ፡ ርሞን ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይብል ፡ ዕፀ ፡ አርያም ፡ ብሂል ፡ ወሶዕበ ፡ ሰትዩ፣ እምኔሁ ፡ እለ ፤ አኅዞሙ ፡ አጋንንት ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይብል ፡ ይትፌወሱ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ሰትይዎ ፣ በአ ሚን ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ በአሚን' ፡ለደሙ ።፡ ለክቡር ፡ ተፈወሱ ፡ እምኀጢአቶሙ ። ወጋኔንኒ፣ ዘአኀዞሙ ፡ ወዘ ፡ ቀነዮሙ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ውእቱ ፡፡
'Moab, grieve for yourself.' In truth the demons have been afflicted, as well as those who crucify. Isaiah says again: Ladëbän (Dibon? Is. xv, 2) has perished, this means that the synagogue, full of advantages that the law and the prophets created, has been destroyed. - When he says that there was planted the tree Kamon, he indicates the tree of prophecy; and when he says that it only serves as a condiment, this means the law and the prophets which were of no use to Adam and which could not save him. They are called by the name of condiments; because they are not complete foods *, for Christ alone is a bread of life, a perfect food. That is why Isaiah says: 'They eat cumin with wheat.' In truth, the prophecy of the prophets has been devoured by the wheat of life, which is Christ. When he says that there is planted the tree remon, he means the tree of the Aryâm (the heavens), and when he recounts that if those who are possessed by demons drink the juice of the fruit of this tree, they are healed of their sickness, this means that when the peoples have drunk with faith the blood of the glorious [Christ], they are healed of their sins. The demon that has seized and possessed them is Satan.
ወኅልቀ ፤ ውእቱ ፡ ወኢተረክበ ፡ እምአመ ፡ ሞተ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ሶበ ፣፡ ይብል ፡ ኀልቆሙ፣ ማየ ፡ሕይወት ፡ ለሰቃልያን ፡ወፈልፈለ ፡ "ሎሙ ፡ ለአማንያን ፡ ወበእንተዝ ፡ይቤ ፡ ነቢይ፣ ይነፅፍ ፤ ማየ ፡ ኔምሬም ፡ ዝኒ ፡ ማየ ፡ ምስጢር ፡ ብሂል ።፡፡ ወካዕበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ነቢይ ፡የዐር ጉ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ አሮሚን ፡፡ ወአማን ፡ ዐርጉ ፡ ጻድቃን ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ አርያም ። ወክዕበ ፡ይቤ፣
When he says that this tree has perished and is no longer found since the death of Moses, he indicates that the water of life has dried up for those who crucified [Christ; for the Jews] and that it has gushed forth * for the believers. That is why the prophet says: 'The water of Nimrim has dried up,' which means the water of the mystery. Then the prophet also says: 'They have climbed the road of Aromin.' In truth,


[p. 58] {#po.1.p58}
ኢሳይያስ ፡ ይክውን ፣ ድልቅልቅ ፡ ወአማን ፡ ይቤ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ አድለቅለቀት ፡ ምድር ፤ ወተንሥኡ ፡ ብዙኃን ፣ አብድንቲሆሙ ፡ ለጻድቃን ፡ ወቦኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገር ፣ ቅድስት ፤ ላዕለ ፣ ፍኖት ፡፡ ወሐዋርያሃኒ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ዐጸድ ፡ በርተሎሜዎስ ፡ ኅሣሜ ፣ ኅቡኣት ፣ ወመ ልአክኒ ፡ ኅብርያኖስ ፡ ኪሩብ ፡ ዐቢይ ። ወዝውእቱ ፡ ዘ፲ዐጸድ ፡ 8አፅማዲሁ ፤ ወብሩህ ። ኩልንታሁ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፤ ወእምኔሁ ፡፤ ይወፅእ ፣ ጢስ ፡ "ዘይመስል ፡ ነድ ፡ ወዝ ፤ ዘይከውን ፡ በበዓመት ፣ በበዓለ ፡ ሠዊት ፡ ወ በፄሄአሕባል ፣ ወሄመኃትው ። ወፍካሬሁኒ ፡ ነገሩኒ ፤ ሙሴ ፡ ወአሮን ፡ ወይቤሉኒ ፡ ወ፪ዐምድ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይብል ፡8አብ፣፤ ቅዱስ ፡ ወብርሃኑ ፣ ወልድ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ፈኑ ፣ ብርሃነከ ፡ ወጽድቀከ ።፡ ወካዕ በ ፣ ይቤ ፤ የማንከ ፣ ወመዝራዕትከ ፡ ወብርሃነ ፡ ገጽከ ፡፡ ወካዕበ ፣ ይቤ ፡ ይምጻእ ፡ ብርሃ ን ፡ እምጽርሕከ ።፡ ወጢስኒ ፡ አምሳለ ፡ ብርሃን ፣ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ነቢይ፤ ወመልአ ፡ ጢስ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ስብሐቲሁ ፡ ወዮሐንስ ፣ ወመልአ ፡ መቅደስ ፡ ስብሐተ ፡ወኅይ ለ ።= ወሕዝቅኤልኒ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ወመልአ ፡ መሥዋዕ ፣፤ ጢሰ ። ወጴጥሮስኒ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ተፈነወ ፡ ስብሐት ፡-ምስለ ፡ ጢስ =፡ ወ"ኤርምያስኒ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ተገልበ ፡ አዳም ፡ በተነ ፡ ጢስ ። ወክ ፅበ ፣ ይቤ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ አምደቡብ ፡ ይወፅእ ፣ ጢስ ፣ ወምንተ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ነገሥተ ፡ አሕዛብ ፤ ዘእንበለ ፣ ዘይጌምኑ ፤ ኪያሁ ። ፡
the righteous have climbed the path of the heavens. Isaiah says again: 'There will be an earthquake'; and truly it is reported in the Gospel that the earth shook and that several bodies of the righteous rose and entered the holy city at the top of the path. - The apostle of this enclosure is Bartholomew (Bartolomëwos), the seeker of secrets, and the angel is Heberyânos, the great cherub. Here is the description of the tenth enclosure. Its columns are four in number; it is extremely illuminated on all sides; there comes out of it a smoke * similar to a flame, and this takes place every year at the feast of the ears; it has seven rafters and seven torches. And the explanation of it was given to me by Moses and Aaron; they said to me: The four columns of which he speaks are the holy Father, and his light, the Son, as the prophet says: 'Send your light and your justice.' He says again: 'Your right hand, your arm, and the light of your face.' Then he says again: 'May the light come from your dwelling.' And the smoke similar to the light is the Holy Spirit. As the prophet says: 'The smoke filled the house [destined] for his praises.' And John [says]: 'The temple was full of [smoke of] praises and power.' Ezekiel says: 'The altar was filled with smoke.' Peter also says: 'The glory was sent with the smoke.' * And Jeremiah says: 'Adam was enveloped by a vapor of smoke.' The prophet says again: 'From the north will come a smoke. And what will the kings of the nations say,' if not that they have contaminated it?


[p. 59] {#po.1.p59}
ወበበዓለ ፡ሠዊት ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይብል ፡ ከማሁ ፡ በሐዳስኒ ፣ ሕግ ፣ በበዓለ ፣ ዛወረደ ፡ መንፈ ስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ፻ሰዓት ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ዕርገተ ፡ ዜጐርባን ። ያሐብል ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይብል ፡ ጀሰማያት ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤሉ ፡ አኃዝያነ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ነሣእነ ፡ ማየ ፡ ጽሩየ ፡ ወፈጠርነ ፡ ይሰማያተ ፡ ወባዕዳኝሂ ፡ ሰማያተ ፡ በነደ ፡ እሳት ።
When he speaks of the feast of the ear, he means that similarly in the new law, on the day of the feast of the 50 (Pentecost), the Holy Spirit descended at the third hour, at the moment of the offering of the sacrifice (or of the elevation). When he mentions three rafters, these are three heavens, as the masters of the world say: 'We have taken pure water and we have created three heavens; we have created others with the flame of fire.' When he says seven torches, these are the seven tabernacles of fire, the veils
ወማኅትወ ፡ ሶበ ፣ ይብል ፡ ኒደብተራ ፡ አሳት ፡ መንጦላፅቶሙ ፡ ለሥላሴ ፡ ወዛቲ ፤ ዐጸድ ፡ ወምቅዋሞሙ ፡ ለጻድቃን ፡ ወለደቂቀ ፡ ቃዓት ፡ ወቃዓት ፡ ወለደ ፡ ሌዊ ፣ወእን በራም ፡ ወለደ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ "ወአሮን ፣፡ ወማርያም ፣፡ ወሙሴ ፡ ወለደ ፡ ጌርሰም ፡ ወኤልየዛር ። ወአሮን ፡ ወለደ ፣ ናዳብ ፡ ወአብድዩ ፣ ወአልዓዛር ፣ ወለደ ፣ ፊናሐስ ። ወፊናሐስ ፣ ወለ ዶ ፡ አብሴ ፡ ወአብሴ ፡ ወለደ ፡ በቃ ፡ ወባቃ ፣ ታምርን ፡ ወታምርን ፡ ወለደ ፤፡ አኪ፣፡ሜ ሴክ ፡ ወአሉ ፡ ካህናተ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ ሕግ ፡ ወሥርዐት ፡ ወእሉ ፡ ወባዕዳንሂ ፣ ጽሑፋን ፤፡ ው ስተ ፡ መጽሐፍ ፡ ዘሕፁፃን ፡ ወሐዋርያሃሂኗ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ዐጸድ ፡ ፊልይስ ፡ ለባዊ ፣፡ ወሬዛ ፡ ብ
of the Trinity. This enclosure is the dwelling of the righteous and the children of Kohath (Qa'ât), and Kohath begat Levi, and Amram (Enbarâm) begat Moses * and Aaron and Mary, Moses begat Gershom and Eleazar ('Élyäzâr); Aaron begat Nadab and Abihu ('Abdyu); Eleazar ('Al'âzar) begat Phinehas (Finâhas); Phinehas begat Abishua ('Absë); Abishua begat Bukki (Bâgâ), and Bukki, Tamren; Tamren begat Ahimelech ('Aki-Mêlêk). These are the priests, servants (sons) of the law and the rites; they and others are mentioned in the book of Chronicles. - The apostle of this enclosure is Philip (Filpos), which means wise young man, and the angel is Sadâkyâl, the comforter of the afflicted.
ሂል ፡ ወመልአክኒ ፡ ሰዳኪያል ፡ ናዛዜ ፡ ሕዙናን ፡፡ ወዝንቱ፲ወ8ዐጸድ ፡ ወባቲ ፡ ፳አፅማድ ፡ ወጓወ፪አሕባል ። ወፍካሬሁኒ ፡ ነገሩኒ ፣ ሙሴ ፣ ወአሮን ፣ ወይቤሉኒ ፡ ፳አፅማድ ፡ ሶበ ፣ ይብል ፣ ፳እም ኔሆሙ ፡ ኦሪተ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ወ፻ኒ ፡ 8ዘኢያ”ሱ ፣ ወ፳ዘመሳፍንት ፣ ወ8ዘሩት ።
This is the eleventh enclosure; it has 8 columns and 49 rafters. And the explanation of it was given to me by Moses and Aaron; they said to me: When he mentions eight columns, five of them are the law of Moses, the other three are one [the book of] Joshua *, the other that of Judges, and the third that of Ruth.
ጓወ፪አሕባል ፡ ሶበ ፣ ይብል ፡ግዘመጻሕፍት ፡ነቢያት ፣ ወመልአ ፡ማጣወ፪ ፡ እሉ ፡ ፳ነቢያ
The 49 rafters of which he speaks are the 49 books of the prophets; (this number

of) 49 is completed by these eight prophets who knew the past and the future. One of them says: The king of the south (Azêb) will go to the king of the north; she will marry there; the two kings will tell each other falsehoods at a meal, and because of that there will be a great massacre between them both. And the explanation of this passage was given to me by Moses and Aaron in these terms: 'The king of the south is the Roman who dwells in the south and to the west of heaven.' And now he is his servant and they have perverted him for 6 years and 5 ...; there will come an impostor (bearer of a false name) and two years after he has come, [will come also] the king of the north bearing a false name, and this one will be descended from Japheth *. As Ezekiel says: A prince (?) will come out of Japheth

When he says: they will both converse about lies at the same table, this means that the king of the south says to him: After I have seized the king of Ethiopia, I will hand him over to you, and the other says to him: I will give you 7,000 horses. They have acted with perfidy one toward the other; that is why there will be a great massacre between them. - She will flee on a horse and return to her father. The king of the south will chase her after having made an alliance with the king of Ethiopia, for they will have in their hands the spear of the cross; when he sees this sign, the impostor will flee far from them, then he will accumulate the wrath of all the peoples. Then will come the lord with his army: he will make war on him * and will overthrow him from his throne. His troops will be exterminated and he himself


[p. 61] {#po.1.p61}
ን ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ሐዲስ ፡ ወምድር ፡ ሐዲስ ፡ ወአማን ። ወዛቲ ፡ ዐጸድ ፡ ምቅዋሞሙ ፡ ለደቂ ቀ ፣ይሰኮር። ወይሰዐር'፣ወልደ ፡ ቃዓት ። ወቃዓት ፡ ወልደ ፡ሌዊ ፡ ወይሰዐር ፣ ወልደ፣ ፋቁራ ፤ ወቆሬ ፤ ወቆሬ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ፋሬግ ፡ ወፋሬግ ፡ ታሙዝ ፣ ወታሙዝ ፡ ፌሌቶ፣፡ወእ ሉ ፡ ውእዩ ፡ በእሳት ፡ ወኢተርሩ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ ናሙኤል ። ወናሙኤል ፡ ወለደ ፡ ኤልፋሳሬ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ አቡሆሙ ፡ ለብዙኃን ፡=፡
will be thrown into the fire, as the apostles say. - Then there will be a new heaven and a new earth. - This enclosure is the dwelling of the children of Issachar (Yesakor); the son of Kohath will be overthrown (7), of Kohath son of Levi; the son of Phakurah (Fâqurä) will be deposed (7) as well as Korah (Qorê), son of Pharez (Farëg); and Pharez Tammuz, and Tammuz Phileto (Fêlêto). These were burned by fire and only the children of Namuel (Nämu'êl) remained. Namuel begat Elphasaph ('Élfäsäfi) who was the father of many children.
ወዝውእቱ ፡ ዘ፲ወ፳ዐጸድ ፡ ወቦ፪8አዕማድ ፡ ወ፪ወ፲አሕባል ። ወፍካሬሁኒ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ 8አዕ”ማዱ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይብል ፡ ሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወሀየንተ ፡ ፪እንስ ሳ ፡ 8መናብርት ፡ ወህየንተ ፡ 8አፍላግ ፡ 8ወሐይዝተ ፡ ወንጌል ። ወሀየንተ ፡ 8ነፋሰተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡፤ ፪ርባሕቱ፤' ፡ ማዕዝኒሃ ፡ ለምድር ፡ ወህየንተ ፡ 8ሰረገላ ፡ ኪሩቤል ፡ ፪ማዕዝኒሁ ፡ ለመስቀል ፡ ወሀየንተ ፡ 8መዳልወ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ 8ማዕዝኒሃ ፡ ለታቦት ፡ ወህየንተ ፡ 8አብሕር ተ ፡ እሳት ፡ 8ማእዝኒሃ ፡ ለቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወህየንተ ፡ 8አፅማዲሃ ፡ ለወርኅ ፡ 8አእባን ፤ ዘውስተ ፡ ልብሱ፣።ለአሮን ፡ ወህየንተ ፡ ፪ትዕይንት ፡ ዘሰማይ ፣ 8ጾታ ፡ ትእይንተ ፡ ምግዓዞ ሙ ፡ ለእስራኤል ፡ 8አቅርንት ፣ 8ሕማማተ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ወእገሪሁ › ለእግዚእነ ፣ ወሀየን ተ ፡ በአንቀጸ ፡ ሰማይ ፡፪ገጸ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወህየ'ንተ ፡ 8ዕፀወ ፡ ገነት ፡ 8ቀስፋሀ ፡ እለ ፡ ይ ትወሀቡ ፡በዕለተ ፡ ጥምቀት፣፡ሐሊበ ፡ ወመዓረ ፡ ወሥጋኒ ፡ ወደም። ወሀየንተ ፡ 8መሳክወ ፡ ማሀው ፡ ፻ቀናዲለ ፡ ዘቤተ ፣ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወህየንተ ፣ 8መላእክት ፡ እለ ፣ ይቀውሙ ፡ ፪ገ
And this is the twelfth enclosure, with 4 columns and 12 rafters. Here is the explanation: The four columns * of which he speaks are the patriarchs of the Church. In place of the four animals are the four seats; in place of the four rivers, the four rivers of the Gospel; in place of the four winds of heaven, the four corners of the earth; instead of the four chariots of the cherubim, the four (angles) arms of the cross; instead of the four scales (?) of the sun, the four corners of the altar; instead of the four seas of fire, the four angles of the Church; in place of the four pillars of the moon, the four stones which are on the garment of Aaron; instead of the four camps of heaven, the four series of the encampment of Israel at the moment of its migration; [instead] of the four horns, the four pains of the hands and feet of Our Lord; instead of the four gates of heaven, the four faces of heaven *; in place of the four trees of the garden, the four external signs (?) which are given on the day of baptism, milk, honey, flesh, and blood; instead of the four glass windows, the hundred torches of the Church; instead of the four angels who stand, on the four sides of the Lord of spirits, the four elements


[p. 62] {#po.1.p62}
በዋቲሁ ፡ ለእግዚአ ፣ መናፍስት ፡ ፪ጠባይዐ ፡ ፍጥረተ ፡ ለአም! ፡ ወእሉ ፡ ተፈጥሩ ፡ በበ፣ ጸወስብሐት ፡ ለኬንያ ፤ ለዘፈጠረ ፤ ዘንተ ፣ ወኩሉ ፣ ይቀውም ፣፤ በሥላሴ።
of the creation of the world and of those who were created from each [of these elements]. Glory to the artisan who created this and everything subsists by the Trinity.
ወ፲ወ፪አሕባል ፡ ዕበ ፡ ይብለከ ፡ ፲ወ፪ሐዋርያት ፡ ወዛቲ ፡ ዐጸድ ፣፡ ምቅዋሞሙ ፡ለደቂ ቀ ፡አዛሔል ፡ ወልደ ፡ ቀዓት ፡፤ ወልደ ፡ ሌዊ ፤ ወአዛሔል ፡ ወልደ፣፡ኔስ ፡ እንዘ ፤ ተወል ይ ፡ በወርጎ ፡ ኔሳን ፡ ወኔሳን ፣ ወልደ" ፡ አያብ ፡ ወአያብ ፡ ጳኮን ፡ ወጳኮን ፣ ወልደ ፡ ደ ብር ፡ ወደብ'ር ፡ ወልይ" ፡ ናር ፡ ወናር ፣፡ ዌልስ ፣፡ ወሐዋርያሃኒ ፡ ለዛቲ ፣ ዐጸድ ፡ ያዕቆ ብ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እልፍዮስ ፡ ወመልአቫኒ ፡ አቃምያል፤ ፡ ዐቃቤ ፡ ገነት ፡፡
The twelve rafters of which he speaks to you are the twelve apostles. - This enclosure is the dwelling of the children of Azahêl, son of Kohath, son of Levi. And Azahêl was the son of Nes, who was born in the month of Nisan, and Nisan begat Ayab, Ayab begat Pakon, Pakon begat Dabr, Dabr * [begat] Nar and Nar, Wëles. - And the apostle of this enclosure is James (Ya'eqob), son of Alphaeus (Alfeyos), and the angel is 'AgqâmyÂâl, the guardian of paradise.
ወዝውእቱ ፡ ዘ፲ወ፻ዐጸድ ፤ ወ፻፻፲ወ፳ ፤ አሕባል ። ወሕበሪሆን ፡ ጻዓዳ ፡ ከመ ፡ ጽጌ ፡ ረዳ ፡ ወአሐቲ ፡ ሐብል ፤ እምኔሆን ፣ ቦአመ ፡ ታስተርኢ ፡ እማእከሌሆን ፡ ወፍካሬሁኒ ፡ ነገሩኒ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ወአሮን ፣ ወይቤሉኒ ፣ አዕማድ ፡ ዕበ ፡ ይብለከ ፡ ኦሪት፣፣ ወነቢያት ፡ ወሐዋርያት ፡ ፻፻፲ወቿአሕባል ፡፣ ዘይቤ ፣ ያ፻፲ወ፳ርቱዓነ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፣፡ ወሕበሪ ሆን ፡ ከመ ፡ ጽጌ ፡ ረዳ ፤ ዕበ ፡ ይብል ፡ ጽጌ ፡ ረዳሰ ፡ ጸዓዳ ፡ ወትምህርቶሙኒ ፡ ለ፻፻፲ወ ፳ብሩህ ፡ ከመ ፡ ፀሐይ። ወአሐቲሰ ፡ ሐብል ፡ ቦአመ ፡ ታስተርኢ ፡ ማእከሌሆን ፡ ወ"ቦ፣፣ አመ ፡ ኢታስተርኢ ፡ ዕበ ፣ ይብል ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ወአምላክነ ፤ ወቦአመ ፣፡ ያስተርኢ ፡ማ እከሌሆሙ ፡፣ ወቦአመ ፡ ይትኀብኦሙ ፡፣ ቦአመ ፡፣ ይከውኑ ፡ ፻፻፲ወ፳ ። ወዝ ፣፤ ዘይከው ን ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትአመሮሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እግዚእ ፡፡ ወዛቲ ፡ ዐጸድ ፣፡ ምቅዋሞሙ ፡ለ
And this is the thirteenth enclosure, which has 318 chevrons, whose color is white like the rose flower, and there is a time when one of them appears in their midst. The explanation was given to me by Moses and Aaron; they said to me: When one speaks to you of columns, these are the Law, the Prophets, and the Apostles; the 318 chevrons in question are the 318 orthodox fathers (of Nicaea); when it is said that their color is like the rose flower, it is because the rose flower is white and the doctrine of the 318 fathers is brilliant like the sun; when it is said to you that there is a chevron that sometimes appears in their midst and * sometimes disappears, it is Our Lord and our God: sometimes he shows himself in their midst and sometimes he hides himself, sometimes they are 318. It is thus so that they may know that he is the Lord. - This enclosure is the dwelling of the children of Kebron, son of 'Oren, and Oren of Amhan, and Amhan of Tegran,


[p. 63] {#po.1.p63}
ደቂቀ ፡ ኬብሮን ፡ ወልደ ፡ ዖሬን ፡ አምሐን ፣፤ ወአምሐን ፡ ትግራን ፡ ወአልሂዝ ፣ ወሴዌ ን ፡ ወልደ ፡ ዋክር ፣፡ ወሐዋርያሃኒ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፦ ወመልአካከኒ ፡ አክያል ፡ መልአክ ፡፡
and 'Alhiz and Sewen son of Waker. - And the apostle [of this enclosure] is Paul (Pawlos) and the angel is the angel 'Akyal.
ወዝውእቱ ፡ ፲ወ8ዐጸድ ፡ ወባቲ ፡ ፪አዕማድ ፡ ወ፳ዐምድ ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ወ፳8ንኡስ ፡ ወቦቱ ፡ ለአሕባሊሆን ፣ ከመ ፡ ቅርፍተ ፡ ሮማን ፡ ወባቲ ፡ ፻አሕባል ። ወፍካሬሁኒ ፡ ነገሩኒ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ወአሮን ፡ ወይቤሉኒ ፡ ፪አዕማድ ፡ ዕበ ፣ ይብሉ ፡ "፪ሰማ ዕት ፡ አለ ፡ አንጾኪያ ፡ ወበኢየሩሳሌም ፡፡ ፳ዓምድ ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይብል ፡ ሰማዕተ ፡ አ ንጾኪያ ፡፡ ፳ዐምድ ፡ ንኡስ ፡ ዕበ ፡ ይብለከ ፡ ንኡስ ፡ ሕፃናት ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ዘኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወሕበሪሆሙ ፡ ለአዕማድ ፡፤ ከመ ፡ ቅርፍተ ፡ ሮማን ፡ ዕበ ፣ ይብል ፡ አምሳለ ፡ ክዕወተ ፡ደ ሞሙ ፡ ለሰማዕት ፣ ወ፻አሕባል ፡ ሶበ ፣ ይብል ፡ አምሳለ ፣ ሥላሴ ፡፣ ዘየዐቅብዎ ፣ ሰማዕ ት ፡ ወባሕቱ ፡ ሰማዕተ ፡ አንጾኪ ፡ የዐቢዩ' ፡ እምሰማዕተ ፤፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወበኅቤነ" ፡ ወበ ኅበ ፡ ልዑል ፡ ለሊሁ ፡ ያአምር ፡ ዘየዐቢ ፡ ወብዙኃን ፣ እምደቂቅ ፣፡ እለ ፡ኮኑ ፡ ሰማዕተ፣፣ ወዐቀቡ ፣ ድንግልናሆሙ ፡ ወፈድፋዶ ፡ ይቤሉ ፡ በእንተ ፣ ቴዎድሮስ ፡ ግሙራ ፡ አልቦ፣ አመ ፡ ሰከበ ፡ ም”ስለ ፡ አንስት ፡ ወኢያውፅአ ፡ ዘርአ ፡ላዕለ ፡ አንስት ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ በሕል መ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ አው ፡ ሶበ ፣፡ ቀደምዎ ፣፤ አብያጺሁ ፣ እንዘ ፣ ይትሜሀር ፡መጽሐፈ፣፤እንዘ፣፤ ይበኪ ፣ ወይሬኢ ፡ ሀብተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወዝንቱ ፡ አስማት ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብተራ ፡ ዘይ ብል ፡ ጸባኦት ፣ ሰዳይ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡፡
And this is the fourteenth enclosure; it comprises two columns, one large and one small, and the chevrons are like pomegranate bark; there are three of them. And the following explanation was given to me by Moses and Aaron. They said to me: When one mentions two columns *, these are the two [kinds of] martyrs, those of Antioch and those of Jerusalem. When it cites a large column, these are the martyrs of Antioch, and the small one, the child martyrs of Jerusalem. The color of the columns is like pomegranate bark, he says, it is the image of the shedding of the blood of the martyrs. - When it says three chevrons, it is the image of the Trinity that the martyrs venerated, but the martyrs of Antioch are greater than those of Jerusalem for us, and for the Most High Himself knows what is greater. Among the children, many were martyrs, and kept their chastity; one cites especially Theodore (Tewoderos), who nunquam concubuit * cum muliere, neque semen effudit super mulierem nisi in somnis noctis; when his comrades surpassed him, while he was learning in the Scripture, weeping he saw the gifts of heaven. In the tabernacle are written the words: Sabaot, Sadday and Samuel said.


[p. 64] {#po.1.p64}
ዘ፲ወ፳ ፡ ዐጸድ ፡ ወባቲ ፡ ፪አዕማድ ፡ ፳ዐምድ ፡ ይመስል ፡ሕብረ ፡ ማይ ፡ ወክልኡ ፡ይ ነድድ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሳት ፡ ወ8ሐብሎን ፡ ለክልኤሆን ፡ አዕማድ ።
[Description] of the fifteenth enclosure: It has two columns, one of which resembles the color of water and the other shines like a flaming fire, and there is a single chevron for these two columns.
ወፍካሬሁኒ ፡ ነገሩኒ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ አሮን' ፡ ወይቤሉኒ ፡ ክልኤ ፡ አዕማድ ፡ ሶበ ፣ ይብል ፡ ኤልያስ ፡ ወዮሐንስ ፡ ወ፳ዐምድ ፡ ይነድ!ድ ፣ ከመ ፡ እሳት ፡ ሶበ ፣ ይብል ፡፤ ኤልያስ ፤ ውእቱ ፡ ዘንዱድ ፡ ልቡ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ቅንአተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወዝኒ ፡ ዘይቤ ፣ ፳"ዘሕብ ረ ፡ ማይ ‹ ዮሐንስ ፡ ዘአጥመቀ ፡ በማይ ፡ አሕዛበ ፡ ወለሐብለ ፡ ክልኤሆን ፡ አሐቲ ፡ሶ በ፣ይብል ፡ አዲም ፡ ይቀንቱ ፡ ክልኤሆሙ ፡ ወዛቲ ፡ ዐጸድ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ ሚራሪ ። ወሚራ ሪ ፡ ወለደ ፡ ኃሙሲ ፡ ወሎቢሂኒ ፡ ወለደ ፡ ስምይ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ዘይብል ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ደብተራ ፡ያ ኤል ፡ ሰማዔ ፡ስአለታት ፣ ብሂል ። ወለ፲ወኗዐጸድ ፤ ከመዝ ፡ ነገራቲሃ ፡ ወባቲ ፡ ጳአዕማድ ፡ ወ፳፻አርጋብ ፣ ወ፪፻ጸዓድው ፡ ወ፳፻ጸሊማን ፡ ወባቲ ፡ ፪አሕባል ።
The explanation was given to me by Moses and Aaron; they said to me: The two columns of which it speaks are Elijah (Elyas) and John, and when it mentions a column that burns like fire, it is Elijah whose heart was inflamed with zeal for God. When it says that one * was the color of water, it is John who baptizes the nations (peoples) in water. When it says that the chevron is unique for both, it indicates the leather that they both girded themselves with. - This enclosure is that of the children of Mirari, Mirari begat Hamusi and Lobini begat Semey. Thus what is said is the tabernacle of Yä'el, meaning the one who listens to prayers(?). The description of the sixteenth enclosure is as follows: It contains four columns and 500 doves, of which 400 are white and 100 are black, plus two chevrons.
ወፍካሬሁኒ;!ነገሩኒ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ወአሮን ፡ ወ8ዐምድ ፡ ዕበ ፡ ይብል ፡ 8ፎመምህራን ፡ ወእ ሉ፣መቃርስ ፡ ወእንጦንስ ፡ ወሲኖዳ ፣ ወጳኩሚስ ።፡ ወ፪፻ጸዓድው ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ እሉ ፡ 8መ ምህራን ፡ ፳፻8ሊማን ፣ መነኮ”ሳት ፣ ደኀርት ፣ ወ፪አሕባል ፡ ጾሞሙ ፣ ወጸሎቶሙ ፡ ለቅ ዱሳን ። ወውስቴቱ ፡ ስዑል ፡ ስፅሎሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፡ ለአበው ፡ ቀደምት ፡ ቀዳሜ ፣ አዳም፣
And Moses and Aaron gave me this explanation: When it says four columns, these are the four hegumens, namely Macarius (Magares), Anthony (Antons), Shenoute (Sinoda, Σινούθιος) and Pachomius (Pakuemis). The 400 white doves are the children of these four priors and the 100 black ones are the later * monks. The two chevrons are the fasts and prayers of the saints. Inside are represented the portraits of the first holy fathers.


[p. 65] {#po.1.p65}
ሴት ፡ ሄኖክ' ፡፡ ቃይናን ፡፡ መላልኤል ፡ ያሬድ ፡ ሄኖክ ፣ ማቱሰላ ፣ ላሜኅ ፡ ኖኅ፡ሴም፣7፣ አልፋክስድ ፣ ወቃይናም ፡ ሳላ ፡ እቤር ፣ ፋሌክ ፣፤ ራግው ፡ ሴሮኅ ፡ ናኮር ፡ ታራ ፡ ኣብ ርሃም ፡ ይስሐቅ ፡ ወያዕቆብ ፡፡ ወተዓፀው ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ዕፃ ፡ ወሰዐልዎ ፣ ማእከል ፣ ደብተ ራ ፡ ወለአበውሂ ፡ ሰዐሎሙ ፡ መልአከ ፡ገጽ ፡ ወድኅረ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ፋሬስ ።፡ ኤስሮም ፡ አ ራም ፡ አሚናዳብ ፡ አሶን ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡=፡ በዔዝ ፣ ኢየቤድ ፡ እሴይ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ ሮ ብአም ፡ አብያ ፡ አስፍ ፣ ኢዮሳፍጥ ፡ ኢዮራም ፣ ኢዮ”አስ ፣ አሜስ ፡ አዘርያስ ፣ ዖዝያ ን ፡ ኢዮአታም ፡ አካዝ ፡ ወሕዝቅያስ ፡ ምናሴ ፣፡ አሞጽ ፡ ኢዮስያስ ፡ ኢኮንያን ፡ ሰላትያ ል ፡ ዘሩባቤል ፡ አብዩዳ ፣ ኤልየቄም ፡ አዘር ፡ ሰዶቅ ፣ ዓልዓዘር ፡ ኤልዩድ ፡ አኪም ፡አ ልዓዘር ፍ፡ ማታን ፡ያዕቆብ ፡ ወዮሴፍ ፡፡ ወእሉ ፡ ጽሑፋን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ወበእንተ ዝ ፡ ዘመደ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ድንግል ፡ ዘኮኑ ፡ ድኅኑ ።
First Adam, Seth, Enos, Qaynan, Malal'el, Yared, Enoch, Matusala, Lamech, Noah, Shem, Alfaksad, Qaynam, Sala, 'Eber, Falek, Ragw, Seroh, Nakor (Arphaxad, Cainan, Heber, Phaleg, Reu, Sarug, Nachor), Abraham, Isaac and Jacob - They drew lots, and the one whom the lot designated was represented in the middle of the tabernacle. As for the fathers, it is an angel of the face who represented them. - Then Judah, Phares, Esrom, Aram, 'Aminadab, 'Ason, Solomon, Ba'ez, Iyabed, 'Esy, David, Solomon, Robeam, 'Abya, 'Asef, Iyosafet, Iyoram *, Iyoas, Ames, Azaryas, 'Ozyan, Iyo'atam, 'Akaz and Hezeqyas, Menase, 'Amos, Iyosyas, Ikonyan, Salatyal, Zarubabel, 'Abyuda, 'Elyagem, 'Azar, Sadog, 'Al'azar, 'Elyud, 'Akim, 'Al'ezar, Matan, Ya'eqob and Joseph (Yosef). These are inscribed in the Gospel - and being relatives of Mary, the Virgin - it is for this reason that they were saved.
ለ፲ዉፄዐጸድ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ነገራቲሃ ፡ ወባቲ፻ ፡ አፅማድ ፡ ለቃዳሚትሰ ፡ ዐምድ ፡ ቿአሕባ ሊሃ ፡ ወለካልእሰ ፡ ዐምድ ፡ ፪ወለሳልስት ፡ ፲ወ፪ ።
And here is the description of the seventeenth enclosure: it has three columns, the first has eight chevrons, the second two and the third twelve.
ወፍካሬሁኒ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ፻አዕማድ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይብል ፡ አብርሃም ፡ ይስሐቅ ፡ ወያዕቆብ ፣ ወለቀዳሚት ፡ ዐምድ ፡ ፳አሕባሊሃ ፡ "ሶበ ፡ ይብል ፡ ደቀ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ጸዘወለደ ፡እምኬ ጡራ ፡ ወ፳እምሰራ ፡ ወ8ኒ ፡ እምአጋር ፡ ወአስማቲሆሙ ። ዘንበር ፡ ወያክንስ ፡ ማዳይ ፣ ወማዳን ፡ ኢያዝበቅ ። ወሴሕያ ፤ ወይስማዔል ፡ ወይስሐቅ ። ወለካልእት ፡ ዐምድ ፡ ፪አ
The explanation is this: when it says three columns, these are Abraham, Isaac and Jacob; when it says that the first has eight chevrons, these are * the children of Abraham, six that he had by Keturah, one by Sarah and another by Hagar. - And here are their names: Zanbar, Yakens, Maday and Madan, 'Iyazebaq, Sehya, Yesma'el and Yeshag. - When it says that the second column had two chevrons, this


[p. 66] {#po.1.p66}
ሕባሊሃ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይብል ፡ ዳቂቀ ፡ ይስሐቅ ፣ ያዕቆብ ፣ ወዔሳው ፣ ወለሣልሲት ፣ ዐምድ፣ ፲ወ፪አሕባሊሃ ፡ ዕበ ፡ ይብል ፡ ዳቂቀ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ እሉ ፡
are the children of Isaac, Jacob and 'Esau. - And when it says that the third had twelve chevrons, these are the sons of Jacob.
ወዝኩሉ ፡ ም[ስ]ጢራ ፡ ለደብተራ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ነገረ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወሕ ዝብኒ ፡ ይቀውሙ ፣ በበ ፡ ነገዶሙ ፡ እንተ ፡ መንገለ ፡ ምሥራቅ ፡ ይቀውሙ ፡ ነገደ ፡ይ ሁዳ ፡ ወእለ ፡ ይትዐየኑሂ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ወነገደ ፡ ሮቤል ፡ መንገለ ፡ የማን ፡ይቀውሙ፣ ወእለ ፡ ይትዐየ”ኑሂ ፡ በበ ፡ ነገዶሙሂ ፡ ወነገደ ፡ ዮሌፍ ፡ ይቀውሙ ፡ መንገለ ፡ ባሕር ፣ ወአለ ፡ ይትዐየኑሂ ፣ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ወነገደ ፡ ዳን ፡ ይቀውሙ ፡ ድኅረ ፣ዓረቢሁ፡፣፡ ለደብተ ራ ፡ ወእለ ፡ ይትዐየኑሂ ፡ ኅቡረ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይግዕዙ ፡ በጾታ ፣ ወከማሁ ፡ ይቀውሙ ፡ ው ኢይትዐደው ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሳብዕ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ምሥዋዕ ፡ ወአሉ ፡ አበው ፡ ስዑላን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልብሱ ፡ ለአሮን ፡ ለይሁዳ ፡ ሰርድዮን ፡ ወለይሳኮር፣ ጳዝዮን ፡ ለዛብሎን ፣ መረግድ ፡ ለአታ ፣ ወለሮቤል ፡ ኢያስዲስ ፣ ለስምዖን ፡፣ ወለሌዊ ፡ ከመ ፡ ፍሕም ፡ ለጋድ ፡ ሰንፔር ፡ 8ጾታ ፡ወለዮሴፍ ፣ አሜቴስጢኖስ ፡ ወለብንያም ፡ አቀ ጠቀማጡስ ፡ ወለምናሴ ፡ ጥልም ፡ ለጋድ ፡ አቅታ ፡ ቤተሜኔስ ፡ ወለንፍታሌም ፡ ሶበው ም ። ወለአሴር ፡ በ”ረሌ ፡፡.
These are all the mysteries of the tabernacle, such as the angel of God explained them to me. And the people are distributed according to their tribes: towards the east is placed the tribe of Judah, with those who camped with him; the tribe of Reuben (Robel) is towards the right, as well as those who camped * with him according to their families; the tribe of Joseph is arranged towards the sea (the west), with those who camped with him: the tribe of Dan is placed behind, to the west of the tabernacle, as well as those who were camped with him. With the same order according to which they march, they arrange themselves when stopping, and they do not pass into the seventh [tabernacle] where the altar is. The fathers of the tribes were represented on the costume of Aaron: Judah by the sardius, Issachar (Yesaakor) by the topaz, Zebulun (Zabelon) by the emerald. Aia and Reuben (Robel) by the jasper, Simeon and Levi by the carbuncle, Gad by the sapphire, in one row; Joseph by the amethyst, Benjamin (Benyam) by the agate; Manasseh (Menase) by the Telem, Gad by the chrysolite, Naphtali by the onyx, Asher by the beryl *.
ወፍካሬሁኒ ፡ ከመዝ ። ሰርድዮን ፣ ብሂል ፡ ተመሥረተ ፡ በእግዚአብሔር ፡ ጳዝዮን ፣ብ ሂል ፡ ዜነወት ፣ ሕማማተ ፡ ዋሕድ ፡ ብሂል ፡ መረግድ ፡ ብሂል ፡ መረግደ ፡ ሕዛባ ፡ በእሳ
Here is the explanation of these stones: the sardius signifies: it was founded by God; the topaz, it announced the sufferings of the Only-Begotten; the emerald, ... the people(?) by fire; the jasper, it violently swore


[p. 67] {#po.1.p67}
ት ፡ ብሂል ፡ ኢያስሏስ ፡ ብሂል ፡ አፈድፈደት ፡ ተሐልፎተ ፡ ቃል ፡ ብሂል ።፡ ከመ፡ፍ ሕም ፡ ሶዕበ ፡ ይብል ፡ ከመ ፡ ፍሕም ፡ ቅንአቶሙ ፡ ለካህናት ፡ ብሂል ። ሰንፔር ፡፣ብሂል፣ ንሰግድ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መካን ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቆመ ፡ እግረ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ብሂል ፡ አሜቴስጤኖስ ፡ ብሂል ፡ እንበለ ፡ ዕድሜሁ ፡ አፍረየት ፡ ሠዊተ ፡ ብሂል ፡ አክጥስ ፣ ብሂል ፡ አቀጥቀጠቶሙ ፡ ለ አማንያን ፡ ወድኅረ ፡ አምነት ፡ ለሊሃ ፡ ጥልም ፡ ብሂል ፡ ጠለዋ" ፡ከመ ፡ እንተ ፡ ገለብ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፕልቄ ፡ እለ ፡ ከማሃ ፡ ብሂል ። አቅትማ ፡ ብሂል ፡ አ"ቅተለቶ ፡ ለአግዚአብሔ ር ፡ ትቤ ፡ አርእዩነመ ፡ ኪኖ ፡ ብሂል ፡ ወዝ ፡ ፍክሬሁ ። ወዝኒ ፡ ምስጢር ፡ ወመንክር፣
the carbuncle is the emblem of the zeal of the priests; the sapphire signifies: we prostrate ourselves at the place where the feet of Our Lord have been; the amethyst signifies: before the time it produced ears of corn; the agate, it broke the believers, then it was a believer itself; telem signifies: they were treacherous(?) like a hook among those who were like them. 'Agtema signifies * it made God be killed and tebe signifies: show us his art. Such is the explanation [of these stones] and such is the admirable mystery. - The apostle of this enclosure is Stephen ('Estifanos) and the angel, the one who spoke to me.
ውእቱ ። ወሐዋርያኝሃኒ ፡ ለዛታ ፡ ዓጸድ ፡ እስጢፋኖስ ፡ ወመልአካኒ ፡ ዘነገረኒ ። ወቀዳሜ ፡ ዐምድ ፡ ሶበ ፣ ይብል ፡ ብርስባሔ ፡ ፒልሎስ ። ወክልእ ፡ ዐምድ ፡ ይብል፣ ኤሴዴሬስ ፡ ወቀዋሚሆሙ ፡ ለካህናተ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ወሣልስ ፡ ዐምድ ፡ ይብል ፡ ወናጢ ኤ ፡ አአቅ ፡ ለበጤር' ፡ ወፍካሬሁኒ ፡ ብርስባሔ ፡ ብሂል ፡ አብ ፡ ዘይሴባሕ ፡ በወልድ ፣፡ብ ሂል ፡ ወፒሎስኒ ፡ ብሂል ፡ ወስብሐቲሆሙኒ ፡ እምኀቤሆሙ ፡ ብሂል ፡ ወኤሴዲሮስሂ፣ ብሂል ፡ አብ ፡ ኢይቀድም ፡ እምወልድ ። ወኤሲዲሮስኒ ፡ የማነ ፡ አብ ፡ ብሂል ፡ ወልድ ፣ ዝኒ ፡ ወቀዋሚሆሙ ፡ ለሊ"ቃናተ ፡ እስራኤል ፤ ዕበ ፡ ይብል ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ቆመ፣ ማእከሎሙ ፡፣ ለሐዋርያት ፡ እንዘ ፡ እዕፅፅው ፡ ኀዋኅው ፡'በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዳዊት ፣ እግዚኦ ብሔር ፡ ቆመ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ አማልክት ፡ ወጢኖጢኪ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይብል ፡ ርጤን ፡ዘ ኮነነ ። መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወአክለቤፔርሂ ፡ ቀዳሴ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ወነፍስ ፡ ብሂል ።
When it says the first column, it is Bersebähe Pilelos; the second column is named 'Esederes, and the chief of the priests of Israel; the third is called Natie-A'aq-labater. Here is the explanation of these names: Bersebähe signifies the Father who is praised by the Son; Pilos signifies: their praises come from them; 'Esediros signifies: the Father did not precede the Son. Esidiros also signifies the right hand of the Father which is the Son. If it says that he is the chief of the princes of Israel *, it is because this one (the Son) appeared in the midst of the apostles, even though they had closed the doors, as David says: "God stands in the assembly of the Gods" and when it says: tinotiki, it is the balm that the Holy Spirit has been for us; Aklabepër signifies the one who sanctifies the body and the spirit.


[p. 68] {#po.1.p68}

I make the sign of the cross and I proclaim the Trinity, and I believe in this trinity which comprises three persons and one single divinity, one single reign, one single power, one single dominion, one single will, one single desire; three aspects and one single appearance, three persons and one single name, a complete equality, one single totality, just as it is said: "The sun dwells in the sky and its light dwells on the earth * and its fire sets ablaze." By this example the Trinity is known. There are four chevrons, the ark in which is the law of Moses, the golden box of Manna, the rod of Aaron that sprouted, the golden censer, the four whips of cords. There are represented four cherubim and a mountain the color of transparent crystal above the ark. And here is the explanation: when it mentions four chevrons, these are the four books that Ezra ('Ezra) wrote; when it speaks of the ark, it is the new ark, the pure Mary. There was the ark of Moses and here we are, ourselves, the ark of the Holy Spirit, as Paul says: "You are the ark (the temple) of the Holy Spirit, Christ." There was only the decalogue placed in the ark and * here the Word of the Father, the only Son alone in the womb of Mary. Just as the book of Kings says: "There is nothing inside the ark but the decalogue
ወጎሞርኒ ፡ አምሳለ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወመናሂ ፡ ዘውስቴታ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለወልድ፣

Son; the golden lid the apostles; the rod of Aaron that budded is interpreted by the key of Peter and the rod of Moses by the cross of Christ. The rod of Moses, by striking the sea, made Israel cross and the cross of Christ, having struck the head of the dragon which is the devil, made the peoples pass from death to life. There the rod of Moses accomplished ten miracles and here * the cross of Christ broke the head of ten demons.

When it speaks of the whip of cords, that means that there they punish with forty lashes of the whip the one who has sinned, as the Law says: "If someone has profaned the chrism, one will strike him with 143 blows, and if it is to heal [from an illness] that he has drunk it, let him be led to a place from which he will not return and where he will be led to be put to death." The four cherubim are the four evangelists. When it says that on the ark there is a mountain the color of transparent crystal, the mountain signifies a cloud and when it says that it was the color of crystal, these are the children of baptism (the Christians), for when Our Lord was baptized, a luminous cloud covered his head, as the Evangelist says: "There appeared a bright cloud, which overshadowed them." * Paul also says: "We ourselves have witnesses who surround us like a cloud." Exodus (the Law) also says: "The tabernacle was covered by a cloud." Job says for his part: "A cloud

very bright all at once." Zechariah also says: "For it has arisen from the cloud of his saints." And David says: "He guided them during the day by the cloud." - Solomon also says: "The cloud has caused the dew to fall." Isaiah says on the other hand: "I will cover my people with a cloud." Isaiah says again: "A vineyard was to the beloved in a fertile land; I enclosed it in a fence of solid stones, I surrounded it with a hedge and I planted there choice vine among the wild vine; I built a tower in the middle, then I waited for it to produce grapes *, and it produced thorns. Judge between my vineyard and me, says God." - Here is the explanation of this passage: the vineyard in question is Israel as this prophet says; the fertile land is Jerusalem upon which descended the life-giving dew from the heights of the heavens. When it says: I surrounded it with a wall, that means that Israel made in the desert a platform of bronze 70 cubits long, that they covered it with earth and that they planted a vineyard there, just as David says: "They planted the vineyard and sowed the fields." When the moment arrived they watered it with water; when they began to march, it marched with them and rose by the order of God the Holy Spirit. This ordinance * subsisted until the death of Moses.
ከ፡አመ፡ሞተ፡ሙሴ። ወኩሉ ፡ ኑባሬሆሙ ፡ ለእስራኤል ፡ በሥርዐት ። ወዝንቱ ፡፣ በዓላቲሆሙ ፡፣ ፋሲካ ፡ና
The entire condition of Israel was regulated by ordinances. Here were their feasts: the passover of unleavened bread, the feast of the ear of corn, the sophar, the bader,

ዕት ፡ በዓለ ፡ ሠዊት ፡ መጥቅዕ ፡ በድሮሙ ፡ መጸለት ፡ ሰንበት ፡ አሉ ። ወበበ ፡ በዓላቲሆ ሙ ፡ ይትጋብኡ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ወይትማሐሉ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብተራ ፡ ወበይእቲ፤ ፅለት ፡ ይነሥእ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ክህናት ፡ እጐልተ ፡ ቀይሕ ፡ ዘኢሰሐበት ፣፡ አርዑተ ፡ ወይብል ፤ ዛቲ ፡ ላሀም ፡ ቤዛ ፡ ኀጢአቶሙ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ወየሐርዳኗኒ ፡ ርኩስ ፣ ወአለ ፡ አኅዝ ዋኒ ፡ ርኩሳን ፡ ወዘገሰሳኒ ፡ ርኩስ ፣፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወዘሦዐኒ ፣ ርኩስ ፡ ወዘከዐወ ፣ ደማኒ ፡ ርኩ ስ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወዘነዝኀኒ ፡ ለሕዝብ ፡ ደማ' ፡ ርኩስ ፣ ወሕዝብሰ”" ፡ እለ ፡ ተነዝ ፡ በደ ማ ፡ "ቅዱሳን ፡ አመንክር ፡ ዝነገር ፡ ወፍካሬሁኒ ፡፣ ከመዝ ፡ እጐልት ፡ ቀይሕ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይብል ፡ ማርያም ፡ ዘኢሰሐበት ፡ አር ዑተ ፡ሶበ ፡ ይብል ፡ ዘኢያአመረት ፡ ብእሴ ፡ ብሂል ፡ ወበከመ ፣ ማርያም ፡እጐልት፣0ቀ ይሕ ፡ ወከማሁ ፡ እግዚእነሂ ፡ ተመስለ ፡ በአእኾልት ፡ ቀይሕ ፡ እስመ ፡ ለብሰ ፡ ሥጋሃ፣ ለ ማርያም ፡ ወእለ ፡ ሦዑኒ ፡ ሰቃልያን ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ አይሁድ ። ወሕዝብኒ ፡ እለ ፡ ተነዝጉ ፡ በደሙ ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ቅዱሳን ፣ እሙንቱ ። ወበይእቲ ፡ ፅለት ፣ ይበውእ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ካህናት ፣ ውስት ፣ ቅድስተ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ወይበው እ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ ካሀን ፡ ዘኅረየ ፣፡ ወአሐቲ ፡ ወለት ፡ ድንግል ፡ ኅሪት ። ወይ"ሜ ጥዋ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እደዊሃ ፡ አጽቀ ፡ ወይን ፡ ወሰብለ ፡ ስርናይ ፣ ወስበኒአ" ፡ ይትናገር ፡ ት ሁብ ፡ ትእምርተ ፡ ቃሉ ፡ ለሕዝብ ።
the tabernacles, the sabbath. On feast days, all the people gathered and all took an oath in the tabernacle. That day, the high priest took a red heifer that had not yet borne the yoke and said: "This heifer is [offered for] the redemption of the sins of the people," and he slaughtered it as impure. Those who had seized it were impure, even the one who touched it; the one who offered it in sacrifice and the one who poured its blood were also impure; the one who sprinkled the people with its blood was also impure, but those who were sprinkled with it * were holy. O admirable wonder! Here is the explanation: when it says: A red heifer, it is Mary. When it adds that it has not undergone the yoke, that means that she has not known man; and, just as Mary is the red heifer, so Our Lord is likened to this same heifer, for he has clothed himself in the flesh of Mary. Those who immolate the heifer are the crucifiers, the assembly (the synagogue) of the Jews, and the people who are sprinkled with its blood is the people of the Christians, who are holy. This same feast day, the high priest entered into the holy of holies, accompanied by a priest and a virgin whom he had chosen. One placed * in the hands of the latter a branch of vine and an ear of wheat. Then, after having taken the turban (?), he said: "You will give a sign," such is his word to the people.


[p. 72] {#po.1.p72}
ወፍካሬሁኒ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ካህናትሰ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ ወቅድስተ ፡ ቅዱሳንኒ ፡ አምሳለ ፡ ጥምቀት ፡ ቅድስት ። ወካሀንኒ ፡ ዘይቀውም ፡ በየማን ፡ አምሳለ ፡ ዮሐንስ፣፡መ ጥምቅ ፡ ወድንግልኒ ፡ ኅሪት ፡ አምሳለ ፡ መልከ ፡ ጴዴቅ ፡ ካሀን ፡ ወአጽቀ ፡ ወይን ፡ወሰ ብለ ፡ ስርናይ ፡ አምሳለ ፣ ኅብስት ፡ ወወይን ፡ ወመልከ ፡ ጺዴቅ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይትናገር ፡ ነገ ረ ፡ ለሕዝብ ፡ ወትሁብ ፡ ትእምርተ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይብል ፡ ትእምርተ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወደሙ ፡ ዘወሀቦ ፡ መልከ ፡ ጹዴቅ ፡ ለአብርሃም ። ወ”በእንተዝ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ አንተ ፡ ካህኑ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ በከመ ፡ ሚመቱ ፡ ለመልከ ፡ ጴዴቅ ።
This passage is explained thus: The high priest is Christ; the holy of holies is the image of holy baptism; the priest who stands on the right represents John the Baptist; the chosen virgin, Melchizedek (Malka-Sedeq) the pontiff. The branch of vine and the ear of wheat are the symbols of wine and bread (of the Eucharist); when Melchizedek speaks to the people, and when he says: "You will give a sign," he alludes to the sign of the body and the blood (of Christ) that Melchizedek gave to Abraham, and * it is for this that David says: "You are a priest forever according to the order of Melchizedek."
ወኢሳይያስኒ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ እንተ ፡ሀገረ ፡ ሐጌ ፡ ወየኅልፍ ፡.እንተ ፡ መጌይን። ወያነብር ፣ ንዋዮ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ምክማስ ፡ ወየኅልፍ ፡ ቁላ ። ወያስተርኢ ፣ እምአዳብር ፡ ወይትሌዐል ፡ እምአውግር ፡ ወበጺሖ ፡ ሐጌ ፡ ይፈርሁ ፡ ሬማ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሰሙኤል ። ወት ጐይይ ፡ ወለተ ፡ ጋለን ፣ ወይሰማዕ ፣ በኤሶን ፣ ወአናቶት ፣ ወይደነግፁ ፣ ሜዴናዊያን ።
Isaiah also says: "He will come towards the city of Hage, will pass to Magedon, he will establish his riches at Mekmas and will cross the valley; he will be seen from the mountains, will rise above the hills and at his arrival at Hage, Rema, the city of Samuel, will be struck with fear. The daughter of Galan will flee; she will be heard at 'Eson and at 'Anatot, and the Medenawi (the inhabitants of Madmena) will tremble."
ወፍካሬሁኒ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ውእቱ ። ሐጌሰ ፡ መልአከ፣ ሐይወት ፡ ብሂል ፡ ወሀገርኒ ፡ ዓለ ም ። ወመጌዶንኒ ፣ ሕንብርተ ፡ ምድር ፡ ብሂል ፡ ወያነብር ፡ ንዋዮ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምክማስ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይብል ፡ ወንዋዩሰ ፣ ለወልድ ፡ "ሥጋሁ ፡ ለወልድ ፡ ወደሙ ፡ እለ ፡ አርአየ ፡ አ ንቢሮ ፡ ኀበ ፣ መልከ ፡ ጹዴቅ ። ወቄላኒ ፡ ሕዝብ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ወቄ
Here is the explanation of this passage; Hage signifies the angel of life; the city is the world; Magedon is the navel of the earth. When it says that he will place his riches at Mekmas, these are the riches of the Son, that is to say * his body and his blood, which he showed after having deposited them with Melchizedek. The valley is the people; it is for this that David says "The valleys will be full of wheat." The wheat is the word of God; the mountains are the prophets and the hills the apostles. - When it says


[p. 73] {#po.1.p73}
ላትኒ ፡ ይመልእ ፡ ስርናይ ፡ ወስርናይሰ ፡ ቃለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአድባርኒ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ ወአውግርኒ ፡ ሐዋርያት ። ወበጺሖ ፡ ሐጌ ፡ ይፈርሁ ፡ ሬማ ፡ ሀገረ ፣ ሳሙኤል ፡ሶበ፣ ይብል ፡ በጺሖ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ፡ ፈርሁ ፡ ዘመደ ፡ ሰጥናኤል ፡ እለ ፡ ወረ ዱ ፡ እምሬማ ። ወሳሙኤልኒ ፡ አዳም ፡ ብሂል ፡ ወትጐይይ ፡ ወለተ ፡ ጋላን ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይ ብል ፡ ሲኦል ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ወጋላንሂ ፣ ወሬዛ ፣ ብሂል ፡ ወወሬዛሂ ፡ ኀጢአት ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ሰይጣን ። ተሰምየ ፡ ወሬዛ ፡ "ይቤ ፡ ኢዮብ ፡ ወይቀውም ፡ ከመ ፡ ወሬዛ ፡ ንኡስ ። ወ ሰሎሞንኒ ፡ ይቤ ፡ አሌ ፡ ለኪ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ዘወሬዛ ፡ ንጉሥኪ ። ወይሰማዕ ፡ በኤንሶን ። ወአናቶት ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይብል ፡ በኦሪት ፣ተየድዐ ፣ ምጽአተ ፣ ወልድ ፣ ወይደነግፁ ፣ መንዴ ናዊያን ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይብል ፡ አጋንንት ፡ ወሰቃልያን ፡ ደንገፁ ፡ ሶበ ፣፡ ተንሥአ ፣ ወልድ ።
that at his arrival at Hage, Rema, the city of Samuel, will be struck with fear, that means that at the arrival of the Lord in Sheol, the tribes of Satna'el, who descended from Rema, were struck with terror. Samuel signifies Adam. - When it adds that the daughter of Galan will flee, it is Sheol; Galan signifies a young man and a young man signifies sin, that is to say Satan. - One calls him a young man *; Job says: "And he has the bearing of an adolescent." Solomon also says: "Woe to you, O city, whose king is a young man." - And when it says that he will be heard at 'Enson and at 'Anatot, that means that the coming of the Son was known by the law. - When it says that the Mandenäwi will be frightened, that means that the demons and the crucifiers were frightened when the Son was resurrected.
ወበትንሣኤሁ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ኢሳይያስ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ እትነሣእ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፣ ወይእ ዜ ፡ እሴባበሕ ፡ ወይእዜ ፡ እትሌዐል ፡ ወይእዜ ፡ ትሬእዩ ፣፡ ወይእዜ ፣፡ ታአምሩ ። ወበእንተ ሂ ፡ ሕማማቲሁ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ኢሳይያስ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ከመ ፡ በግፅ ፡ ይጠባሕ ፣ ወከመ ፡ በግዕ ፣ ዘኢይነብብ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ ዘይቀርጾ ፡ ከማሁ ፣ ኢከሠ”ተ ፣ አፉሁ ፣ በሕማሙ ፡ ወካዕበ፣ ይቤ ፡ ኢሳይያስ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሕማሙ ፡ ለወልድ ፡ አንስት ፣ ብዑላት ፣ ተንሥአ ፣ ወስምዓ ኒ ፡ ቃልየ ። አዋልድ ፡ ምእመናት ፡ አፅምአኒ ፡ ነገርየ ። ወለለ ፣፡ በዓል ፡ በበዓመት ፡ ግ በራ ፡ ተዝካርየ ። ለዕለተ ፡ ሕማምየ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትትኀዘባ ።
And concerning his resurrection. Isaiah says: "Now I rise, says the Lord; now I am glorified, I am exalted, and now you will see and you will know." In view of his sufferings (of his passion) Isaiah also says: "He came like a sheep that is going to be slaughtered, like a sheep that does not bleat before the one who shears it, he did not open * his mouth in his pain." - Isaiah says again concerning the passion of the Son: "Rich women, rise up and listen to my voice; believing daughters, lend an ear to my word. And at each feast, each year, celebrate my commemoration, by commemorating the day of my passion."

And here is the explanation that was given to me of this passage. - When it says rich women, these are the Christian churches, which are rich by the two laws, the old and the new, as well as by the glory of God. - When it says: Listen to my voice, it is the voice of the Gospel. - The young girls are the souls of the just. - When it says: Each year, celebrate my memory, it is the passover, the commemoration of his resurrection. - When it says: "Thinking of the day of my suffering" *; that means that Our Lord said: "Announce my death until I return." And Isaiah tells you clearly: "Will seven women take a husband? [They will say to him]: We will eat our bread and we will wear our clothes; let us only be called by your name and take away our reproach." - That day, they will shine in the council of his praises. Here is the explanation of this passage: When it says seven women, these are the seven guards of the Church, and the bread is the word of God, just as Paul says: "You who have been baptized by Christ, you have clothed yourselves with Christ." - When it says: "Take away our reproach," that means: Do not let us fight with goats and bulls. The light is the Apostles; just as he said to them * himself: "You are the light of the world." - The rest of Zion signifies the Christians who have remained, that is to say who have been saved. Isaiah tells you clearly: "Produce wings like the eagle." On his

side, Ezekiel expresses himself thus concerning the eagle: "This eagle with great wings, full of talons, entered into Lebanon and took away the most choice part of the cedar; he cut it in the middle, at the most tender part." And here is [the explanation]: the eagle is the Spirit, its wings are the prophets and its talons the apostles. Formerly, on the day of the feast of Bader of Israel, it hovered for two hours and left after having torn their veil, but since they crucified Our Lord, it has not returned to them, just as the prophet says: When the spirit has departed, it does not return. The prophets also * announced the coming of the Son; Moses says: "You are going to see with your eyes. The one who will not have seen the sign of the serpent will die, but the one who will have seen it will not die." The evangelist also says: "The one who will have seen the Son and will have believed in him will live eternally; but the one who will not have believed in him and who will not have seen him will die." The evangelist tells you clearly: "Just as Moses lifted up the serpent in the desert, so the Son of man will be suspended (crucified)." And when Isaiah says: "He will come to be slaughtered like a lamb," he speaks of the crucifixion of Our Lord. - Jeremiah says: "I am like the gentle sheep; they have plotted against me a bad plan and they have said: We will put a piece of wood in his bread." The wood is the cross and the bread is Christ, as he said himself: "I am the bread of life * that has descended from the heavens jealous and

And here is the explanation of this passage: Lamech was blind and it is the image of Satan, just as Cain is the image of his father Adam. Lamech killed Cain and Satan killed Adam by making him eat the fruit of the tree. At the time of Lamech arrived the flood; [by] seven cataracts of the sky and seven of the earth, and just as Lamech [is punished] seven times more than Adam, so Satan [will be punished] seven times more than the work accomplished on the day of Sunday. And as for the punishment of Lamech, Christ, the shoot of the patriarchs, came, carried on seventy-seven pearls, to punish Satan. And the seventy-seven pearls are those of which Luke, the evangelist, speaks *. His two wives are explained by the two peoples of Ham (Käm) and Japheth (Yafet). The man who killed is Adam and the young man is Abel. Let the one who is intelligent understand, says Abba-Basalota-Mika'el. Then Peter was sent to me and he said to me: I am going to explain to you the incarnation of the Only-Begotten Son. The Father, the Son and the Holy Spirit said: "We have created everything by agreement and when Adam sinned, the Son descended by obedience on the wing of Gabriel. It is because of Mary that all creation was made" and if it says: because of Mary, it is that the smallest thing will not be lost in the heavens(?). Mary carried in her womb

ቅ ፡ ወወለደቶ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተወልደ ፡ በሕገ ፡ ኦሪት ፡ ተገዝረ ፡ በሰሙን ፡ ዕለት ፣ ወበሣ፣ “ዕለት ፣ ስምዖን ፡ ተወክፎ ።
the Sun of Justice, and she gave birth to him. After his birth, he was, according to the law of Moses, circumcised on the eighth day. Then on the fortieth day, Simeon received him.

Simeon, the priest, had lived for a long time in the hope of the coming of the Lord. Formerly, when he was studying the book of Isaiah the prophet, he had found this word: "Behold, a virgin shall conceive and bear a son." He had said: "Will it then be so?" and he threw down his pen (reed) saying: "How will this birth come to pass without a man, just as grain without seed?" - But the Holy Spirit announced to him that he would not die before having seen the Messiah. Now, before seeing the Messiah, he waited 300 years, and when he caught sight of the child, he cried out: "Now, dismiss your servant." He uttered these words (and the following words) until the end (of the Nunc dimittis, Luke ii, 29) and God said to him: Tell those who are in Sheol: "Your salvation approaches; I myself have held him in my hands; the Savior of the world has arrived." The prophets also * [were filled with joy], just as the Lord says: "Joy has come from the prophets." All were stirred, so that the word of the prophet might be fulfilled: "The mountains will be shaken to their foundations." Adam then uttered cries of gladness, Noah leaped for joy, Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob rejoiced; Moses, Aaron, and Joshua gave thanks; David played the cithara, Job was astonished, Solomon sang, Isaiah bounded, Jeremiah danced, Ezekiel and Daniel clapped their hands; Tobit struck with his foot,


[p. 78] {#po.1.p78}

Ezra was amazed, Hosea composed praises, Amos glorified [God], Joel called upon the Most High, and Micah called upon the Merciful One.

The other prophets each gave thanks and returned to their dwellings. One returned to the east, another * to the west, another to the north, another to the south. Likewise, among the trees of paradise, there were four groups: those who were in the west are called prophets; those in the east, apostles; those in the north and those in the south were called priests, monks, and martyrs (and were called) Abraham, Isaac, Jacob, Moses, David, and the children were with them.
ወዕፅሂ ፡ በዘ ፡ ሰቀልዎ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ እምአይቴ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ትብል ፡ አኮኑ ፡ እምውስ ተ ፡ ኽት ፡ አምጽኣ ፡ ንስር ፡ ወወገራ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወይእቲሰ ፡ ዕፅ ፡ እትዔ ሪ፤ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፅፀው ፡ ወፈቀዱ ፡ ይስቅሉ ፡ ባቲ ፡ አለ ፡ አምውስተ ፡ ነዊኃን ፡ ወአምውስ ተ ፡ ኅጺራንሂ ፡ ተጎፅር ፡ ወኢዐረየት ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ዳአሙ ፡ በስቅለተ ፣ እግዚእ ነ ፡ ወለእግ”ዚእነሂ ፡ አብጽሕዎ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ስቅለት ፡ ይሁዳ ፣፡ ኣስቆሮታዊ ፡ ወውእቱሂ ፡ስ ሕተ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እሙ ፡ በዝሙት ፡ ወአቡሁኒ ፡ ቀተለ ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ወእኅቱሂ' ፡ ወገረ ፡ በአብ ን ፡ ወአምሔዉሂ ፡ አበየ ፡ በረከተ ፡ ይብልዎ ፡ ስሙ' ። ወሶበ ፡ መጽአ ፡ነቢይ ፡ ውስት ፣ ደወሎሙ ፡ ተባረኩ ፡ ኩሉ ፡፣ እስራኤል ፡ ወውእቱሰ ፡ ኢተባረከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይብጻሕ ፡ ቃ
"The tree upon which Our Lord was crucified, whence did it come?" you will say. Was it not from paradise that an eagle brought it and threw it into Jerusalem? And this tree was not equal to the other trees. The trees that were among the tallest wanted [Our Lord] to be crucified on this tree, so that it was shortened more than the smaller ones. It was not equal to them before, but it became so only by the crucifixion of Our Lord. * It was brought to Our Lord for his crucifixion (?) by Judas Iscariot (Yehudä Asqorotawi). This man had committed the sin of impurity with his mother; he had put his father to death by his own hand and had stoned his sister; his grandfather also had refused the blessing, they told him his name (?). When a prophet came into their region, all Israel drew blessing from him, he alone was not, so that the word of David might be fulfilled: "He refused the blessing which shall depart

from him", and the children of Israel cursed him because he did not have the gift of blessing.

Our Lord having died, rose again in his power and, at the moment of his ascension, he ordained the apostles as priests, and established the mysteries for them. To Peter, he appeared in the form of the mystery, and when he saw the flame, Peter fled, falling upon John. To John, he showed himself under * the aspect of white crystal, for he was a virgin. To James, he appeared under the aspect of a sword, for it was by the sword that he was to accomplish his martyrdom; to Philip, under the aspect of the sea; to Bartholomew, under the appearance of a vine; to Thomas, in the form of an ox, for the faith of Thomas was robust as bronze; to Matthew, under the aspect of a child; to Thaddaeus, in the form of an ear of corn; to Nathanael, under the appearance of a white dove; to James, son of Alphaeus, under the aspect of lightning.
ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ዙሉ ፡ ተናገሮሙ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ኢትፍርሁ ፡ አነ ፡ ውእቱ ። አእመ ርክሙኑ ፡ ፅበሰክሙ ፡ ይእዜ ። ወደንገጹ ፡ ወስእኑ ፡ ተናግሮ ፣ ወክት ፣ ኩሎሙ ፡ ከመ፣ አብድንት ።
Then he began to speak to them and said to them: "Fear nothing, it is I; have you not known your weakness now?" They were terrified, unable to speak; they were all like corpses.

He resumed speaking * and said to them: Is it not Thursday evening (the evening of the fifth day) that I gave you my body and my blood? That time I did not appear to you in this apparition; but consider well and know that you are body and blood (?). And he said to Peter: if my body had not

taken the form of a wheat loaf, no one would have been able to receive it. Having said this, he received the bread of his holy body and the Lord of the Universe ascended to the heavens. The Lord said again to Peter: Of all sins, the greatest is unbelief and perfidy, the counsels of injustice and iniquity. If a man performs the works of justice and worships (God) and leads a completely holy life, if he does not have the sign of charity on his face, the angels who watch (ἐγρήγοροι) will prevent him from entering through * the gate of heaven.

And the souls, when they depart from the body, where do they go to dwell? In the land of their creation. Just as Enoch says: I saw the souls in four places, for there are four gates of heaven, which are: Dabra Syon, Dabra Sebah (of the East) which is Dabra Zayt, Dabra Sina, and Dabra Tabor. The souls remain there for a day, then they present themselves before the creator. According to the hour in which they departed [from the body], the souls of the Christians stand there (for a day). Those who have charity have a city built of charity. I tell you this, said the angel who had been sent to me; but what is greater than everything are the sufferings that Our Lord suffered on the cross: He who drew the world from where it was not [from nothing] was brought before the tribunal * so that he might be crucified. He who could have in an instant submerged those who were crucifying him, suffered the crucifixion; they spat in his face, rushed upon him, slapped him, pierced his right side with the lance, and nailed his hands and feet. When the angels of heaven saw

the nakedness of the Lord, they wept, sobbed, descended after having taken their swords, and wanted to kill everyone. The Most High prevented them, full of patience and long-suffering, saying: "Do not destroy my creature. Is it not for her that I suffer?"

And [the angels], having heard these words, cried out: Praises to you, O merciful and long-suffering one! Then the angels of Mika'el took off their crowns and covered the nails of his * right hand; those of Gabriel covered the nails of his left foot; those of Ura'el covered the wound made by the blow of the lance that he had received in his side; the seraphim (Surafel) shaded his head and the cherubim (Kirubel) covered his face; Ura'el and Rufa'el also covered his face, but no one can entirely hide him whose divinity fills everything; it is he who covers the nakedness of the face of every creature. Praise to him!

On the day of his passion, the angel of death was overthrown, but before that his power was taken from him, on the first day of tegemt, when (Christ) said: "Do not make my father's house a house of trade." It was then that his power was taken from him and, from that day until his crucifixion, 545 days elapsed, not as those that one counts, but double.

broken the virginity of the Virgin. Glory to him! He can do everything and nothing is impossible for him. The saints have seen his vision, in various times, for his vision will not cease until the end of the world. The first and the last of the prophets have seen his vision. Daniel also saw beasts that rose from the sea at the breath of the wind. He said: "The first was like a lioness, and it had wings, and its wings were like those of an eagle. I watched it until the feathers of its wings were torn off; it rose and stood on a man's foot and a man's heart was given to it. And I said to it: Rise and eat much flesh."

Here is the explanation of this passage: This beast signifies Nebuchadnezzar (Näbukadanasor), * as well as the lion of which he speaks, for the kings of the peoples are called lions, says Jeremiah: "The sheep that was lost, the lions have eaten it." The sheep are the house of Israel. - If he says: I watched it until its wings were torn off, it is because his kingdom (of Nebuchadnezzar) was taken from him. - And it stood on a man's foot, he says, this means that his pride made him a beast and that he then became a man again and that a man's heart was given to him. - The meat that he ate is that he governed much.
ወካልእ ፡ አርዌ ፡ከመ ፥ነምር ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ እስክንድር ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ፊልኦስ ፡ ዘእ ምዘርአ ፡ ያፌት ። ወ፪ክንፈ ፡ ዖፍ ፡ዘይቤሂ፣፪ለእስክንድር' ። ወ8አርእስቲሁ ፡ ዘይቤሂ ፡
[Daniel] also spoke of another beast similar to a leopard. It is Alexander, son of Philip, of the race of Japheth. - When he says that it had four
