፪መንግሥት ፡ ዘነግሠ ፡ ዘመደ ፡ እስክንድር ። ወአርዌ ፡ ዘይመስል ፡ ድበ ፡ዘይቤሂ ፡ዳር ዮስ ፡ ውአቱ ፡ ወይመስል ፡ "ድበ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ እስመ ፡ ድብ ፡ ይትወለድ ፡ እም፪አቡሁ ፡ ነ ምር ፡ ወእሙ ፡ ዝእብ ። ወዳርዮስ ፡ ሂ ፡ ምንዴናዌ' ፡ አቡሁ ፡ እምነገሥተ ፡ ያፌት ፡ወ አሙ ፡ እምነገሥተ ፡ ግብጽ ። ወድኅራዊ' ፡ ርኢኩ ፡ አርዌ ፡ ግሩም ፣ ዘይቤሂ ፡ መንግሥተ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ ዔሳው ፡ ውእ ቱ ። እንተ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ለዕዝራ ፡ ራብዕ ፡ መንግሥት ፡ እንተ ፡ አስተርአየቶ ፡ ለዳንኤል ፡ እጉክከ ። ወደቂቀ ፡ ዔሳው ፡ ይነግሠ ፣ በሮሜሂ ፡ ወበዘቢድሂ ፡ በስልድንሂ ፡ ዐበምስርሂ ፡ ወአኮ ፡ ወትረ ፣ ዘይነግሥ ፣ መብዝኅቶሙ- ፣ ዘይነግሥ ፡ በበ ፡ አብሬቶሙ። ወ8እምድኅረ ፡ 8ይነግሥ ፡ ዘመድ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዳንኤል ፡ ቱሱሓን ፡ በዘርአ ፣ እኾለ ፡ እመሕያው ።
wings of a bird, these are the four … of Alexander; its four heads are the four kingdoms that the parents of Alexander governed. - The beast that resembles a bear is Darius, whom he says is similar to the bear, * for the bear is procreated by two (different) animals: its father is the leopard and its mother the hyena, and Darius had for a father a Mede (mendênäwi), descendant of the kings of Japheth, and his mother was of the family of the kings of Egypt. Finally, says Daniel, I saw a terrible beast; it is the kingdom of the children of Esau (Esäw), concerning which the angel said to Ezra: it is the fourth kingdom that appeared to your brother Daniel; and the descendants of Esau will reign in Rome, in Zabid, in Selden, and in Egypt. But they will not reign forever, most will reign in their turn. One family will reign after another. That is why Daniel says: "They will be mixed with the posterity of humans."
ወንስርሂ ፡ ዘርእየ ፡ ፅዝራ ፤ ከመዝ ፣፤ ፍካሬሁ ፡'"ንስርሰ ፡ መንግሥቶሙ ፡ ለደቂቀ ፡ ዔሳው ። ወካዕበ ፡ ይተረጐም ፡ በሐሳቄ ፣ መሲሕ ። እስመ ፡ ለሐሳዊ ፣ መሲሕ ፡እሙ፣ እምቤተ ፡ ዔሳው ፡ ወበእንተዝ ፡ ይትፕለቀ፦ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አሙ ። ወርኢኬ ፡ ባሕርኒ ፡ ዓለ ም ።፡ ወ፻አቅርንቲሁ ፡ የነገሥት ፣ አለ ፡ ነግ ፡ በቅድሜሁ ፡ ወእሉ ፡ ይትፌጸሙ ፡ በዓሥ ር ፡ቀመር ። ወከመዝ ፡ አስማቲሆሙ ፣ ያዲን ፡ ከመዲን ፡ ለፈዲን ። ወዘይቤሂ ፡ ፲ወ፪ክነፊሁ ፡ ፲ወ፪ነገሥት ፣ እሙንቱ ፣ ወአስማቲሆሙኒ ፡ ከመዝ ። ያቢ
The eagle that Ezra saw is explained thus: * The eagle is the kingdom of the descendants of Esau; it is also interpreted by the false messiah, for the mother of the false messiah will be of the house of Esau and that is why he will be imputed to his mother. And see now: The sea is the world; the three horns are the three kings who reigned before him and these things will be accomplished in the tenth cycle. - Here are their names: Yadin, Kamadin, Lafadin. The twelve wings of which he speaks are twelve kings, whose names are: Yabibin,


[p. 84] {#po.1.p84}
ቢን ፡ ከቲ፣ሰዓዲ ፡ ሆዲ ፡ማዲ ፣ሐዲ ፡ዳዲ ። ጋዲ ፡ ሳዲ ኒዲ ፡ አዲ ፡ ለውዲ።ወ እሉሂ ፣ ይትፌጸሙ ፡፣ በ፲ወ፪አቅማር ። ወይሰርር ፡ በክነፊሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ምድር ፣ ዘይቤሂ ፡ ዘይብል ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ስግዱ ፣ ለ"አምሳልየ ። ወደመናት ፡ ይትጋባእ ፡ ላዕ ሴሁ ፡ ዘይቤሂ ፡ ሄኖክ ፣ ወኤልያስ ፣ እሙንቱ ፡ ወነፋሳት ፣ ዘይቤ ፣ ጻድቃን ፡ ወአርእስ ትሂ ፡ ወክንፍ ፡ ደቃቃት ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ነገሥት ፡ እሉኒ ፡ አለ ፡ ይትነሥኡ ፡ በበ፣ዘመኖሙ፣ ወአግረረ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ዘታሕተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ዘይቤሂ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ይሁቦ ፡ ለስልጣኑ ፡ በከመ ፡ ነገ ሩ ፡ መጻሕፍት ፡ ኩልክሙ ፡ ኢትትግሁ ፡ ዘይቤሂ ፡ ዮልክሙ ፡ ኢትግበሩ ፡ ጽድቀ ፡ ብ ሂል-። ወከመዝ ፡ ይብሎሙ ፡ ለመኳንንቲሁ ፡ ኩልክሙ ፡ ትነግሠ ፡ ወትመኩነኑ ፡ በድ ኅሬየ ፡ ጳደጵብእሲ ፡ በበ፲፻ዓመት ።
KAI, Sa'adi, Hodi, Madi, Hadi, Dadi, Gadi, Sadi, Nidi, Adi, Lawdi; these will be accomplished in twelve cycles. - And when he says that he will fly with his wings over the whole earth, that indicates that he says: "Prostrate yourselves before my * image." - When he says: that the clouds accumulate upon him, these are Enoch and Elijah ('Elyäs); the winds of which he speaks are the righteous; the heads and the small wings are the kings who will rise each in his time. - And he has subjected everything that is under the heaven, he says, this means that he will deliver everything to his power, as the Scriptures recount. - When he says: Do not all watch, he means: "Do not all do justice." It is thus that he spoke to his governors: "You all, you will reign and you will govern after me, each for a thousand years."
ኦሐሳዌ ፡ ስም ፡ መኑ ፡ መሀረከ ፡ ዘከመዝ ፡ ሐሰተ ፡ ቀዲሙኒ ፡ ዘከማከ ፡ መሐመድ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ትንሣኤ ፡ ሙታን ፡ ያወስብ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ በበ፲፻አንስት ፡ ወዘይቤሂ ፡ት ነግሠ ፡ ፳፳ብእ”ሲ ፡ በበ፲፻ዓመት ፡ ከንቶ ፣ ሐሰወ ። ወዘይቤሂ ፡ ያወስብ ፡ ፳ብእሷ ፡ ፲፻ አንስት ፡ ከንቶ ፡ ሐሰወ ፡ ውእቱኒ ።
O impostor! who taught you such lies? Formerly, like you, Mahamad (Muhammad) said: "After the resurrection of the dead, a man will marry a thousand women." He who said: "You will each reign * a thousand years", lied; and he who said: "A man will marry a thousand women", lied likewise.
አኮ ፡ እምርአሱ ፣ ዘይወፅእ ፡ አላ ፡ እማእከለ ፡ ሥጋሁ ። ዘይቤሂ ፡ እስመ ፡ እምዘ መደ ፡ ብአሲት ፡ ይትወለድ ፡ በቤተ ፡ ዔሳው ። ወዘይቤሄ ፡ ፳ነገሥት ፡ እለ ፡ እኩይ ፡ ክ ራማቲሆሙ ። ወዝውእቱ አስማቲሆሙ ። ናዲ ፡ ጸዲ ። ሐልቃዲ ፡ ሳሕወዲ ። አባዲ፣ አስራዲ ፡ ሐመዲ ፡ አሮዲ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ዘይመውት ፡ ብእደ' ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ርቱዓ ፡ ሃይማኖ ት ። ወአመ ፡ ሞተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይተርፍ ፡ እምነ ፡ ፲ወይቀመር ፡ ፻ዓመት ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሾወ
When he says: It is not from his head, but from the middle of his body that he will come out, this means that he will be born from the lineage of a woman of the house of Esau.


[p. 85] {#po.1.p85}
ይሱባዔ ፡ እለ ፣ ይትወሰክ' ፣ዲቤሆን ። ወእሉሰ ፡ ነግሥ ፡ በበዕድሜሆሙ ። ወእምድኅሬ ሆሙኒ ፡ ነገሥት ፡ ይትነሥኡ ፡ በበዘመኖሙ ፡፣ በሮሜሂ ፡ወበ”ብሔረ ፡ ሰዓለዲንሂ ፡ዘይ ብልዎ ፡ መምዕላይ ። ወባሕቱ ፡ ይመውአዖ ፡ ርቱዓነ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ እለ ፡ ይትፈጠሩ ፡ ነገ ሥተ ፡ በኢትዮጵያ ፡
- When he says again: eight kings whose years will be bad and here are their names: Nadi, Sadi, Halgadi, Sahwadi, 'Abadi, Asradi, Hamadi, 'Arodi - and this one will die by the hand of an orthodox king, and when he dies, there will remain of thirteen cycles one hundred years less three and three weeks that will be added to it. - And these will reign in the epochs that have been fixed for them. After them, kings will rise, in their time, in Rome * and in the land of Sa'aladin, whom one will call a tyrant. But he will be defeated by the orthodox
ወዘንተ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ነገሮ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ለብጹዕ ፡ በጸሎተ ፡ ሚከኤል ። ወበእንተሂ ፡ ይነገሥት ፡ እለ ፡ ይቤ ፣ ቦሙ, ፡ ፻ክረማተ ፣ ቀዳሜ ፣ ማኅለቅተ ፡ነገሥት ፡እ ለ ፡ ይትነሥኡ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ለሐሳዊ ፡ መሲሕ ፡ ወሣልሶሙ ፡ ውእቱ «፡ ዘገሃደመ ፡ ይብለከ ፡ ይመውቱ ፡ በከክ፡ናት ፡ እምኔክ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ጸብአ ። ወጳይመውት ፡ በውስተ ፡ ምስክቡ። ተጽዒሮ ፡ዝይቤ ፡ ሂ ፡ ሐሳዊ ፡ መሲሕ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘይጹዐር ፡ በመጥባሕተ ፡ እግዚአብሔ ር። ወዐንበሳሂ ፡ ዘተንሥአ ፡-እምገዳም ፣ ወልድ ፣ ውእቱ ። እምአሕዛብ ፡ ብሂል ። በከ "መ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ኢሳይያስ ፡ ወይዌውዑ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ ዕፀወ ፡ ገዳም ። ወሰሎሞንኒ ፡ ይቤላ ፡ ለቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ተንሥኢ ፡ ወፅርጊ ፡ እምገዳም ፡ እምአሕዛብ ፡ ብሂል ። ወዳዊትኒ ይቤ ፡ ትትሐሠይ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ዘውስቴታ ፡ ወዝኒ ፡ ብሂል ፡ ይትሐሠዩ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ ብሂል ። ወለእለ ፡ ተርፉ-፣፡ ሕዝብየ ፡ አስተፌሥሖሙ ፡ ዝኒ ፡ እስከ ፡ ትፌጽም ፡ ፅለተ ፡ ደይን።
who will have been created kings in Ethiopia. That is all that the angel recounted, before its realization, to the blessed Basalota-Mika'el. - As for the three kings of whom it is spoken, they will reign one hundred years, before the disappearance of the kings who will rise with the false messiah, who will be the third. And he tells you clearly: "They will die by a javelin blow from your hand in a war and one will die in his bed." - When he says: "After he has suffered", it is the false messiah who will suffer by the sword of God; and the lion that surges from the desert is the Son, [who will rise] from the midst of the peoples. Just as * Isaiah says: And the peoples will cry out, (the peoples who are) the trees of the plain. And Solomon says to the Christian Church: "Rise and come up from the plain", that is to say, from the peoples. David also says: "The plain will rejoice, with all that it contains", which means: the peoples will rejoice. - And I will gladden those who remain of my people until the day of judgment is finished.


[p. 86] {#po.1.p86}
ዘይቤሂ ፡ እስመ ፡ ለዕለተ ፡ ደይን ፡ ኑኀ ፣ ፲፻ዓመት ፡፣ ወለኅሩያን ፡ አሜሃ ፡ ፍሥሓሆ ሙ ፡ ወእንዘ ፡ እሙንቱ ፣ ይትፌሥሑ ። ኃጥኣን ፡ አሜሃ ፡ ይትወቀሱ ፡ ብፁዕኬ ፡ አማ ኒ ፡ በክርስቶስ ። ኅጢአተ ፡ አማኒ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ደብር ፡ ወእመ ፡ በዝኅ ፣፡ ወ ለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፣ ጽድቁ ፡ መጣነ ፡ ቀለምጽጽ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ን"ስቲት ፡ ትመውኦ ፡ ወት ስዕሮ ፣ ለኩሉ ፡ ኅጢአት ፡ ወስብሐት ፡ ለዘይሁቦ ፡ አሚነ ፡ ለዘ ፡ አፍቀረ ፡ ወኢአሚነ ፡ ለዘ ፡ ጸልአ ። ዙሉ ፡ ዘይብሉ ፡ ይትሀጐሉ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ርቱዓነ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ውእቱኬ ፣፡ ውፁእ ፡ እምክርስቶስ ። ወእመሰ ፡ ይሰመዩ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወዐቀቡ ፡ ሥርዐተ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ሎሙሰ ፡ ኢይክል ፡ደይን ፡ ርእዮቶሙ ።
If he speaks thus, it is because the day of judgment will have a length of a thousand years. It will then be the joy of the elect and while these will rejoice, the sinners will contest. Blessed therefore is he who has believed in Christ. Even if the sins of the believer were as big as mountains and numerous, if his justice is like a spark, this little justice * will prevail and erase all his sins. Glory to him who gave faith to him whom he loves and unbelief to him whom he hates. All those who say: "The orthodox Christians will be damned", those are outside of Christ, for if they are called Christians and if they observe the precepts of justice, the judgment (damnation) cannot reach them.
ወሐዋርያትኒ ፡ ዕበ ፡ ተካፈሉ ፡ አህጉረ ፡ ዓለም ። ይቤ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ዘኢኮነ ፡ ዙሉ፣ሰ ብእ ፡ ምእመነ ፡ ኢይጸድቅ ፡ ብሂልየ ፡ እሜህር ። ወእንድርያስኒ ፡ ይቤ ፣ ዘኢኮነ ፡ መሃ ሬ፡ ኢይጸድቅ ፡ ብሂልየ ፣ እሜሀር ። ወያዕቆብኒ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዘኢተመትረ ፡ በእንተ ፡ እግዚ አብሔር ፡ ኢይጸድቅ ፡ ብሂልየ ፡ እ”ሜህር ። ወዮሐንስኒ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዘኢኮነ ፡ ንጹሐ ፡ በድ ንግልና ፡ ኢይጸድቅ ፡ ብሂልየ ፡ እሜሀር ፡ ወፊልኦስኒ ፣ ይቤ ፡ ዘመሐለ ፡ በሐሰት ፣ ኢ ይጸድቅ ፡ ብሂልየ ፡ እሜህሀር ። ወበርተሎሜዎስኒ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዘኢያክበረ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወእሙ ፡ ኢይጸድቅ ፡ ብሂልየ ፡ እሜህር ። ወቶማስኒ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዘኢተጠብሐ ፡ ከመ ፡ ላህም ፡ ኢይ ጸድቅ ፡ ብሂልየ ፡ እሜህር ። ወማቴዎስኗኒ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዘኢኮነ ፡ የውሀ ፡ ከመ ፡ ርግብ ፡ ኢ
When the apostles were about to divide the countries of the world, Peter said: Every man who is not a believer will not be justified (will not save his soul), that is my doctrine and what I will teach. - Andrew said: He who is not compassionate will not be justified, that is what I say and what I will teach. - James said: He who is not beheaded (?) for God will not be justified, that is the word that I will teach. - * John said: He who is not chaste in virginity will not be justified, that is the doctrine that I will teach. - Philip said: He who has sworn in vain will not be justified, that is what I will teach. - Bartholomew said: He who does not honor his father and mother will not be justified, that is what I will teach. - Thomas said: He who has not been slaughtered like a calf will not be justified, that is my teaching.


[p. 87] {#po.1.p87}

- Matthew said: He who is not gentle as the dove will not be justified, that is what I will teach. - Thaddaeus said: He who is not zealous for the law will not be justified, that is what I will teach. - Nathanael said: He who does not make his body suffer on the cross will not be justified, that is what I will teach. - James, son of Alphaeus, said: He who has not suffered stoning * will not be justified, that is what I will preach. - Matthias said: Whoever has not become poor will not be justified, that is what I will teach. When all the apostles had spoken thus, Paul took the floor and said to them: Listen to me, my brothers, here is the doctrine that I will teach: He who believes and has been baptized will be saved, but he who does not believe will be condemned. He who does not love his neighbor will not be justified; he who hates his neighbor will be damned. Thus, he said, I will preach. When the apostles had discoursed thus, Our Lord Jesus Christ arrived and said to them: Peace be upon you, my disciples. And they rose and worshipped him and knew that it was Our Lord. * He said to them: What were you recounting then yesterday? Certainly I was pleased with the word of Paul, my elect, his word was more agreeable to me than all of yours. Did I not tell you in the Gospel: He who believes and has been baptized will be saved and he who does not believe will be condemned. And by what work will man be saved, if he does not have faith and love [of neighbor] (?) and faith? That is worth more than being a martyr. There is no man who is absolutely without stain;


[p. 88] {#po.1.p88}
ቱ ፡ ነውር ፡ ፍጹም ፡ ወፍጹምሰ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ባሕቲቱ ። ወአንትሙኒ ፡ አኮኑ ፡ ሰብእ ፡አ ንትሙ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ ሰማየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ ንጹሐ ፡ በቅድሜየ ።
God alone is perfect. And you, are you not men? See the heaven; it was not even pure before me.

You, Peter, you denied me three times during the night and I forgave you your sin. However, there is none greater * than to deny the creator. - You, John, while I was attached to the cross, you rejoiced with the princes of the priests. - You, Andrew, you went out to Hëguâ (?). - You, James, after having left your clothes, you took flight. - You, Philip, you went out into the middle of the city. - You, Bartholomew, you fled with Râkub, the son of your sister. - You, Thomas, you disappeared, mounted in a chariot. - You, Matthew, you hid in the city and you went out, at night, in secret. - You, Thaddaeus, you went out wrapped in a veil. - You, Nathanael, you hid in a wood. - You, James, son of Alphaeus, you hid in a field. - That is what you did, yet * I did not leave you, but I chose you to make you my heirs.
ወእምድኅረ ፡ ዕርገቱ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ይቤሉ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ ይደልወነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንሙት ፡ በእንተ ፡ስሙ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፣ አምላክነ ።
After the ascension of Our Lord, the apostles said: It is fitting that we die for the name of Christ, our God.

And the martyrs came successively, saying: let us deliver our souls for the name of Christ, our God. After them all came the monks, for it is among them that the perfect reward was placed. Here are the works and the holiness of Anthony, the first of the monks, for no one in the world has struggled (has

ሳት ፡ አልቦ ፡ እምሰብእ ፡ ዘተጋደለ ፡ ከመ ፡ እንጦንስ ። ወአቡሁ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ዓለም ፡ባ ዕል ፡ ጥቀ ፡ብእሲሁ ፡ወፅምደ ፡ አባዕሪሁ ፡ ፳፻ወእንዘ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ይነብር ፡ አቡሁ ፣፡ ሞተ፣ ከመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ሰብ”እ ፡ ወእንጦንስኒ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ኮንከ ፡ አባ ፡ ወአይቴ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ቃ ልከ ፡ ዘትኤዝዞሙ ፡ ለአግብርቲከ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘነከየከ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ንስቲት ፡ እስትንፋስ ፡ ዘአልቦ ፡ ወኢሀሎ ፡ ኀቤከ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂሎ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ገዳም ። ወአሜሃ ፡ ኮኖ ፡ ፲፳ዓመተ ፣ ወተመከረ ፡ እንጦኒ ፣ ፲ወ፪መከራ ። ወባዕዳንሰ ፣፡ መከራ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ፕልቄ ። ወበርሀ ፡ እንጦኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ ፲ወ፪መጠና ፡ ለፀሐይ ። ወጻድቃንሰ ፣ይበርሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ፀሐ ይ ። ወቦከመ ፡ ወርዓኅ ፤ ወቦከመ ፡ ከዋክብት ።
led an ascetic life) like Anthony. His father was, in the world, very rich, possessing 500 pairs of oxen. And being in this situation, he died like any man. * And Anthony cried out: What have you become? O my father, where is your voice that commanded your servants? And this accident was caused to you only by a slight breath which is no more and which you no longer have. After having pronounced these words, Anthony went out into the desert; he was then eighteen years old. Anthony had twelve principal temptations, but he had many others; he shone like twelve times the measure (of the light) of the sun, while the saints shine only like seven: there are some who shine like the moon, there are some who shine like the stars.
ወዝውእቱ ፡ ትዕግሥታቲሁ ፡ ለእንጦንስ ። ቀዳሚ ፡ በብእሲት ፡ ወዳግም ፡ በዐረብ ። ወሣልስ ፡ በአጋንንት ፡ አመ ፡ አውረድዎ ፡ እምደብር ፡ ጌዜ ። ወራብዕ ፡ በራኅብ'። ወ ኃምስ ፡ በጽምአ ፡ ማይ ፡ ወ”ሣድስ ፡ በአርዌ ፡ ወሳብዕ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ ወሳምን ፡ ርግዘተ ፡ በኩናት ፡ ወዝኒ ፡ በርግዘት ፡ በኀበ ፡ ዐላዊያን ፡ወታስዕ ፡ በአንቃል ፡ዘውእቱ ፡ ቀማል ፥ ብሂል ፡ ወዓሥር ፡ በብሂል ፡ ግባእ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወ፲ወ8በጽፍዐተ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወ፲ወ ፪በሐለስትዮታት ። ወእሉ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፣ ሞእዎ ፣ ለሰይጣን ፣ ተዘኪሮሙ ፡ ዓለመ ፡-ኀላፌ ።
And here is what the constancy of Anthony was. [He was tested] firstly by a woman, secondly by the Arabs, thirdly by the demons who made him descend thirty times from the mountain, fourthly by hunger, fifthly by thirst, sixthly * by animals, seventhly by the sword, eighthly by a spear blow, and this spear blow [he received it] among the infidels (7); ninthly by the "angâl" that is to say by lice, tenthly by the voice which said to him: return to the world; eleventhly by the slaps that men gave him; twelfthly by the onagers. - And these saints (monks) overcame the demon, by remembering
ወተስአለ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለመልአክ ፡ ዘተፈነወ ፣ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ምን ተ ፡እንከ ፡ ይከውን ፡በኅልቀተ ፡ ዓለም ። ወይቤሎ ፡ መልአክ ፣ ይነግሥ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ዐመ
that this world is fleeting. And the man of God questioned the angel who had been sent to him and said to him: What will there be then at the end of the world? - The angel answered him: There will reign


[p. 90] {#po.1.p90}
ፃ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ መጸሕፍት ። ወዮሐንስኒ ፡ አቡቀለምሲስ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ እግዚ እ ፡ ተጽዒኖ ፡ በፈረስ ፡ ጸዐዳ ።
an iniquitous king, as the Scriptures say. And John of the Apocalypse says: The Lord will come, mounted on a white horse
ወበእንተ ፡ ሐሳዊኒ ፣ ከመዝ ፣ ግብሩ ፡ ይወፅእ ፣ "ሠጢጦ ፡ ከርሠ ፡ እሙ ፣ ወይቀት ል ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወይነብር ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበር ፡ ወይነግሥ ። ወዕሶበ ፣ ተፈጸመ ፣፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ንግ ው ይፀብኦ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ምስለ ፡ ሐራሁ። ወቅድመ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ይወግኦ ፡ ኤልያስ ፡ ትስብያ ዊ፣0፣ውስተ፣እንግድዓሁ። ወኢያሱ ፡ወልደ ፡ነዌ ፡ ውስተ ፡ፍጽሙ ። ወዳዊት ፡ ሰንቃዊ ፡ ይረግዞ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩልያቱ ። ወጴጥሮስኒ ፡ ይረግዝ ፣ ፈረሶ ። ወቴዎድሮስ ፡ ወገላውድዮ ስ፣ይቀነጥቡ፣ድርዖ ፡መቲሮሙ ፡ርእሶ። ወዮሐንስኒ ፡ ይደግም ፡ወጊአ ፡ ፈረሱ። ወዐቢየ ፡ ነገረ ፣ ይከውን ፣ በብሂል ፡ አነ ፡ ቀተልኩ ፡ ወአነ ፣ በደርኩ ። ወአሳተ ፡ መ ዓት ፡ ትቀትሎ ፡ ወፃአ'፣ እምአፈ፣እግዚእ ። ወሰራዊቱኒ ፡ ይመውቱ ፡ ምስሌሆ ። ወቀጸ ላ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ይትዐነቅ ፡ "ኤልያስ ። ወኢያሱ ፡ በሐመልሚል ፡ ልብስ ፡ ዘወርቅ ፡ ይትረሰ ይ ። ወጴጥሮስኒ ፡ ሽፍሽፍት ፡ ዘወርቅ ። ወለዮሐንስ ፡ ገማ ፡ ጸዐዳ ። ወለቴዎድሮስ ፡ ወ ገላውዴዎስ ፡ አክሊል?" ፡ ቀያሓን ። ለዳዊት ፡ ወለባዕዳን ፡ ጻሯቃን ፡ ወገረት ፡ ዘወርቅ ። ወአምድኅረዝ ፡ ይከውን ፣ ትንሣኤ ፡ ወቅሥት ። ትሰፋሕ ፡ ለኅሩያን ፡ ሰማይ። እንተ፣ሀ ለወት ፡ ጥብልልተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ፊደል ፡ እንተ ፡ኀብአ ፡ አምላክ ፡ዐውደ፣መንበሩ ። ወዝ፣
As for the impostor, such will be his work: || will be born * after having torn his mother's womb and will kill his father; he will sit on a throne and will reign. And when the days of his reign are finished, our Lord will fight him with his army. - But Elijah, of Tisbe (Tesbeyäwi), will strike him first in the chest; Joshua, son of Nun, in the forehead; David, the musician, will pierce him with the spear in the loins; Peter will strike his horse with the spear, and Theodore and Claudius, after having cut off his head, will pierce his breastplate; John will deal a new blow to his horse. Then a loud voice will be heard crying: It is I who killed him, it is I who arrived first (to kill him). - And the fire of wrath, coming out of the mouth of the Lord, will kill him and his army will perish with him. Elijah will put a gold crown around his neck, * Joshua will take a green gold garment; Peter a gold headband(?); John his white necklace(?), Theodore and Claudius the red crowns; David and other righteous ones his gold bands(?). Then will take place the resurrection, as well as the judgment: the heaven, which is rolled around his throne, like a letter that God has hidden, will unfold to the elect. This earth will be like a land trodden by the foot of the righteous.
ምድር ፡ ይከውን ፡ መጠነ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ኪደት" ፡ እግሩ ፡ ለጻድቅ። ወይከውን ፡ አሜሃ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ሐዲስ ፡ ወምድርኒ ፡ ሐዲስ ፡ ወገነትኒ ፣ ይትረአይ ። ወ
There will then be a new heaven and a new earth and paradise will

ፅፀዊሃኒ ፡ ለገነት ፣ ሶበ ፡ ይፈርዩ ፣ ሰብእ' ፣ይፈርያ'። ወዕበ ፡ ይፈርዩ ፡ዘይቤ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይገ ብሩ ፡ ም”ግባረ ፡ ሠናይ ። ለዘ ፡ ይሜህር ፡ በመዝሙር ፡ ዘዳዊት ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ለለዕለ ቱ ፡ ትፈሪ ፡ ፳ወዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፣ ምስጢር ፡ ኢትክሥት ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እንበለ ፣፡ ለማእ ምራን ። ወኅባእ ፡ ወኢትክሥት ፡ ዘእምኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወባሕቱ ፡ እምወርቅ ፡ ወ እምብሩር ፡ ይጌይስ ፡ ዘዘገበ ፡ ቃለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። እስመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘሥጋ ፡ ከመ፣፡ሣ ፅር ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ክብሩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ፍሬ ፡ ሣዕር ፡ ወቃለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይነብር ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
show itself. - When the trees of paradise produce fruit, these fruits will be men. And when he says when they produce fruit, that means when they do good works. * For he who teaches a word in the psalms of David, it produces 10,000 fruits each day. Do not reveal this mystery except to the doctors, hide and do not reveal a secret that comes from God. He who keeps the word of God is worth more than gold and silver, for everything that touches the flesh (the body) is like grass and all honor like the fruit of the grass, but the word of God remains eternally.
ተፈጸመ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ምስጢር ፡ ወይትነበብ ፡ ለአንክሮ ፣ ሰሚዖቱ ፣ መድምም ፡ ለዝን ቱ ፡ መጽሐፍ ። ወያበርህ ፡ ልበ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ከመ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ ብርሀት ።
The book of the mystery is finished; it is read to amaze the one who will hear the admirable reading of this book and it illuminates, like the resplendent sun, the heart of man.
